# Ethiopian Synaxarium: Sane (Les mois de Sanê, Hamlê et Nahasê : I. Mois de Sanê)

**Author**: Anonymous (Ethiopian Synaxarion)  
**Series**: PO Tome 1  
**Columns**: pp. 526–703  

[p. 526] {#po.1.p526}

### THE BOOK OF THE SYNAXARY

In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. Month of Sane, blessed; the first day has 15 hours, then goes on decreasing. FIRST DAY OF SANE (May 26) LESSON. On this day is the dedication of the church of Saint Lawentewos (Leontius) originally from Syria and one recalls the miracle of which he was the author. Here is the fact: when this saint had been martyred in the city of Tarabelos (Tripolis), the 22nd of the month of Hamle, a Christian woman came, wife of one of the high magistrates; she took the body of the saint after having given much money to the soldiers and wrapped it in a gold-woven garment that belonged to her husband. Then she placed it in a shrine, at her home, and hung before it a lamp that gave light; she also had his image painted.

Now her husband was a governor; Diyogeltyanos (Diocletian) became angry with him and had him put in prison in the city of Antioch. As his wife had wrapped the body of Saint Lawentewos, had treated it as was proper and clothed it in a gold-woven garment, the martyr of Christ, Lawentewos, did not want a woman to do him favors without reward but he gratified her with a beautiful reward in this world, since he delivered her husband from prison, and he was still to lavish his favors upon her, in the world to come, and intercede for her before Our Lord Jesus Christ. That very night, the saint appeared to the magistrate, her husband, in the prison; and a great light rose over him, to the point that it illuminated the entire prison. Then the prisoner saw the saint in the likeness of a horseman resplendent with light; he was standing in the prison near him, clothed in that gold-woven garment that the governor recognized as his own clothing. Then the saint, namely the horseman, said to this governor: "Do not be sad, nor be afflicted, for you will be free tomorrow; you will eat in the company of the emperor, at his table, and you will go to your home." Then this magistrate was stunned by the multiplicity of events: because he had seen this horseman; then for this light that had illuminated him; for the fact that the horseman had penetrated to him in a prison that was nevertheless sealed; and finally for this garment

woven of gold that was his and that he had left at his house. Then Saint Lawentewos disappeared from beside him, and went to the emperor, and, that very night, he struck the emperor's foot and the emperor Diyogeltyanos woke up. At the sight of Saint Lawentewos, he was afraid and was seized with extreme terror. And the saint said to the emperor: "When the morning has come, have the magistrate such-and-such brought out of prison; honor him and let him go to his home, so that you do not perish." The emperor answered him, all trembling with fear of him, and said to him: "Yes, my Lord; everything that you have ordered me, I will accomplish." The morning having come, the emperor sent to have the governor brought out of prison, rendered him great honors, clothed him in magnificent clothes, had him sit in his company at his table and told him how this horseman had appeared to him. This story filled the governor with stupefaction and he was seized with great astonishment. The emperor thought that it was he, the governor, who by charms had done this, but the latter answered him saying: "As for me, I do not understand anything at all about charms, and I do not know who is the one who appeared to you." The emperor did not dare to say the slightest bad word to him, but sent him to his city with great honors. While the governor was on the road, there appeared to him [O, P: the angel of God in the guise of]


[p. 530] {#po.1.p530}
ላውንቴዎስ ፣ ወኮነ ፡ ይትናገር ፣ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወይናዝዞ ፡ እስከ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገሩ ፡ ጠራብሎስ ፡ ወተኃብአ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ሶቤሃ ።
Saint Lawentewos, who spoke to him and comforted him until he arrived at his city, Tarabelos, and at that instant he disappeared from beside him.
ወፀአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መስፍን ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ወተአምጥፕሙ ፡ለሰብአ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ወነገሮ ሙ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፈረሳዊ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይለብስ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ልብሰ ፣፡ ዘወርቅ ፣ ወዘከመ ፣ ተካየዶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ያድኅኖ ፣ እምቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ አውፅኦ ፡ "እምቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ፤' ፡ በጽባሕ ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ አ ስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይለብስ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ልብሰ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወአ እመረት ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡፣ ላውንቴዎስ ፡ ወአውሥአቶ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ትሬአእዮ ፡ ታአምሮኑ ፣ ወአውሥአ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ እወ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አብአቶ ፡ ውስ ተ ፣ መካን ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀሎ ፣፡ ሥዕሉ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ ላውንቴዎስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዝ ንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በአማን ፣ ዘአስተርአየ ፡ ሊተ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አርኃወት ፡ ሣጹነ ፡ ዘው”ስ ቴቱ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ላውንቴዎስ ፡ ወርአዮ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይለብስ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ልብሰ ፣ ወርቅ ፡ ዘዚአሁ ፣ ወከሠተ ፡ ግልባበ ፡ ገጹ ፡ ወጠየቀ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘአስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ ወአድኃኖ ። ወተሰአለ ፡ መስፍን ፡ ለብእሲቱ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ "ወይቤላ ፡ መኑ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወነገረቶ ፡ ገድሎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ። ወወደሳ ፡ ለብእሲቱ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘገብረት ፡ ወሰ ብሖ ፡ ለአግዚአብሔር ፡ ልሁል ፡ ገባሬ ፡ ተአምራት ፡ በላዕለ ፡ ቅዱሳኒሁ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፣ ለብእሲቱ ፡ ከመ ፣ ኢታጽርዕ ፡፣ ማኅቶተ ፡ ወዕጣነ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ። ወኮነት ፡ ትገ
The governor came to his dwelling and greeted the people of his house. He told them how this horseman had appeared to him in the prison, clothed in the gold-woven garment, how he had made him the promise to deliver him from prison, how he had made him leave the prison, in the morning, and how he had appeared to him on the way, clothed in the gold garment. Then his wife understood that it was S. Lawentewos. She therefore answered her husband and said to him: "If you saw him, would you recognize him?" He answered her and said to him: "Yes." Then she had him enter the place where the image of Saint Lawentewos was, and when he saw it he said "It is he, truly, who appeared to me." Then she opened the shrine in which the body of Saint Lawentewos was, and he saw it clothed in his own gold garment, and he moved aside the veil from the face and made sure that it was the personage who had appeared to him and delivered him. And the governor questioned his wife about him and said to her: "Who is he?" And she told him the struggle, the martyrdom of the saint; he praised his wife for having acted thus, and glorified God most high, author of miracles, for his saints. Then he commanded his wife not to let incense and the lamp be lacking before him, before his body. She acted thus until God had caused Diyogeltyanos to perish;
ብር ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፣ አጥፍአ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ወሐነጹ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣፡ ወአፍለሱ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፣፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወቀ ደስዋ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወኮነ ፡ እምሥጋሁ ፣ ተአምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ፡ ዓበይ ት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ።
then they built a church for the saint, they transferred his body there which they deposited there, and they consecrated it just on this day; there were great miracles and wonders through his body. May God have mercy on us, through his prayer! Amen.


[p. 531] {#po.1.p531}
ሰላም ፡ ለለውንትዮስ ፡ ጸዋሬ ፡ መቅሠፍት ፡ በነፍሱ ፡፡ እስከ ፡ ተበልዓ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወእስከ ፡ ተበጸለ ፣ ማዕሱ ፡=፡ ድኅረ ፡ ተአተተ ፡ እምድር ፡ ለዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ንግሠ ፡=፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያኑ ፡ ቀደሱ
[Salutation to Lawentyos who endured torments in his soul until his flesh was devoured and his skin torn. After the dominion of Diyogeltyanos had disappeared from the earth, on this day his church was consecrated, and his bones, which were a precious pearl, were transferred there.]
ወአዕዕምቲሁ ፡ ባሕርየ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ አፍለሱ ፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ዕረፍቱ' ፡ ለዮሴፍ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፣ እስራኤ ል ፡ ወአስኔት ፡ ብእሷቱ ።=፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also, death (O: memory) of Joseph, the Just, son of Jacob-Israel, and memory of Asnet, his wife. May God have mercy on us, through their prayers! Amen.]
ሰላም ፡ ለዮሴፍ ፡ ዘተብሀለ ፣ አምሳሉ ፡፡፡ ለአግዚአብሔር ፡ መልአከ ፡ ኃይሉ ፡፡ ለዝ ፡ ጠቢብ ፡ ጸዋሬ ፡ ዕንቀ ፡ መዝገበ ፡ ብዕሉ ፡=፡ እንዘ ፡ የኃልዩ ፡ አዕፅምትየ ፡ ዙሉ ፡፡
[Salutation to Joseph, who was called the image of the leader of the hosts of God. While all my bones sing of this wise man who carried a precious pearl, the treasure of his wealth, they cry out: he is indeed your figure, to Mary!
አንቲ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለአስኔት ፡ ከመ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ፀዳላ =፡ ወከመ ፡ ጽጌ ፡ ረዳ ፡ ቀይሕት ፡ እንተ ፡ ወፅአት ፡ እምነ ፡ ዩጽላ ፡፡ ፡ እለ ፡ ሴሰያሃ ፡ አንሀብት ፡ በአክናፈ ፡ መንፈስ ፣ ተልዕላ ፡፡
Salutation to Asnet, her radiance is like the sun, and like the flower of the red rose, which has come out of its foliage. The bees that nourished her


[p. 532] {#po.1.p532}
ወአለ ፡ ፈቀዳ ፡ ኪያሃ ፡ ያፉስላ ፡፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወድቃ ፡ ወግብተ ፡ ተኃጉላ ፡ገ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝክሮሙ ፣ ለሰማዕታት ፡ ወቶማስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ ዘሀገረ ፣ እስክንድርያ ፍ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፣ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ ክፅበ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፣ ለቅዱስ ፣ ቆዝሞስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ዘእምሀገረ ፣ ጠ ሐ! ፡ ዘእምላዕላይ ፣ ግብጽ ፡ ወአብያጺሁ ። እግዚአብሔር ፣ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶመ-፣ አሜን።
rose on the wings of the wind, but those that wanted to wound her, fell to the ground and instantly perished.] [On this day also falls the commemoration of the martyrs and of Tomas, patriarch of Alexandria. May God have mercy on us, through their prayer! Amen.] On this day also, falls the commemoration of Saint Qozmos, martyr, from the city of Taha in upper Egypt, and of his companions. May God have mercy on us, through their prayer! Amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ክዕበ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ኮነ" ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ ቢፋሞን ፡ ሰማዕት ፣ መስተጋ ድል ፡ ወቅዳሴ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያኑ ፡ ቀዳሚት ፡ እምአብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ አለ ፣ ተ ሐንፃ ፡ በስሙ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፣ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። አሜን ። ሰላም ፣ ለቀዝማ ፣ ወለአብያጺሁ ፡ አምኃ ዘሀገረ ፡ ጣሃ ፡=፡ ፡ ለቢፋሞን ፡ ሰላም ፡ ዘተጋደለ ፣ ብዙኃ ፡=፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይፍረይ ፡ አሜከላ ፡ ወሦከ ፡ በሊሐ ።፡ ገራህተ ፡ ልብየ ፡ ያንጽሑ ፡ ርሱሐ።
On this day also, it is the commemoration of the holy martyr Bifamon [ascetic, and the consecration of his church, the first of the churches that were raised under his name]. May God have mercy on us, through his prayer, in the centuries of centuries! Amen. [Salutation to Qazma, and to his companions originally from the city of Taha; salutation to Bifamon, who struggled much. May they purify the uncultivated, poorly maintained field of my heart, so that it produces neither thistles nor sharp thorns.]


[p. 533] {#po.1.p533}
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። አመ ፡ ፪ለሰኔ ። በዛቲ ፣ ዕለት ፣ አስተርአየ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፤ ዮሐንስ ፣ መጥምቅ ፡ ወሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ ኤልሳዕ ፡ ነቢይ ፤ ረድኡ ፡ ለኤልያስ ፡ ቀናኢ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ እስክን ድርያ ። እስመ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለሉልያኖስ ፡ ከሐዲ ፡ ዕበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፣ ይሕንጽ ፡ ምኩራቦሙ ፡ ለአይሁድ ፡፤ በኢየሩሳሌም ፣ እምድኅረ ፡ አንሐሎ ፡ አስባስያኖስ ፡ ንጉ ሠ ሮም ፡ ወወልዱ ፡ ቲቶ ። እስመ ፡ ሉልያኖስ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ በእከየ ፣ ምክሩ ። ከመ ፡ ይሥዓር ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ዘይቤ ፣፡ አስመ ፣ ኢይትኃደግ ፡ ዝየ ፡ እብን ፡ ዲበ ፡ እብን ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይትነሰት ። ወሶበ ፡ " ሐነጸ ፣ ቀዳማይ ፡ ወዳግማይ ፡ ወሣልሳይ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይትነሰት' ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝን ቱ ፡ መካናት ፡ አብድንቲሆሙ ፡ ለልሂቃን ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ኢታወፅኦሙ ፡ እ ምዝየ ፡ ወእመአኮ ፡ ኢይትሐነጽ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ሉልያኖስ ፣ ከመ ፡ ያውፅኡ ፣፡ አብድንቲ ሆሙ ፣ ለቅዱሳን ፣ እምውእቱ ፡ መክን ፡ ወያውፅይዎሙ ፡ በአሳት ። ወሶበ ፡ ረከቡ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፣ መጥምቅ ፡ ወለሥጋሁ ፡ ለኤልሳዕ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወፈቀዱ ፡ ከ መ ፡ ያውዕይዎሙ ፡ በአሳት ። ወመጽኡ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ወወሀቡ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ለ
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. SECOND DAY OF SANE (May 27). On this day were discovered the body of Saint John the Baptist and the body of Saint Elisha, prophet, disciple of Elijah, the zealous. This fact took place in the city of Alexandria, in the time of Lulyanos (Julian) the Apostate, when he wanted to rebuild the temple of the Jews in Jerusalem, after the destruction of it by Asbasyanos (Vespasian) emperor of Rome and his son Tito (Titus). For Julian wanted, in his evil design, to reduce to nothing the word of Our Lord, in the holy gospel, which says that there will not be left here stone upon stone that will not be overturned. Having rebuilt the temple, a first, a second, and a third time, the reconstruction was destroyed. Then the Jews said to him: "It is because there are in these places the bodies of great Christian personages; either remove them from there, or one will not build there at all." Lulyanos therefore ordered the Christians to remove the bodies of the saints from this place and to consume them by fire. When they found the bodies of the saints John the Baptist and Elisha the prophet, one wanted to consume them by fire. But there came faithful who gave much money to the soldiers and carried away the bodies of the saints, after the soldiers had made them swear not to leave the bodies
ሐራ ፡፤ ወነሥኡ ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ እምድኅረ ፣ አምሐልዎሙ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ከመ፣፡ ኢ ያትርፍዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይሥማፅዕ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወኢያጥፍኦሙ ። ወዜናሁሰ ፡ ለሉልያኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ዓገትዎ ፣ ጸላእቱ ፣ በው ስተ ፡ ፀብእ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ መርቆርዮስ ፣ በመንፈስ' ፣ እምድኅ ረ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ። ወረገዞ ፡ በከ‹ናት ፡ ውስተ ፣ ክሳዱ፤። አምቅድመ ፣ ይሙ ት ፡ ሉልያኖስ ፡ ከሐዲ ፡ ”ነሥአ ፡ እምደሙ ፡ ምልአ ፡ እራሑ፤ ወዘረወ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አ የር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኦኢየሱስ ፡ ንሥአ ፡ ለነፍስ ፡ ዘወሀ”ብከኒ ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ሞተ ፡ በእኩ ይ፣0ሞት ። ወሥጋሆሙሰ ፡ለቅዱሳን ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ መጥምቅ ፡ ወኤልሳዕ ፡ነቢይ ፡ አብጽሕዎ ሙ ፡ እሎንቱ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ አትናስዮስ ። ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ፍሥሐ ፡”ወአንበሮሙ ፡እስከ ፡ አመ ፣ የሐንጽ፣ ሎሙ ፡ቤ ተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ አባ ፡፣ አትናስዮስ ፡ ይነብር ፣፡ ውስ ተ ፡ አአዋድ ፡ ዘለአበዊሁ ፣ ወምስሌሁ ፣ ቴዎፍሎስ ፡ ጸሐፊሁ ። ወይቤ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ወሀ በኒ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ አነ ፡ አሐንጾ ፣ ለዝንቱ" ፡ መካን ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክ ርስቲያን ፡ በስሙ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ዮሐንስ ፣ መጥምቅ ፣ ወለኤልሳዕ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወአነብር ፣ ሥ ጋሆሙ ፡ ውስቴታ ። ወሶዕበ ፣ ተሠይመ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ቴዎፍሎስ ፡ ተዘከረ ፣ ውእተ ፡ቃለ፣ ዘተናገረ ፡ አባ ፡ አትናስዮስ ፡ ወሐነጸ ፣ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወነሥአ ፣ ምስሌሁ ፣፤ ብዙኃ
in this city, for fear that the emperor would learn of it and have them perish. As for the story of the emperor Lulyanos, when his enemies had surrounded him, during the war, God sent S. Margoryos in spirit, after he had received the crown of the martyr, and he pierced his neck with a javelin; before dying, Lulyanos the Apostate took a handful of his blood and threw it into the air saying: "Jesus, take back the soul that you gave me." And at that instant he died a bad death. As for the bodies of Saint John the Baptist and Elisha, the prophet, these people transported them to the city of Alexandria to Saint Abba Athanasius. He felt a lively joy and deposited them at his home until he had raised a church for them. One day when Abba Athanasius was in the domains of his parents, accompanied by Tewoflos his secretary, he said: "If God gives me happy days, I will make of this place, (I will build there) a church under the name of the saints John the Baptist and Elisha the prophet, and I will deposit their bodies there." Once ordained, Saint Tewoflos remembered this word that Abba Athanasius had pronounced; he built the church, then had himself accompanied by a numerous clergy and all the people. One went near the bodies of the saints and carried them away in great pomp, to bring them to the church

During the procession, one arrived near a house where there was a

አረማዊት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ነበረት ፡ ፀመዓልተ ፡ በማሕጽ ፣ ማሕምም ፡ ዘወሊድ ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ት ጸርኅ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ወመዓልተ ፡ እስከ ፡ አልጸቀት ፡ ለመዊት ። ወሶበ ፣ ሰምዓት ፡ ማኅሌቶ ሙ ፡ ለክሀናት ፡ እንዘ ፡ የኃልፉ ፡ ምስለ ፣ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ወነጸረት ፡ እመስኮ ተ ፡ ቤታ ፡ ወተስእአለት ፡ ወትቤ ፡ ምንት ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ ወይቤልዋ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱ ስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ መጥምቅ ፡ ወሥጋሁ ፡ ለኤልሳዕ ፡ ነቢይ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ዓብፅአት ፡ በአሚኖ ት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ ኦቅዱሱ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ድኅንኩ ፡ እምዝን ቱ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ አንሰ ፡ እከውን ፡ክርስቲያናዊተ ። ወእምቅድመ ፡ ይትፈጸም ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቃ ል ፡ እምአፉሃ ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ወለደት ፡ ሕፃነ ፡ ሕያወ ፡ ወሰመየቶ ፡ ዮሐንስ ። ወእምድኅረ ዝ ፣ ተጠምቀት ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ቤታ ። ወሥጋሆሙሰ ፡ ለቅዱ ሳን ፡ አንበርዎሙ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወአስተርአየ ፡ አምሥጋሆሙ ፡ ተአም ራት ፣ ወመንክራት ፡ ብዙኃት፤ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ አባ ፡ ቴዎፍሎስ ፣ ወብዙኃን ፣ ቅዱሳን ፡ ርአይዎሙ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣፡ ዮሐንስ ፣ ወለኤልሳፅ ፡ ነቢይ ። ወእሙንቱ ፡ የዐውዱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡፣ ምስለ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይቄድሳ ፡ ለቤተ ፡፣ ክርስቲያን ። ወ ኮነ ፡ አርአያሁ ፡ ለዮሐንስ ፡ መጥምቅ ፡ " ብአሲ ፡ ዘጸጐር ፡ ወጽሕሙ ፡ ይወርድ ፡ ኀ በ ፣ እንግድዓሁ ፣ ወኤልሳዕ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ነዊኅ ፣ ቆሙ ፡ ወበራህ ፣ ርእሱ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፣ ሰማዕተ ፡ ረሰዩ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በትንብልናሆሙ ፡ ለዙሎሙ ፡ ለዓለመ ፤ ዓለም፣ አሜን።
pagan woman who, for four days, had been in the grip of the pains of childbirth; she was crying day and night; she was near death. When she heard the singing of the clergy who were passing with the bodies of the saints, she looked from the window of her house, and asked saying: "What is this?" One said to her: "These are the bodies of the saints John the Baptist and Elisha the prophet." And immediately she made a vow, with faith, saying: "O saint of God, John! if I am delivered from this torment, yes, I will be a Christian." These words had not yet completely left her mouth, when she brought into the world a living child and she named him John. In the following, she received Christian baptism as well as all the people of her house. As for the bodies of the saints, they were deposited in the church. They were the cause of many wonders and miracles. The holy abba Tewoflos, he, and many holy personages, while circulating in the church with the patriarch while the latter was consecrating it, saw Saint John and Elisha the prophet. John the Baptist had an entirely hairy appearance, and his beard descended onto his chest. Elisha, the prophet, was of tall stature and had a bald head. Later, when the holy abba Magares (Macarius) bishop had undergone martyrdom, one placed his body with theirs. May God have mercy on us, through the intercession of them all, in the centuries of centuries! Amen.


[p. 536] {#po.1.p536}

[Salutation, I say, to the manifestation of the body of John the Baptist and the body of Elisha, without separation; Tewoflos, seeing that they were able to heal the pagan woman of the pains of childbirth, seeing their wonders, offered them praise and supplication.]
ተአምሪሆሙ ፡ ቴዎፍሎስ ፡ ርእዮ ፡፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፣ ዕረፍቶሙ ፣ ለቄርሎስ ፣ ወአኬልጥስ፤' ፣፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ወቀውስ ጦስ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ዘአግዓዚ ።፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ አሜን ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። አመየለሰኔ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አፅረፈት ፣ ቅድስት ፡ መስተጋድልት ፡ ወንጽሕት ፡ ማርታ ። ዛቲ ፡ ቅ ድ"ስት ፡ ኮነት ፡ እምው"ሉይ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ምስር ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ አበዊሃ ፡ ብዑላን ፡ ፈድ ፋደ ። ወፈተወት ፡ ርኩስና ፣ ወዝሙተ ፡ እምንእአሳ ። ወኮኑ ፣ ውሉደ ፡ ልሂቃነ ፡ ሰብ አ ፡ ወክቡራኒሆሙ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሃ ፡ በኅቡእ ። ወሶዕበ ፡ ተከሥተ ፡ ግብራ ፡ አግሃ ደት ፡ ገቢረ ፡ ርኩስና ፡፣ ወኮነት ፣ ትዜሙ ፡ ገሃደ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ልደት ፡ ቅድስ ት ፡ ዘለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ሖረት ፡፣ ኀበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወፈቀደት ፡ ከመ ፡-ትባ
[On this day also took place the death of Qerellos and of Akeltes, martyr, and of Qawstos, Ethiopian monk. May God have mercy on us, through their prayer! Amen.] In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. THE THREE OF SANE (May 28). On this day died the holy penitent and pure Marta. This saint was originally from the land of Egypt; her parents were very rich. She felt the desire for debauchery and impurity from her youth, and the sons of the elders, of the chiefs of the people, and of great personages, came secretly to her. Her conduct having been discovered, she gave herself publicly to her impudic actions and began to prostitute herself, quite in broad daylight. When arrived the feast of the holy nativity of Our Lord Jesus Christ,


[p. 537] {#po.1.p537}
እ ፡ወከልአ ፡ ዓቃቤ ፡ ፕኅተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ አንቲሰ ፡ ኢይደልወኪ ፡ ከ መ ፡ ትባኢ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እስመ ፡ አንቲ ፡ ርኩስት ። ወሶበ ፡ አገበረ ቶ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ተኅሥሥ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትባእ ፡፣ ወውእቱ ፡ ይከልአ ፡ኮነ ፡ ማአከሌሆ ሙ ፡ ጽልእ ፡ ዓቢይ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ይእተ ፡ ሁከተ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ወበጽ ሐ፣፡ግበ ፣ ፕኅተ፣።ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ከመ ፡ይርአይ ፡ ምንት ፡ዘኮነ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእያ፣ኤ ኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ለማርታ ፡ ይቤላ ፡ አኮኑ ፡ ታአምሪ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ቤት ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወንጹሕ ፡ ወአንቲሰ ፡ ርኩስት ፡ ወኢይደልወኪ ፡ከመ ፡ ት"በኢ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ፣ አግዚአብሔር ። ወበከየት ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ እምይእዜ ፣ ኢይትመየጥ ፣ ወኢይኤብስ ፡ አላ ፣ ለእመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ተወከፍከኒ ፡ አነ ፡ እኔስሕ ፣ ወእመነኩስ ። ወይቤላ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ አ ንሰ ፡ ኢየአምነኪ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢያምጻእኪ ፡ ብፅለኪ ፣ ወኩሎ ፡ ንዋየኪ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ወ ናውዕዮ ፡ በቅድሜኪ ፡ በእሳት ። ወሖረት ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤታ ፡ ወጾረት ፣፡ ዙሎ ፡ ንዋ ያ ፡ ወሰርጐሃ ፡ ዘብዙኅ ፡ ጫጦሙ ፣ ወአብጽሐቶ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ወወገረቶ ፡ ቅ ድሜሁ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያውዕዩ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ንዋያ ፡ በእሳት ፡ ወአውዓይ ዎ ፡በቅድሜሃ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ወ”ላጸየ ፡ ሥዕርተ ፡ ርአሳ ፡ ወአውፅ አ ፡ አልባሲሃ ፡ ወአልበሳ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ጸጐረ ፡ ፀምር ፡ ወፈነዋ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ደናግል ። ወተጋደለት ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ደብር ። ወኮነት ፡ ወትረ ፡ ትጌሥጽ ፡
she went to the church and wanted to enter; but the guardian of the door of the church (ostiarius) prevented her and said to her: "You, it is not fitting for you to enter the house of God, for you have an impure conduct." She pressed him by asking him to enter, but he prevented her, and a strong dispute arose between them. Hearing this tumult, the bishop rose and came to the door of the church to see what had happened. When the bishop saw Marta, he said to her: "Do you not know that the house of God is a holy and pure house, and you, you are impure, and it is not fitting that you enter the house of God." Then she wept and said to the bishop: "Henceforth I will not return to my sins; but if you welcome me well I will do penance and I will be a nun." The bishop said to her: "I will not believe you at all, if you do not bring your fortune and all your riches here and we will consume them by fire in your presence." Then she went quickly to her dwelling, took all her goods and her finery which were of great price, brought them to the bishop and threw them before him. The bishop had all her riches that she had brought consumed by fire, and they were burned in her presence, of Martha. Then the bishop prayed and cut her hair, made her leave her clothes, clothed her in a wool garment and sent her to a monastery of virgins. She devoted herself in this monastery to the exercises of a great penance: she was always reproaching herself and saying: "If one did not let you enter these places built of stones and clay (in the church),
ርእሳ ወትብል ፣፤ ለእመ ፡ ኢኀደጉኪ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትባኢ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መካናት ፡ አለ፣ ሕኑጻን ፡ በአእባን ፡ ወፅቡር ፣ እፎ ፡ ትከውኒ ፡ በኀበ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ ማኅደር ፡ ብርሃናዊ ት፤' ፡ ምንተ ፡ ዘአገብር ፡ ወመኑ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘይትሐበየኒ ፡ በህየ ። አው ፡ መኑ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘይተነብል ፡ በእንቲአየ ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ትቤ ፡ በጸሎታ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢተክህለኒ ፡ ከ መ ፡ እጾር ፡ ኃፍረተ ፡ እምዓቃቤ ፡ ኖኅተ ፡ ቤተ ‹ ክርስቲያንከ ። ወአንስ ፡ እስአለከ ፡ ኦ እግዚእየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢተስተኃፍረኒ ፡ በቅድመ ፣ መላእክቲከ ፣ ወቅዱሳኒከ ። ወነበረት ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትትጋደል ፡ ፳ወ፳ዓመተ ፡ እስክ ፣ ሞአቶ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ ጸላኢ ። ወኢወፅአት ፡-እምአንቀጸ ፡ ደብር ፡-መጠነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ፣
how will you be in these luminous dwellings of paradise? What will I do? Who will be my guarantor in this place? Or, who is it who will intercede for me?" And she was accustomed to say in her prayer: "O God! if it was not possible for me to endure an affront from the guardian of the door of your church, I, O my Lord, I pray to you, do not cover me with shame before your angels and your saints." This saint remained in this penitent life for 25 years, until she had conquered Satan, the enemy; she did not cross the doors of the monastery during all this time, until she died in peace. May her prayer and her blessing be granted to us in the centuries of centuries. Amen!


[p. 538] {#po.1.p538}
አዕረፈት ፡ በሰላም ። ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡፣ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ ለማርታ ፡ ጥዕምተ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ወዜና ፡፡ ዘተአጽፈት ፡ ሠቀ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ወርቅ ፣ መኒና ፡=፡ አመ ፡ ገሠጻ ፡ ኢትባእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ እንበለ ፡ ንጽሕና ፡=፡ እስከነ ፣ ሞአቶ ፡ ለእኩይ ፡ ሕሊና ፡፡
[Salutation to Marta, sweet of memory and of renown; she who wore the sackcloth, despising the gold garments. When the guardian of the door (ostiarius) rebuked her saying: "Only purity enters the church", he was the cause of her victory, even over bad thoughts.]
ዓቃቤ ፡ ኖኅት ፡ ምክንያተ ፡ ኮና ፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አላርዮስ'፣ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆኦስ ፡ ሰማዕተ ። ዝንቱ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ገሠጾ ፡ ለዮላንክዮስ" ፡ ከሐዲ ፡በእንተ ፡ አምልኮቱ ፡ ለጣዖታት ። ወአውሥአ፡፣ ዮላንክዮስ ፡ አንተሰ ፡ ትቤለኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ አነ ፡ ከሐዲ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ኢያምለክ”ዎ ፡ ለዘ ፡ ሰቀል
On this day also was martyred Saint Alaryos, bishop. This saint rebuked the emperor Yolankyos (Julian) the Apostate regarding the worship he rendered to idols. Yolankyos answered him and said to him: "You, you tell me that I am an impious man when I do not serve the one whom the Jews crucified; but

here I am, through a crowd of tortures and torments, I will make you leave his worship." Then, he delivered him to one of his magistrates, with the order to make him undergo great torments every day and without mercy. The latter therefore took him and made him endure in turn all kinds of tortures, and he continued to torment him for the space of an entire year, without ceasing to torment him, not even a single day in this year. They threw the saint into the fire, but he stood there, glorifying God and exalting His holiness. As a result of this wonder a great number of people believed in Our Lord Jesus Christ, and underwent martyrdom. Then they pulled him from the fire, as if he were coming out of a garden. When the magistrate was tired of torturing him and no longer knew what to do with him, he gave the order that his head be cut off; they therefore cut it off, his holy head, by the sword, and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May God have mercy on us, through his prayer and through the prayer of all those who were martyrs with him. Amen.
ሎቱ ፡ ወበጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፣ አሜን ። ሰላም ፣ ለኢላርዮስ ፡ ዘተአገሠ ፡ ሥቃያተ ፡=፡ መጠነ ፡ ፳ዓመት ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ጽርዓተ ፡=፡ ሽ እንዘ ፡ ኢይፈርህ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ዘያንበለብል ፡ አሳተ ፡= ማእከለ ፡ እቶን ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡፡ እስከ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ አምኑ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡
[Salutation to Ilaryos who endured torments for a year, without respite; fearing in no way the fire that flames, in the middle of the furnace he made a prayer, to the point that many believed and were martyrs.]


[p. 540] {#po.1.p540}

In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. THE 4 OF SANE (May 29). On this day, it is Saint Sanusi, martyr, from the city of Balkim. This saint, in his youth, guarded his father's sheep; he gave his food to the young shepherds, every day, and he, he fasted until the evening, without his parents knowing his conduct; he also visited the sick and the prisoners. One night, the angel of God appeared to him who said to him: "Rise, O Sanusi, go toward the governor and confess your God, to receive the crown of martyrdom." Upon his awakening, he told his mother of the appearance of the angel of God; she became sad, wept, but could not hold him back. He heard speak of a holy woman from the city of Sabra, whose name was Marya, she received the poor and the travelers and showed them much mercy. This saint went to her and he agreed with her that both would receive the crown of martyrdom. They went together to the governor named Orsanos; they found him on a ship at the edge of the sea, of the river of Egypt, and cried out in his presence saying: "We are openly Christians." He then ordered them to be tortured, and they were tortured by every kind of torment. Saint Marya gave up the spirit in the middle of the torture and received the crown of martyrdom. As for Saint

ሥአት ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ሰኑሲ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይት”ዔገሥ ፡ በውስተ ፣ ኩነኔ ፡ በኃ ይለ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘየኃድር ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ወሶበ ፣ ደክመ ፡ መኩንን ፡ አምኩ‹ነኔሁ ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ኅበ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ሀገረ ጳአንዴናው ፡ ወኩነኖ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ አንዴናው ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስቀሩ ፡ ሰኮናሁ፡ወ ይስሐብዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ኩልንታሃ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ወኢበጽሖ ፡ወ ኢምንትኒ ፡ እሙስ”ና ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አምጽአ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መሠርየ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ አክሚ ም ። ወሠረየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ጽዋዓ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ መስቴ ፡ ወድሙር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሕምዝ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስተዮ ። ወዓተበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፣ በትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወሰተ ዮ ፡ ወኢበጽሖ ፡ እሙስና ፡ ወኢምንትኒ ። ወዕበ ፡ ደክመ ፡ መኩንን ፣፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ አ ዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርአሶ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ርአሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምፅ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወከማሁ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መሠርይ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አምነ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ኢ የሱስ ፡-ክርስቶስ ፡ ወነሥአ ፣ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
Sanusi, he showed patience in the midst of torments, thanks to the strength of Our Lord Jesus Christ, which rested upon him. When this magistrate found himself at the end of his strength in torturing him, he sent him with a great number of martyrs to the governor of the city of Andênäw (Antinoopolis), who tortured him greatly, and he ordered his foot to be pierced and for him to be dragged through the entire city. They did to him what they had been ordered to do, but not the slightest harm came to him. Then this magistrate had a sorcerer brought to him from the land of Akhmim, who performed incantations over a cup containing a beverage mixed with poison, and he ordered him to drink. The saint made the sign of the cross over himself, then drank it, and not the slightest harm came to him. At the end of his resources to torture him, the governor ordered his head to be cut off; they cut off his head with the sword, and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. That sorcerer who believed in Our Lord Jesus Christ was also a martyr like him, and received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May God have mercy on us, through their prayer! Amen, in the ages of ages, amen!
ሰላም ፡ ለሰኑሲ ፡ ምቱረ ፡፣ አብራክ ፡ ወእድ ፡፡ ወኅርው ፡ ሰኩናሁ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ንድ ፡=፡ ለማርያም ፡ ሰላም ፣ መፍቀሪተ ፡ ነግድ ፡፡ ዘኩነንዋ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወሣቀይዋ ፡ በዓውድ ፡= ምስለ ፡ መሠርይ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ዘአምነ ፡ በወልድ ፡
[Salutation to Sanusi, to whom knees and hands were cut off, whose foot was pierced (carved) like a trunk. To Maryam, salutation, the friend of the traveler (of pilgrims): She was tormented, she was tortured with him, at the tribunal, with the sorcerer also, who believed in the Son.]


[p. 542] {#po.1.p542}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካክዕበ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ዘእምሀገረ ፡ ሐረቅሊ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ስመ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ዘካርያስ ፡ መኩ›ንነ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንጠርስን ፣ ወአብልያ ፡ ወስ መ ፡ እአሙኒ ፡ ኤልሳቤጥ ፡ ወአሙንቱሰ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተጸገው ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ለዝ ንቱ ፡ ወርኅ ፡ ሰመይዎቻ ፡ ስሞ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ እስመ ፡ አሉ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ኅቡራነ ፡ ስም ፡ ምስለ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወአሙ ፡ ለዮሐንስ ፡ መጥምቀ ፡ አምላክ ።፡ ወእምዝ ፣ አልሐቅዎ ፡ በተግሣጽ ፡ ወበፈሪሃ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወመሐርዎመ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ክርስቲያን ፡ ወሐሳባተ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ጥበበ ፡፡ ወነበረት ፡ አሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ታማኅፅኖ ' ፡ አምጣነ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ መዋፅሊሃ ፡ ከ መ ፡ ኢይድኃፅ ፡ እምፍኖተ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ወከማሁ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ይጌሥጾ ፡፡ ወ እምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አእረፈ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወኃደጎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ወል ደ ፡ ፳ዓመት ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መስፍነ ፡ ህየንተ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትዔዘዙ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በሐውርተ ፡ ኦንጦስ ፡ ወሐረቅለ. ፡ ወካልአት ፡ አህጉር ።፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ሀሎ 0፣ በአሐቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይ ቤለከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሑር ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ወአውስብ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ኑማር ያኖስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትንግሥ ፡ አንተ ፡ ወትንበር ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ መንበር ። ወቅ ዱስሰ ፡ ሐለየ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ቦኑ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ሰይጣናዊ ።፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ እ ምክመ ፡ ሰማዕኩ ፡ ስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምአፉሁ ፡ አሐውር ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡=፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ወተራከቦ ፡ ለዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ወዲዮቅልጥያኖስሰ ፡ አፍቀሮ ፡ ወአክበ
[On this day, also, Yohannes (John) of the city of Haragli (Heraclea) was a martyr. The father of this saint, named Zakaryas, was prefect of the cities of Antorsen (O: Antarsen) and Ableya. The name of his mother was Elsabet. Both of them, when they were gratified with this child on the 5th of this month, called him by the name of Yohannes, for they bore the same name as the father and mother of John who baptized God. Then they raised him in good education and in the fear of God, and taught him the scriptures of the Church, arithmetic, and all science. His mother continued to watch over him, all her life long, so that he would not fall out of the ways of God, and likewise his father gave him admonitions. Later, his father died; he left this holy child aged 20. He was prefect in place of his father and under his jurisdiction were the provinces of Pontos (Pontus), Haragli (Heraclea), and other regions. It was in this situation that one night Satan appeared to him, in the guise of an angel, who said to him: "Thus speaks God to you: go to the city of Antioch, and marry the daughter of King Numaryanos (Numerian) so that you yourself may reign and sit with him on a single throne." But the saint reflected and said: "Could this angel not be a satanic angel? Yet, as I have heard the name of God from his mouth, I will go to the city of Antioch." He came there, therefore, and met with Diyogeltyanos (Diocletian), who


[p. 543] {#po.1.p543}
ሮ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ አመ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ርአዮ ፡ ወበሳኒታሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምሳሕ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዕልው ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ”ያምጽእዎ ፡ ለአኦሎን ፡ ወአምጽ እዎ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ወአስተሐቀሮ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ጣዖት ፣ ወለንጉሥኒ ፡ ረገሞ ፡ ወጸአሎ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ።፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፣ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ተምዕዓ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ መዐተ ፡ ወአሠሮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ 0፣ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ በሠረገላ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ወ አኃዞ ፡ ወሰዓሞ ፡ አፈ ፡ በአፍ ፣፡ ወአወፈዮ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ኪዳነ ፤ ወአመ ፡ ሳኒታ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፣፡ ወአውፅኦ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ነዓ ፡ ስግድ ፡ ለአኦሎን ፡ ወለአርዳሚስ ። ወቅዳስሰ ፡ ዓበየ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እፎ ፡ አሰግድ ፣ ለግቡራን ፡ በአድ ፡=፡ ወንጉ ሥሰ ፡ተናገ"ሮ ፡ በብዙኅ ፡ ጊጣነ ፡ ነገር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይፈቅድ ፡ ተዓርቆ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወእም ዝ ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ያስተዋፅእ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ፀባሕተ ፡ ወለአከ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ስርያቆስ ፣ መኩ-ንነ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ወወሀብክዎ ፡ ሥልጣነ ፡ ለዮሐንስ ፡ ሐረቅ ሊ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ጸባሕት ፡ እምኢየሩሳሌም ፡ እስከ ፣፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ አብያተ ፡ አማልክት ሂ ፡ ያንሀል ፡ ወይሕንጽ ፡ በሠናይ ።፡ ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ምክንያተ ፡ወአንሐለ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ጣዖት ፡ ወረሰዮሙ ፡ ምዝቡራነ ፡ ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ነ በረ ፡ ስርያቆስ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምኩናን ፡ ከመ ፡የአዝዝ ፡ መቲረ ፡ አርእስቲሆሙ-፡ ለሰማዕታት ፡=፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እምስዒድ ፡ እለ ፡ ይብልዎሙ ፡ ዲዮስቆሮስ ፡
showed him much affection and honor the day he saw him. But, the next day, as John was in his company at the meal, the impious Diyogeltyanos ordered Apollo to be brought; they brought him into his presence. But the saint, having seen him, mocked this idol, and he cursed the emperor also and covered him with insults because of this. Seeing the saint's action, Diyogeltyanos fell into a violent rage and had him bound in prison. While the saint was in prison, Our Lord appeared to him on a chariot of light, and, in his company, angels. He took him and kissed him mouth to mouth and gave him many promises. The next day, Diyogeltyanos sent to have him taken out of prison, and said to him: "Come, prostrate yourself before Apollo and before Ardamis (Artemis)." The saint refused and said: "How shall I prostrate myself before works of hand?" The emperor addressed many seductive words to him, wanting to be reconciled with him. Then he sent him to the province of Egypt so that he might collect the taxes for him, and dispatched a message about him to Seryagos, governor of Egypt, and he said: "I have given power to Yohannes of Haragli for all the taxes from Jerusalem to Ethiopia; he must also demolish the temples of the gods and rebuild them well." But the saint seized this pretext to overthrow all the idol temples, and put them in ruins. After these days, the


[p. 544] {#po.1.p544}
ወቢፋሞን ፡ ወአህራጋኖስ ፡ ወኪሮን ፡ ወዮልዮስ ፡ ወእለ ፡ እስክንድሮስ ፡ ወዮሴፍ ፡ ወ ይስሐቅ ፣ ወእስጢፋኖስ ፡ እሉ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ከልሑ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ገሃደ ፡=፡ ወአሜሃ ፡ ተምዕዓ ፡ መኩንን ፣ ወከ፡።ነኖሙ ፡ እንበለ ፡ ምሂክ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ዮሐ ንስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኃደገ ፡ ሥርዓተ ፡ ምስፍናሁ ፡ ወፀርኃ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ክር ስቲያናዊ ፡ አነ ፡ ገሃደ ። ወተምዕዓ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ይእስርዎ ፣ በመዋቅሕት ፡ ዘ ሐን ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ሀገረ ፡ እንጽና'፣ ኀበ ፡ አርያኖስ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወውእቱሂ ፡ ሐተቶ ፡በ እንተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ዘኮነ ፡ ወተምዕዓ ፡ እምሰሚዓ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስቅልዎ ፣፡ዲበ ፣፡ ሕንባዝ ፣ ወየአጽርዎ ፡ በመንኩራዥረ ፡ ሐን ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፣ ያውርድዎ ፣ እምዲ በ ፡ሕንባዝ ፡ ወይቅሥዋዎ ፡ በአስዋጥ ፡ ከርሦ ፡ ወርአሶ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተመስወ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፣ ወተመልአት ፡ ምድር ፡ እምደሙ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፣ ይደይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡= ወካዕበ ፡ አምድኅረ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ይጥብሑ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ በመጥባሕት ። ወእ ምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ያምጽኡ ፡ ሠቀ ፡ ፀጉር ፡ ወይመዝምዙ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ቀነስሎ ።፡፡ ወዓዲ ፡ ነጸፉ፣ ሎቱ ፡ ሐመደ ፡ ወአፍሐመ ፡ እሳት ፡ ታሕተ ፡ ገበዋቲሁ ።፡፡ ወክዕበ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ያምጽኡ ፡ ቅንዋተ ፡ ሐን ፡ አለ ፡ ርሱናት ፡ በእሳት ፡ ወያንብርዎሙ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ገጸ. ፣ ወውስተ ፡ እዘ ኒሁ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ከመዝ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወደይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ። ወእም
governor Seryagos sat in his tribunal to give the order to cut off the heads of the martyrs. All at once there came people from upper Egypt, called Diyosqoros, Bifamon, Ahraganos, Kiron, Yolyos, and Eskenderos, Yosef, Yeshaq, Estifanos, all of them crying and saying: "We are openly Christians." Then the governor became irritated and tortured them without mercy. Saint Yohannes, at this sight, abandoned the administration of his powers and cried out, saying: "I am a Christian openly." The governor became angry and had him bound with iron chains and sent him to the city of Ensena, near the governor Aryanos. The latter questioned him about everything that had happened and became irritated to hear his words; he ordered him to be suspended on the instrument of torture, and to be compressed with an iron wheel. Then he ordered him to be taken down from the instrument of torture and to strike, with whips, his belly and his head, to the point that his flesh fell and the earth was filled with his blood; then he had him thrown into prison. Again, after a few days, he had his flesh slashed with a knife; then he had a piece of haircloth brought and his wounds wiped with it; they also spread ashes and burning coals on the ground under his flanks; he also had iron spikes reddened in the fire brought and placed on his face and in his ears. They did this to him; then, they threw him into prison. Then the angel of God appeared to him, that night, and gave him the salutation. The next day, they brought him out and tortured him with rods of burning iron. After all this, they bound his hands and


[p. 545] {#po.1.p545}

[On this day also, Agronyos and Dimunäsya, his sister, Amoni and Minas, Egyptians and soldiers of the emperor Diyogeltyanos (Diocletian), were martyrs. These saints, when they had been thrown by the emperor into the lions' den (O: into the fire with abba Ebsoy), the angel Gabriel came and brought them out of the lions' den (O: fire). Then the saints, having come toward the emperor, addressed curses to him, saying to him: "Why do you delay,

one day after another and do not prescribe to finish our affair?" Hearing this, the emperor became irritated and ordered their heads to be cut off with a sharp sword and their bodies to be burned in the fire. It is thus that they finished their martyrdom and celebrated the feast with the saints in the kingdom of heaven. May God have mercy on us, through their prayer, in the ages of ages. Amen.]

On this day also one celebrates the holy abba Amon martyr and saint Sofiya with him. May God have mercy on us, through their prayer, in the ages of ages Amen. [On this day also the holy and pure Sofya was a martyr, who endured harsh combats, as well as her brother Amonyos, in the time of Diyogeltyanos (Diocletian), the impious. This saint had Christian parents. When they had brought her into the world, they gave her an excellent education in

the fear of God and taught her the scriptures of the Church and all the law of the Apostles. Then, when she had grown up, they wanted to marry her to one of the magistrates of the city. Having known this, the saint of God rose from the place where she was sitting and, her face turned toward the east, she performed one hundred prostrations, stood up, and made a long prayer. And she said in her prayer: "My Lord Jesus Christ, son of God, you whose praise is immense, guide me, O Lord, me your servant Sofya, in your straight path, by which I may arrive toward you, and do not let me wander in the tortuous path that leads to perdition. And do not let my spirit think of the corruptible ways of the world and all the desires of impurity that my parents project against me. Do not let me, O my Lord, me, your servant, become the servant of sin and do not make me serve and carry the heavy weight of the desire of the world; but make my neck incline so that it may carry the light yoke of your obedience." Having finished her prayer, she sat on her bed and having remained thus a little, a thought came to her which said: "I will rise and go out in secret; I will travel a long road, I will enter into the interior of a convent and I will serve the Most High." When she had finished thinking, she called her servant whom her parents had given her to serve her, and commanded her to bring wine and give her to drink. She brought it to her, and Sophie having tasted a little,


[p. 548] {#po.1.p548}
ወይን ፣ ወትቤላ ፡ አምጽኢ ፡ ልብሰኪ ፡ ወንሥኢ ፡ ልብስየ ፡ ወወሀበታ = ወአምዝ ፣ አዘዘታ ፡ ከመ ፡ትኑም ፡ እስመ ፡ አንበዘ ፡ ልባ ፡ እምስካር ። ወሶበ ፡ ከብደት ፡ አመታ ፣ እምንዋም ፡ ወፅአት ፡ በኅቡእ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘርእያ ፡ እስመ ፣ ማኅደራ ፡ ርቅ ፡ እምአበ ዊሃ ፍ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ሖረት ፡ ወበጽሐት' ፡ ኀበ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ወረከበት ፡ ሰብአ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይጐይዩ ፡ እምዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ይፈቅዱ ፣ ይትኃብኡ ፡ ውስተ ፣ አድባር ፡ ወበአታት ። ወአውሥአት ፡ ወትቤሎሙ ፡ እምአይቱ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ ወነገርዋ ፡ ለለ ፣ ሀገሮሙ ። ወትቤሎሙ ፡ አይቴ ፡ ተሐውሩ ፡ ወነገርዋ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ።፡ ወትቤሎሙ ፡ ቦኑ ፡ ታበጽሐኒ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ፍኖ ት ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፣፡ ወይቤልዋ ፡ አወ ። ወሖረት ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ወጸለየት ፡ጸሎተ ፡ ነዊኃ ፡ ወሖረት ፡ ወበጽሐት ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ወተአመነት ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ፣0፣ ስብሐት ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ስመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። መል ዓ ፡ መዓተ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ያቅርብዋ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐት ፡ ይቤላ ፡ ስግዲ ፡ ለአኦ ሎን ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ አፎ ፡ አሰግድ ፡ ለግብረ ፡ እደ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ለጣዖታት ፡ ርኩሳን ።፡ ወበጊ ዜሃ ፡ ተምዕዓ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ይቅሥፍዋ ፡ በአስዋጠ ፡ ኃዊን ፡፡ ወካዕበ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ይደዩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ክሳዳ ፡ እብነ ፡ ክቡደ ፡ ወይስሐብዋ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ፕፃ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይት
ordered her servant to drink, to the end, the wine she had brought. The latter drank, and, when her spirit had been ravished as a result of the drunkenness of the wine, her mistress said to her: "Bring me your garment and take mine"; and she gave it to her. Then she commanded her to sleep because her spirit was weakened as a result of the drunkenness. When her servant was weighed down by sleep, Sophie went out in secret and without being seen, for her dwelling was far from her parents. Having arrived at a road, she met people who were fleeing Diyogeltyanos because of the faith, and who wanted to hide in the mountains and the caverns. She spoke in her turn and said to them: "Where are you from?" And they told her each his country. She said to them: "Where are you going?" And they told her the whole story of their flight because of Diyogeltyanos and the faith. She said to them: "Does this road lead me toward Diyogeltyanos?" And they said to her: "Yes." Then she walked a little and made a long prayer, then walked again and arrived toward Diyogeltyanos, and confessed Christ - may he be praised! - Diyogeltyanos, upon hearing the name of Christ - may he be praised! - was filled with anger, and had her approach into his presence; when she had come, he said to her: "Prostrate yourself before Apollo." And she said to him: "How shall I prostrate myself before the work of a human hand, before filthy idols?" At the hour, Diyogeltyanos became irritated and ordered her to be flogged with whips trimmed with iron. Then he also commanded a heavy stone to be placed on her neck and to be


[p. 549] {#po.1.p549}
ነተፍ ፡ ሥጋሃ ፣ ወእምዝ ፡ ወደያ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወበሳኒታ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ያምጽእ ዋ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ሠዒ ፡ ለአፅሎን ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢትሙቲ ፡ እኩየ ፡ ሞተ ። ወአውሥአቶ 0፡ወ ትቤሎ ፡ አኩይሰ ፡ ሞት ፡ ለከ ፡ ውእቱ ።፡ ሊተሰ ፡ ሞትየ ፡ በኀቤከ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ውእቱ ፡ በኀበ ፣ አምላኪየ ። ወይቤላ ፡ ቦኑ ፣ እምድኅረ ፣ አሞትኩኪ ፡ ተሐይዊ ። ወአውሥአ ት ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ኦአብድ' ፡ ኅድግሰ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ለከሂ ፡ ትንሣኤ ፡ ጽኑሕ ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ ትንሣ ኤከሰ ፡ ዘለደይን ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ጣዖታቲከ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ቃለ፣7፣ ተምዕዓ ፡ መፀተ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ይዝብጥዋ ፡ በአብትረ ፡ ሐናን ፡ እስከ ፡ ይሰበር ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ኦ ፅፅምተ ፡ ሥጋሃ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ባቲ ፡ ከመዝ ፡፡ ወካዕበ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ያስክብዋ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዓራተ ፤ ሕን ፡ ወያንድዱ ፡ እሳተ ፡ መትሕቴሁ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ባቲ ፡፡ ወሶበ ፡ መስየ ፡ ብሔር ፡ አ ዘዘ ፡ ይደይዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወበይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ መልአከ ፣፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወገሰሳ ፡ ወፈወሳ ።፡ ወበሳኒታ ፡ አዘዘ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምጽእዋ ፡ ወ መጽአት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ተሐውር ፡ በአገሪሃ ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ትመስል ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ተሐፅበት ፡ ውስ ተ ፡ ቤተ' ፡ ብለኔ ፡ ወወፅአት ፡ ወተቀብዓት ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእያ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ተሐውር ፡ በእገሪሃ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ርእዩ ፣ ጽንዓ ፣ ሥራያ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊት ፣ ትማልም ፡ " አሕመምናሃ ፡ በአሳት ፡ ወዮምሂ ፡ ጥፅየት ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ሶፍያ ፡ ሥራይሰ፣ኢ
dragged on the sand until her body was torn. Then he threw her into prison. The next day, he had her brought and said to her: "Sacrifice to Apollo, so as not to die a bad (terrible) death." She answered him and said to him: "The bad (terrible) death, it is you who will have it; for as for me, to die near you is to live near my God." He said to her: "Is it that, after I have put you to death, you will live?" She answered and said to him: "O fool! not only me, but you too, the resurrection awaits you; but your resurrection is for condemnation in the company of your idols." Hearing this word, he became strongly angry and ordered her to be beaten with iron rods, until all the bones of her body were broken. They did this to her. [He] also commanded her to be laid on an iron bed and to light a fire underneath this bed; they did this to her. When it became evening, he had her thrown into prison. But that very night, the angel of God came near her, who touched her and healed her. The next day, the emperor ordered her to be brought, and she came, walking on her own feet, and she herself was just as if she had washed in a bath, and that, having come out, she had rubbed herself with oil. Diyogeltyanos, seeing her walking on her own feet, said: "See the strength of the charm of this Christian: yesterday we subjected her to the torture of fire, and today she is full of health." Saint Sophie said to him: "Of charm, I know none; but on the contrary my God conjures charms." Immediately the emperor used cajolery to make her consent;


[p. 550] {#po.1.p550}
የአምር ።፡፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ አምላኪየ ፡ ፈታሔ ፡ ሥራያት ፣ ወሶቤሃ ፡ሄጣ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ከመ ፡ ኦ ሆ ፡ ያብሀላ ። ወሶበ ፡ ዓበየት ፡ አዘዘ ፣፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሳ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ይወስድዋ ፡ ይምትር ዋ ፡ ወኦስተርእያ ፡ ላቲ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡=፡ ወተካየዳ ፡ ኪዳናተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ወይቤላ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ ተዝካረኪ ፡ ወዘይሁብ ፡ ምጽዋተ ፡ በስ ምኪ ፡ ወሉ ፡ ዘይጽሕፍ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ገድልኪ ፡ አነ ፡ እትዌከፎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መንግሥት የ ፡ ዘበሰማያት ።፡ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ መተሩ ፡ ርእሳ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአት ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስም ፅ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያ”ት ። እግዚአብሔር ፣ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎታ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለ
and, as she refused, he ordered her head to be cut off; while they were taking her away to cut it off, Our Lord Jesus Christ appeared to her - may he be praised! - He made her many promises and said to her: "Whoever will make your memory and will give alms in your name, and whoever will write the book of your life, I, I will receive him into my kingdom which is in the heavens." Then they cut off her head with the sword and she received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May God have mercy on us, through her prayer, in the ages of ages! amen.]
ም ፡አሜን'። ለክ ፡ አሞንዮስ ፡ አቄርብ ፡ ሰላመ ፡፡ ወለሶፍያ ፡ ምስሌክ ፡ ዘተሳተፈት ፡ ሕማመ ፡፡ በእንተ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሥመሩ ፡ ፍጹመ ፡፡ ከዊነክሙ ፡ ትሕፅኑ ፡ ኪያየ ፡ ደክታመ ፡=፡
[To you, Amonyos, I offer a salutation, and to Sofya, who was your companion in torture. For Jesus Christ, may it please you entirely to raise me, an orphan, by being, you, father, and she, mother.]
ለሊከ ፡ አበ ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ አመ ፡፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ ስምዓ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ አባ ፣ ባሞን ፣ ወአዝንሐስ ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ።
[On this day also, abba Bamon and Aznehas were martyred.] In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God.


[p. 551] {#po.1.p551}
አመደለሰኔ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ምሥራቃዊ ፡ ተአማኒ ። ዝ ንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ተጸምደ ፤ ወተጋደለ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በውስተ ፡ 8ደብር ፡ እምአድባ ራተ ፡ ሀገረ፡ ምሥራቅ ፣ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ዘየዓቢ ። ወበመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለሉልያኖስ ፡ ከሐዲ ፡ ወበመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለዋልንጦስ ፡ መሃይም ን ። ወሶበ ፡ ተቀትለ ፡ ዋልንጦስ ፡ መሃይምን ፡ ወነግሠ ፡ ዋልስ ፡ እሁ ፡ ሀየንቴሁ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ አርዮሳዌ ፣ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ለአርዮሳዊያን ፡ ከመ ፡ ያርኅው ፡ አብያተ ፡፤ ክርስቲያ ናቲሆሙ ፡ወገብሩ ፡በከመ ፡ አዘዘ። ወአፀወ ፣ አብያተ ፡ "ክርስቲያናት ፣ ዘርቱዓነ" ፡ ሃይ ማኖት ፡ እስከ ፡ ይበውኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖቱ ፡ እኪት ። ወበጽሐ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ትአዛዝ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ መንግሥቱ ዞ፡ ወነደ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ”ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በጸጋ ፡ መንፈ ስ ፡ ቅዱስ" ፡ ወተንሥአ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቀሩስጥንጥንያ ። ወረከቦ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ እን ዘ ፡ ይወፅእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ይፃብኦሙ ፡ ለጸላእቱ ፡፣ እለ ፡ ተንሥኡ ፡ እመንገለ ፡ ምፅ ራብ ። ወቆመ ፣ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ አነ ፡ እስአለከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታርጉ ፤ አብያተ ፡ክርስቲያናቲሆሙ ፡ ለመሃይምናን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጸልዩ ፡ በእንቲአከ ፡ ወይትራድእ ከ'፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ጸላእትከ ። ወለአመ ፡ ኢገበርከ ፡ዘንተ ናሁ ፡ እግዚአብሔ ር ፡ ይገድፈከ ፡ ወትጐይይ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ጸላአትከ ። ወተምዓ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡
THE 5TH OF SANE (May 30). On this day died the holy Father abba Ya'qob (James) the Oriental, confessor. This saint served God and struggled, devoted himself to asceticism for him in one of the monasteries of the East, in the time of Constantine son of Constantine the Great, in the time of Lulyanos (Julian) the Apostate and in the time of Wlentos (Valentinian) the faithful; when the faithful Walentos was killed, his brother Wales (Valens) reigned in his place, and he was an Arian. [He] ordered the Arians to open their churches, and they did according to his order; he closed the churches of the orthodox, until they had entered into his perverse faith. This order reached all the provinces of his empire. But the heart of this saint was inflamed with the grace of the Holy Spirit; he rose, and came to the city of Questentenya (Constantinople), and met the emperor at the moment when he was leaving to make war on his enemies who had risen against him from the side of the west. This saint placed himself before him and said to him: "I pray you to open the churches of the faithful, so that they may pray for you and that God may help you against your enemies; if you do not do it, he will reject you and you will flee before your enemies." The emperor became irritated and commanded him to be


[p. 552] {#po.1.p552}
ይዝብጥዎ ፤ ወይሞቅሕዎ ። ወይቤሎ ፡፤ ቅዱስ ፡ አእምር ፡ ወአንተ ፣ ትጐይይ ፣ በቅድ › መ ፡ ጸላእትከ፣ ወትመውት ፡ "በዋፅየ ፡ አሳት ። ወመጠዎ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ለዘ ፡ የዓቅቦ ፡ እስ ከ ፡ ይትመየጥ ፡ በዳኅና ፡ በከመ ፡ ሐለየ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ለአመ፣ አንተ ፡ተመየጥከ ፡ በዳኅና ፣ ኢተናገረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውስተ ፡ አፉየ ። ወሶበ፣ይቤ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘንተ ፣ ነሥአዎ ፣ ወዘበጥዎ ፣፡ ወሞቅሕዎ ። ወንጉሥሰ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፃብኦ ሙ ፡ ለጸላእቱ ፡ ወሶዕበ ፡ ቆመ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሰራዊቱ ፡ አንጻረ ፡ ጸላእቱ ፡ ገደፎ ፡ ወል ደ ፡ አግዚአብሔር ፡ ፈጣሪ ፡ ዘፅሩይ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አቡሁ ፡፣ ዘከሐዶ ፡ ውእ ቱ። ወተራደእዎዖ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ አርዮስ ፡ በጸሎታቲሆሙ ፡ ወጐየ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ጸላ”እቱ ፡ ወአሙንቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይተልውዖ ፡ እስከ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ዓጸድ ፣፡ ወአውዓይዋ ፡ በአሳት ፡ ወጉ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ዚአሃ ። ወተረፈ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወአሊአሁ ፡እለ ፡ የአምኑ ፡ በሃይ
beaten and imprisoned. The saint said to him: "Know it then: you will flee before your enemies and you will die consumed by fire." Then the emperor delivered him to a guard until he returned safe and sound, as he thought; and saint Ya'qob said to him: "If you return safe and sound, God has not spoken by my mouth." After these words of the saint, they took him, beat him, and imprisoned him, and the emperor left for war against his enemies. When he found himself with his troops in front of his enemies, he was abandoned by the Son of God, creator, equal to God his father, whom he had denied. The partisans of Arius helped him with their prayers, but he took flight before his enemies, the latter pursuing him, until he had penetrated into a village to which they set fire; its inhabitants fled. He remained with his own who shared his perverse faith and they were all consumed by fire.
ማኖቱ ፡ እኪት ፡ ወውፅዩ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ በእሳት ። ወተመይጡ ፤፡ እለ ፡ ተርፉ ፡ አምሰራዊቱ ፡ ወገ“ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቀሩስጥንጥንያ ፡ ወ ዜነውዎሙ ፡ ለመሃይምናን ፣ ዘንተ ፡ ዜና ፣ ሠናይተ ። ወተፈጸመ ፡ ትንቢቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ወተጋብኡ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ወአውፅአዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ በክብር ፡ ዓቢይ ። ወጠየቁ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ወከሐዲያን ፡ ከመ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መለኮታዊት ፡ ወመንፈሰ ፡ ትንቢት ። ወተመይጡ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ እምሕዝበ ፡ አርዮ ስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወሰገዱ ፤ ታሕተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወተአምኑ' ፡ ከመ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዕሩይ ፡ ምስለ፣ አብ፣በ
The remains of his army returned and fled to the city of Questentenya (Constantinople), and they told the faithful this admirable story: the prophecy of this saint had been fulfilled. The faithful, having gathered, brought this saint out of prison, in great pomp. Faithful and infidels saw well that upon this saint rested the divine grace and the spirit of prophecy; also a great number of Arians returned to the faith of Our Lord Jesus Christ. They prostrated themselves at the feet of this saint and confessed


[p. 553] {#po.1.p553}
መለኮቱ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሐይወ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ በከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ በተጸምዶት ፡ ወተጋድሎት ፡ ወአምዝኺ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ትፍሥሕተ" ፡ ዘለዓለም ። አግ ዚአብሔር ፣ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
that the Son of God is equal to the Father in his divinity. Later, this holy Father lived as he had done at first in the service of God and asceticism; after which he died in peace and received the eternal joy (O P: of life). May God have mercy on us by his prayer and may his blessing remain with us. Amen.
ሰላም ፡ ለያዕቆብ ፣ በብሔረ ፡ ምሥራቅ ፡ ዘአስተርአየ ፡፡ ከዊኖ ፣ በንጽሕ ፡ ለአግዚአብሔር ፡ ቅኑየ ፡=፡ ላዕለ ፡ አርዮሳዊ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተነበየ ፡=፡ አምገጸ ፡ ጸላእቱ ፡ ዘበኀበ ፡ ዐፀድ ፣ ጐየየ ፡=፡
[Salutation to James who appeared in the land of the East; he was, with purity, the servant of God. As he had predicted against the Arian emperor, in the village where the latter fled from before his enemies, with his troops, all at the same time, he was consumed by fire.]
ምስለ ፡ ሠራዊቱ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ በአሳት ፡ ውዕየ ፡፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ "ስምዓ ፡ ኮነ"፣ አባ ፣ አብዕይ ፡ ኅሩይ ፡ መስተጋድል ። ለ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ መስተጋድል ፡ ኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ወያመልክዎ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡፡ ወየዓቅቡ ፡ ትእዛዛቲሁ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ስመ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ታግሥጦስ ፡ መኩን ነ ፡ ቃው ፡ ዘአንጾኪያ! ፡ ወአሙሂ ፡ ክርሳ ፣ ወአሙንቱሂ ፡ ነበሩ ፣ እንዘ ፣ አል"ቦሙ ፥ ውሉድ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ፲ወኒዓመት ፡ ወየኃዝኑ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ በእንተዝ ፡ ወበአሐቲ ፡ አመዋፅ ል ፡ በሌሊት ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ ለታግስጦስ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ብርሃናዊ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፡ ኦመፍቀሬ!" ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ናሁ ፡ ተሰምዓ ፡ ጸሎትክ ፡ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ናሁ ፡ ብ እሲሷትከ ፡ ትወልድ ፡ ለከ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ወትሰምዮ ፡ ስሞ ፡ እብዕይ ፣ ወይከውን ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ኅ
[On this day also Abba Ebsoy was a martyr, an ascetic of choice. This holy ascetic had Christian parents who worshipped Christ - may he be praised! - and kept his commandments. His father was named Tagestos; he was governor of Qaw (O: and) of Antioch; his mother was called Kersa. They remained without a child for the space of seventeen years, for which they were strongly saddened. But one day, once, during the night, appeared to Tagestos a man all covered with light who said to him: "Salutation to you, O friend of God; here is that your prayer has been heard by God; here, your wife will give birth to a son for you and you will call him by the name of Ebsoy; he will be an instrument of choice for God


[p. 554] {#po.1.p554}

and will be a martyr for Christ", - may he be praised! - "He will be a great martyr; he will endure numerous torments and will receive the crown of life." [He] told his wife the vision he had had.

And after a few days she conceived, then brought into the world the child, the first of Miyazya. Then they raised him in science, wisdom, and the fear of God. When he was seven years old, his parents sent him to the land of Akhmim and entrusted him to an old doctor, whose name was Yosef (Joseph). He remained there devoting himself to fasting and prayer with his brother Petros (Peter) and performing miracles and wonders there. After he had reached his twenty-seventh year, the governor of Upper Egypt came to the city of Qaw. Now this governor proscribed the Christians and killed a great number of the faithful. Then came perverse people who denounced these saints to him, abba Ebsoy and abba Petros, saying: "There are two deacons who do not obey your word, and they refuse to prostrate themselves before the gods." Then, he had them brought and placed before him, and questioned them about all their conduct. Hearing their words, he had them imprisoned. At the end of three months, the angel of God appeared to them who said to them: "I am Gabriel, messenger of good news; God has sent me toward you to announce to you that your memory will remain forever; for you, my beloved Ebsoy, there is reserved for you

Then the governor had him taken out of prison and forced him to prostrate himself before the idols; the saint refusing, he ordered the guards to have him mount the instrument of torture and to compress him strongly there. Then descended the archangel Gabriel who broke the instrument of torture, touched his body, and healed him. After, they threw him into prison where he healed many sick people. Then the governor ordered him to be taken out of prison, to be bound in the bottom of a boat, to attach iron chains to his neck and iron shackles to his hands and feet, and to give him neither bread nor water. It is thus that he remained for ten days; the next day the governor had him taken out and placed in his presence and said to him: "Prostrate yourself before the gods." Upon his refusal to do it, he commanded him to be tortured and to be bound strongly on the instrument of torture. They compressed him so strongly that his blood flowed like water; after which, they chained him in prison. He was there, when again appeared to him the archangel Gabriel who said to him: "Courage, O chosen of God, Ebsoy; do not fear; I will be with you until you finish your course."
ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አብጽሕጾ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቅልቅልያኖስ ፣፡ መኩንን ፡

flogged to the point that his limbs fell to the ground. Then having boiled oil in the fire, they poured it into his ears and his mouth and on his chest. [He] also commanded burning torches to be lit and his flanks to be burned. He also had "mirwad" of iron brought, reddened in the fire, and put them in his eyes. Likewise, he had the muscles of his hands and feet removed so that his limbs would detach, and they did this to him. But immediately came the archangel Gabriel who touched his limbs and healed him. Then they had him come before Diyogeltyanos who tortured him by fire, pitch, sulfur, and with vine shoots; but, from this again, the archangel Gabriel saved him. He also gave the order to throw him into the sea after having attached to his neck, by iron chains, a large stone; they rolled this stone into the sea and dragged it while it was attached to his neck; they plunged him up to mid-body and they tied the other half of the body to a large column planted on the edge of the sea, so that he would not be submerged, and they left him in this torture until the moment of the sixth hour of the day. Then the angel of God descended and made his flanks sound and safe, as before. head fall on the shore of the sea; he took his body and put it in a skin; but his nape applied itself to his body and was as if it had not been cut at all; as for the skin, it began to walk alone, without being carried, until it arrived at the city of Alexandria, the 20th day. [On this day also occurred the martyrdom of Saint Bifamon. This saint was the son of the brother, nephew of the father of Yohannes (John) of Haragli (Heraclea). THE 6TH OF SANE (May 31). On this day is the glorious Saint Tewodros (Theodore), monk and martyr of the city of Alexandria. This saint was a believer and pure: he served God and accomplished the divine combat. He lived in one of the convents of the city of Alexandria. When Constantine, son of Constantine the Great, sent to the city of Alexandria the patriarch named Gargeyos (George), who was an Arian, he sent with him considerable troops who drove Saint Abba Athanasius, the apostolic, from his seat, and established this impious Gargeyos as patriarch of the city of Alexandria, after great struggles had taken place between the inhabitants of the city and the king's soldiers, who killed a large number of the inhabitants of Alexandria. After Gargeyos the impious had sat upon the seat of Mark the Evangelist, he to whom it was not fitting to sit there, he was informed that this holy father Tewodros was contradicting the partisans of Arius, triumphing over them, covering them with confusion, and making them understand their impiety. He ordered him to be seized and tortured; they did to him what he had ordered, but not the slightest harm came to him.

Then this impious man, to whom the title of patriarch does not befit,

ጳሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይአስሩ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወእገሪሁ ፣ ለዝንቱ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ወይእስርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እገሪሆሙ ፡ ለአፍራስ ፡ ዕፁባት ፡ ”ዘእንበለ ፡ ይትለጐሙ' ፡ ወያረውጹ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዐውድ ፡ ስፉሕ ። ወዕበ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተበትኩ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መልያልያቲሁ ፡ ወተ ነትፈት ፡ ርእሱ ፡በበምታር ፣ ወመጠወ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘኮ ነ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፣ በእንቲአሁ ። ወነሥአ ፣ አክሊላተ ፡ ይበእንተ ፣ ሃይማኖተ ፡ እግዚእነ ፣ ኢ የሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ሱታፌ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ መሃይምናን ። ወዳግማይ ፡ አክሊል ፡ በእንተ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወአኦምልኮ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ዘፈጸመ ። ወሣልሳይ ፡ አክሊል ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስምዑ ፡ ወምትረተ ፣ መልያልያቲሁ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕ ት ። ወአምድኅረዝ ፡ አስተጋብኡ ፡ መሃይምናን ፣፡ መልያልያተ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ወአ ንበርዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሣጹን ፡ በክብር ፡ ዓቢይ ። ወገብሩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በዓለ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዛቲ፣ ፅለት ፣ ወደ”ረሱ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ውዳሴ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ጸሎት ፡ በልሳነ ፣ ሮማይስጥ ፡ በከመ ፡ ኩሎ ሙ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወጸሐፍዎ ፣ ውስተ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ጸሎታት። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡
ordered the hands and feet of this saint to be bound and attached to the feet of spirited and unbridled horses that would drag him while running over a vast space. When this was done to him, all his limbs were broken, and his head was cut into pieces. And he surrendered his soul into the hands of God, for whom he had been martyred, and received three crowns: one for the faith in Our Lord Jesus Christ, in which he was the companion of all the faithful; a second crown for the struggles of the monastic life and the admirable worship of God that he accomplished; and the third crown he received for the martyrdom and the dismemberment of his limbs for the orthodox faith. Then the faithful gathered the members of the saint's body and deposited them with great pomp in a reliquary. [15 held a feast for him on this very day and composed his eulogy to be read at the time of prayer, in the language of Rum (Greek) as for all the saints, and inscribed it in the book of prayers. May his prayer and his blessing be with us forever and ever. Amen.
ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፣ ቴዎድሮስ ፣ መነኮስ ፡፡ አሱረ ፡ አፅዳው ፣ ወአዕጋር ፡ በእግረ ፡ ፈረስ ፡ አምኃይለ ፡ ሩፀቱ ፡ ፍሉሕ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ነፋስ ።፡ እምአባልከ ፡ ተመቲራ ፡ ለአንተ ፡ ሰረረት ፡ ርእስ ፡= እግዚአብሔር ፡ አስተቀጸላ ፡ በአክሊል ፡ ሠላስ ፡
[Hail to you, Tewodros, monk, bound by the hands and feet to the foot of a horse. The head which, detached from your body, leaped from the horse by the violence of its course, boiling and rapid as the wind, God crowned with three crowns.]


[p. 561] {#po.1.p561}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ከዕበ'፣፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለጓሰማዕታት ፣ ዘመርዩጥ ፡ ወጓሰማዕታት ፡ ካልአን ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ መነኮስ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለዓ ለመ ፡ ዓለም ። አሜን ።
[On this day also there is a commemoration of the forty martyrs of Maryut, of forty other martyrs, and of Gabra-Krestos, monk. May God have mercy on us through their prayer. Amen.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ኮኑ ፣-፪መኳንንት ፡ዐበይት ፡ "ዘሀገረ ፡ እስና" ፡ እ ሉ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አውሳፍዮስ ፡ ወታማን ፣ ሐርዋግ ፡ ወባኮስ ። ወኮኑ ፣ አዕማዲሃ ፡ ወጸሐፍ ታ፣ለሀገር፣ ወይመጸውቱ ፡ ብዙኃ' ፡"ላዕለ ፡ ነዳያን ፡ ወምስኪናን" ፡፡ ወሶበ ፣ ተመይጠ ። አርያኖስ ፡ መኩንን፤' ፡ ዳግመ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ተቀበልዎ ፡ እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወከል ሑ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ገሃደ ፡ ምእመናን ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ቀዳማዊ ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ቆሙ፣0፣ ሰማያት ፡ ወምድር ፡፡ ወሰሚዖ ፡ አርያኖስ ፡ ኩነኖሙ ፡ በዘዘዚአሁ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡፡ ወሶበ ፡ ደ ክመ ፡ እምኩንኖቶሙ ፡ መተረ ፡ አርእስቲሆሙ ፡ በሰይፍ ፣ ወፈጸሙ ፡ ስምዖሙ ፡ በ ረከቶሙ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስሌነ ፣ ለዓለመ ፣ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also, four great magistrates of the city of Esna were martyrs; they are: Ausafyos, Taman, Harwag, and Bakos; they were the pillars and secretaries of the city and gave great alms to the unfortunate and the poor. Upon the second return of Aryanos the governor to this city, these saints came to meet him and cried out before him, saying: "We are Christians openly, believing in Our Lord Jesus Christ"—may He be praised!—"the primordial Word of God, by whom the heavens and the earth were established." Having heard them, Aryanos inflicted all kinds of tortures upon them. When he was finished tormenting them, he cut off their heads with the sword and they completed their martyrdom. May their holy blessing be with us forever and ever. Amen.
ሰላም ፡ እብል ፡ መኳንንት" ፡ ርብዓ ፡=፡ ዝያጸግቡ ፡ ርበ ፡ ወያረውዬዩ ፡ ጽሙዓ ፡፡ ሀገሪተ ፣ እስና ፡ ያመዝብር ፡ አመ ፡ አርያኖስ ፡ መጽአ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ክርስቶስ" ፣ ከመ ፡ ይኩኑ ፡ ስምዓ ፣ እምነ ፡ ሕዝቦሙ ፡ "ቀደሙ ፡ ወጺአ" ።
Hail, I shall say, to the four magistrates, who satisfied the hungry and gave drink to those who were thirsty. When Aryanos came to desolate the inhabitants of the city of Esna, they were the first to go out from their people to be martyrs in the name of Christ.]


[p. 562] {#po.1.p562}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ከዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ' ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አማንዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀ ገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ሂበመዋዕሊ ሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ሶፍያ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፣ ወ፻ያአዋልዲሃ ፡ ወዐቀበ ፡ መር ዔቶ ፣፡ በሠናይ ፣ ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለአግዚአብሔር ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በሚመቱ ፡ ፲ወ፻ዓመተ ፡ ወአ እረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also died the holy father Amanyos, patriarch of the city of Alexandria; among the number of the patriarch fathers, he is the seventh. In the time of this father, Saint Sophia was martyred, as well as her three daughters. He guarded his flock well and was pleasing to God, and he remained in his office for thirteen years, then died in peace. May God have mercy on us through their prayer forever and ever. Amen.]
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። አመኒለሰኔ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. THE 7TH OF SANE (June 1st).
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ክኩር ፡ ወመዋኢ ፡ አባ ፡ እስኪሮን' ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ዘእ ምሀገረ ፡ ቀሊን ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አምሐራሁ ፣ ለአርያኖስ ፣ መኩንነ ፡ ሀገረ ፣ አንዴናው ዞ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ትእዛዙ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፣ ከሐዲ ፣ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ "”በአምል ኮ ፡ ጣዖታተ" ። ተንሥአ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ በማአከለ ፣ ሕዝብ ፡ ወረገሞ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወለአማልክቲሁ ፡ ወኢደፈረ ፡መኑሂ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩንኖ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሐራ ፡ አላ፣ ሞቅሕዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ ጽርሑ ፡ ለመኩንን ።፡ ወሶበ ፡ ሖረ ፡ መኮንነ ፡ ሀገረ ፣ አንዴና ው፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አስዩጥ ፡ ፈነውዎ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ፳ሐራ ፤
On this day is the holy and glorious victor Abba Eskiron (O, P: Abaskiron) martyr of the city of Qalin. This saint was one of the soldiers of Aryanos, governor of the city of Andenaw (Antinoou). When the order of the impious emperor Diyogeltyanos (Diocletian) arrived to worship idols, this saint stood up in the midst of the crowd and cursed the emperor and his gods. No one dared to judge him because he was a soldier; but he was imprisoned in the governor's house. When the governor of the city of Andenaw went to the city

of Asyut, this saint was sent to him in the company of five other soldiers whose names are: Wa'alfyos (? A: Walelafyos, P: Walfeyos), Armasyos (O P: Armanyos), Arkiyas (A: Armiyos), Petros, Qiranyon (O: Qaranyo, P: Qaranyos). These had agreed with Abba Eskiron to shed their blood for the name of Our Lord Jesus Christ. When they stood before the governor of Andenaw, he ordered their belts to be cut and for them to be tortured; and it was done to them. As for these five soldiers, some were hanged, others had their heads cut off, and they received the crown of martyrdom. As for Saint Abba Eskiron, the governor ordered him to undergo a harsh flogging, then to have the skin of his head cut down to his neck; it was done to him. Then he was tied to the tail of a horse and dragged through the whole city, then he was put into a pan full of molten lead, the opening of which was closed over him when he was inside. Then he was thrown into the furnace of a bath. During all this torture, the angel of God, having come to him, supported and consoled him, exhorted him to patience, and made him stand up safe and sound, without harm.

sun and the moon, to rise into the air, and to converse with the stars. The sorcerer commanded the door of the bathhouse to be closed, and for this bathhouse to be sprinkled with urine. It was done according to his order. He took a serpent, pronounced words over it, and it was broken into two pieces; he took the venom and the liver and placed them on a brass tray, cooked them, brought them to Saint Abba Eskiron, and made him enter the bathhouse; he gave him this cooked venom and he ate it. Then this magician cried out, saying: "O chief of the princes of demons, exercise your power over this Christian!" But as not the slightest harm came to the saint, this magician marveled; and Saint Abba Eskiron said to this sorcerer: "Satan, to whom you asked for help that he did not lend you, will torment you by the power of Our Lord Jesus Christ." Immediately an evil demon possessed this magician and began to make him spin until he confessed Our Lord Jesus Christ. Then the governor had the head of this magician cut off (O, P: by the sword) and he received the crown of martyrdom.

As for Saint Abba Eskiron, the governor, as his anger increased against him, inflicted a harsh torture upon him and cut off his testicles. During all this torment, this saint glorified Our Lord Jesus Christ. After which the


[p. 565] {#po.1.p565}
ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርአሶ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፣ ስ ምዕ ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። እምተአምራቲሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አስመ ፡ሀለወቶ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ውስተ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ዓጸድ ፡ በደበበ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ካህናተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ይገብ ሩ ፡ ምግባረ ፡ እኩይ ' ፡ ወጸንሖሙ ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመየጡ ፤ አምእከዮ ሙ፡ ወይነስሑ ። ወኢተመይጡ ፡ወኢነስሑ ፡ወሰአለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለአግዚአብሔ ር ፡ ወአምጽአ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ሕማመ ፡ ብድብድ ፡ ወሞቱ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ በአሐዱ ፣ ጊዜ ። ወሖረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይንን ፣ ዲበ ፡ ፈረሱ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገር ፣ እንተ ፡ ስማ ፡ ብያሁ ፡ በላዕላይ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ንዋም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ይትና ገሩ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ በብርሃነ ፡ ወርኅ ፡ አምቅድመ ፡ ይኑሙ” ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጴዓን ፣ ዲበ ፡ ፈረሱ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡፣ ሰላም ፡ ለክሙ ። ወሶ በ ፡ርእይዎ ፡ ተንሥኡ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወተቀበልዎ ፣ ወይቤልዎ ፡ "ወአንተ ፡ ሰላም፣፤ለከ ፡ ኦ እግዚእነ ፣ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተሀቡኒ ፡፣ ንስቲተ ፣ ምድረ ፣ ወአመ ረ ፡ በኩናቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ምድር ፡ ወይቤ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ የአክለኒ ። ወአውሥአዎ ፡ ወይቤልዎ እወ ፡ ኦአግዚአነ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዝከ ፣ ይኩን ። ወወሀቦሙ ፡ ፻ዲናረ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡ ወተሰወ ረ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ቦቤሃ ፡ ወአንከሩ ፣ እምራእዩ ፡ ወእምነገሩ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ኖሙ ፡ እ
governor had his head cut off; the head of Saint Abba Ischyrion was cut off by the sword, and he received the crown of martyrdom in all perfection in the kingdom of heaven. Among the miracles of this saint is the following. There was a church in an enclosure in the north of Egypt; the priests of this church had bad conduct. This saint waited for them to convert from their crime, but they did not repent nor did they convert. The saint prayed to God and He brought the plague upon them and they all died in a single instant. Then this saint came, mounted on his horse, and arrived at the city named Beyahu, in Upper Egypt, at the time of sleep, while the inhabitants of the city were conversing with each other in the moonlight before sleeping. The saint arrived toward them, mounted on his horse, and said to them: "I greet you." At the sight of him, they rose immediately, came to meet him, and said to him: "To you also, greetings, our lord!" The saint said to them: "I desire that you give me a little land"—and he pointed to a place on the ground with his javelin, and said: "This is enough for me." They answered him and said to him: "Yes, our lord; as you have commanded us, so be it." And he gave them a hundred gold denarii, then suddenly disappeared from them. Immediately they marveled at his appearance and his word. After these people had fallen asleep in their homes, the saint transported his church from the north of Egypt


[p. 566] {#po.1.p566}
ሉንቱ' ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያቲሆሙ ፡ አፍለላ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያኑ ፣ አምደ በብ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወአብጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ብያሁ ፡ በላዕላይ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወሶዕበ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ፣ በጽባሕ ፡ ረከብዋ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ አንከሩ ፣ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወ ሰብሕዎ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ። አምውእቱ ፡፣ ጊዜ ፣ ይትገበር ፡ ባቲ ፣ ተአምራት ፡ ብዙኃ ት ፡ እስከ ፡ ዮም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፣ ወትንብልናሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ አስኪ
and brought it to the city of Beyahu in Upper Egypt. Rising in the morning, the inhabitants of the city found this church, experienced great feelings of admiration, and glorified God. Since then until today, a crowd of miracles has taken place there. May the prayer, the blessing, and the intercession of this Saint Abba Eskiron be with us forever and ever. Amen.
ሮን ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ ለአበስኪሮን ፡ ዘተዓገሠ ፣ መከራ ፡፡ እስኪቱ ፡ ወርአሱ ፣ እስከ ፣ በመጥባሕት ፣፡ ተመትራ ፡፡ በእደ ፣ አርማንዮስ ፣ ምውት ፡ ለሕይወተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘተናከራ ፡=፡ ዘተከለሉ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወዘተቀጸሉ ፡ ጌራ ።፡፡
[Hail to Abaskiron who endured the trial until his testicles and his head were cut off with a knife. Ischyrion, to them, who were crowned with the diadem, I say hail.]
ሰላም ፡ እብል ፡ ለኃምስ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ እልፍ ፡ ወ፳፻ሰማዕታት”፣ አለ፡ኩነኖሙ፣አ ርማንዮስ ፡ መኩንን ።፡ ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also were martyred the 16,000 martyrs, whom Armanyos judged. May their prayer and their blessing be with us forever and ever. Amen.]

[Hail to the sixteen thousand who were counted, besides those who were burned and besides those who were exiled abroad. They who completed the suffering

አርአስተ ፡ ፀርየ ፡ ከመ ፣፡ በአእጋር ፡ ይኪዱ ፤ መንገለ ፡ ነገድኩ ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ ይንግዱ ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተርኅወት ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣፤ ዘእግዝእትነ ፡ " ቅድስት ፣ድ » ንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ በመራኅብተ ፡ ዘዌላ ፣ በሀገረ ፡ ምስር ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ነበረት ፡ ዕፁታ ፡ ፻ዓመተ ፡ ወፄአውራኃ ። ወውእቱ ፣ አመ፲፻ዓመት ፡ ወ፳ዓመት ፣ ለሰማዕታት ፡ ጻድቃ ን ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በትንብልናሃ ፣ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፣ ድንግል ፡ ማር ያም ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ፣፡ ወአሜን። ሰላም ፣ እብል ፡ ተርኅወ ፣ ቤትኪ ፡ ዮም።=፡ ከመ ፡ ኤልያስ ፡ አርኃወ ፡ ፕኅተ ፣፤ ዝናም ። በስሱ ፣ አውራኅ ፡ ወሠላስ ፡ ዓም =፡ ማርያም ፣ ሥመሪ ፡ አርኅዎቶ ፡ ለሕሊናየ ፡ ሕቱም ፡፡
by the hand of Armanyos; may they go with me to the places where I am going, to trample underfoot the heads of my enemies.] On this day also [re]opening of the church of Our Lady, the holy Virgin Mary, in the Zawela quarter in the city of Misr (Cairo), after it had remained closed for three years and seven months, and this in the year 1020 of the era of the holy Martyrs. May God have mercy on us through the intercession of Our Lady the holy Virgin Mary, and may her blessing be with us. Amen, amen. [Hail, I say: your church was opened today. As Elijah opened the door of the rain, after six months and three years, Mary, deign to open my mind which is sealed, so that upon me may be poured out peace with justice.]
ከመ ፡ በላዕሌየ ' ፡ ይትከዓው ፡ ምስለ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ሰላም ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፣ ወወልድ ፣ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God.

THE 8TH OF SANE (June 2nd). On this day takes place the commemoration of the dedication of the church of Our Lady, the holy Virgin Mary, known by the name of "Mehsab" where is the blessed spring that Our Lord Jesus Christ caused to gush forth, with his mother, Mary, at the time of their return from the land of Egypt; for the righteous Joseph, when the angel of God appeared to him in a dream saying: "Rise, take the child and his mother and go into the land of Egypt," Saint Joseph rose and took Our Lady, Mary, Our Lord Jesus Christ, and Salome, daughter of the sister of the mother (cousin) of Our Lady Mary, and they came to the land of Egypt, to the Quesquam convent, and to other places. When they had remained in the land of Egypt for three years and seven months, Herod died and the angel of God appeared to Joseph and ordered him to return to Jerusalem. Returning, they came to the city of Maharga, then from there to the city of Misr (Cairo), from there to the city of Matarya, and from there to the city of Mehsab; then Our Lord Jesus Christ caused this spring to gush forth, being with his mother the Virgin Mary; and this spring remains until this day. Everyone comes to it from all cities and from


[p. 569] {#po.1.p569}
ግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወይትባረኩ ፣ እምቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያና ፡ ወእም ነቅዓ ፡ ማይ! ፡ ቡሩክ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በትንብልናሃ ፡ ለአግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስ ት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አ ሜን።
all races, asking for the intercession of Our Lady, the Virgin Mary, seeking blessings through her church and through the blessed spring. May God have mercy on us through the intercession of the holy Virgin Mary; may her prayer and her blessing be with us forever and ever. Amen.
ሰላም ፡ እብል ፣ ለቅዳሴ ፡ ቤትኪ ፡ ሕንጽት =፡ ኅበ ፡ አንቅዓ ፡ ወልድኪ ፡ ማየ ፡ በረከት ።=፡ ማርያም ፡ በስምኪ ፡ ስመ ፡ በጐዔት ፡=፡ ይትወሀብ ፡ ለዘጸለየ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ሰዓት ፡፡
[Salam, I say, for the consecration of your church, built where your son caused the blessed water to gush forth; Mary, to whom prays by your name, a profitable name, is (or "may be") granted, at every hour, an admirable gift and a perfect present.]
ሠናይ ፡ ሀብት ፡ ወፍጹም ፡ ፍት ፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ስምዓ ፣ ኮኑ ፣ ፲፻፪ሰማዕታት ፡ ወበዓሉ ፡ ለአትካሮን ፣ ወ አውሎጊስ ።፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝክሮሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ትማዳ ፡ ወውሉዳ ፡ ወአርማኖስ ፡ ወአሙ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለዙሎሙ ፡ አሜን ፡ ወአሜን ።
[On this day also, the 1,002 martyrs were martyred, and it is the feast of Atkäron and Awlogis. May God have mercy on us through their prayer, forever and ever. Amen.] On this day also, the commemoration of the saints Temâdâ and her children, and of Armânos and his mother. May God have mercy on us through their prayers, for all of them. Amen, amen.
ሰላም ፣ ሰላም ፣ ትማዳ ፣ ወውሉዳ ፡=፡ አርማኖስ ፡ ወእሙ ፡ ዘተካፈሉ ፡ ክበዳ ፡=፡ ጽሕሐፋነ ፡ መልክፅ ፡ ወስምፅ ፡ ለኢየሩሳሌም ፡ በውስተ ፡ ዓውዳ ፡፡
[Hail, hail to Temâdâ and her children, to Armânos and to his mother, who shared in her glory, whose malke' and martyrdom are written in the district of Jerusalem. By the blood of their martyrdom may they be my]


[p. 570] {#po.1.p570}

surety for the debt, when, for sin and justice, the retribution shall be made.]
በእንተ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ወጽድቅ ፡ አመ ፡ ይከውን ፣ ፍዳ ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። አመ ፡ ዘለሰኔ ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. THE 9TH OF SANÉ (June 3).

On this day died the great prophet Samuel. The father of this saint was named Helgânâ, of the tribe of Levi, of the race of Aaron the priest; his mother was called Hannah and she was barren. Because of her prayer and her persistence before God—may He be praised forever!—God gave her this prophet, and she raised him at home for three years, then presented him to the sanctuary of God as she had vowed before she had conceived him, and he served the priest Eli until he was grown. The sons of Eli dishonored their ministry and used violence against the children of Israel. Then God sent a prophet to Eli, and he said to him: "Behold, I hold your children and all your race in abomination; rejecting them from the priesthood, I will raise up for myself a faithful priest who will accomplish my good pleasure all the days of his life." And God raised up this great prophet Samuel. When the priest Eli was

grown old, God called Samuel one night, while he was lying down; it seemed to Samuel that it was Eli who had called him, for, at that moment, he did not yet know the vision that comes from God; he rose, came near the priest Eli, and said to him: "Did you call me, my master?" And Eli said to him: "No, go lie down on your bed." When he had gone to lie down, God called him a second and a third time, and he would rise and come near Eli.

Eli understood that God had called Samuel, and he said to Samuel: "Go, lie down; and when you are called, say: Speak, O my Lord; for behold, your servant is listening." When Samuel had gone to lie down, there came to him, from God, a voice that said: "Samuel, Samuel!" And he said to him: "Speak, O my Lord, for behold, your servant is listening to your voice." Then God spoke to him of what He had said to the priest Eli, of what his children were doing, and of what He intended to do toward the sons of Eli and the children of Israel. Then God ordered this prophet to anoint Saul, son of Kish, king over the children of Israel. When Saul had transgressed the command of God, God enjoined this prophet to anoint David, son of Jesse, king and prophet. And this

የ' ፣ ወኩነኖሙ ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ ሳሙኤል ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ አስራኤል ፡ ፳ዓመተ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰ ላም ። ወካዕበ ፡ ይገብሩ ፣ በዓሎ ፡ አመ፳ወጓለወርኃ ፡ ነሐሴ ፣ ወይእቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ ዘጸው ዖ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአዘዞ ፡ ዘይገብር" ። ወተነበየ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ትስ ብእቱ ፡ ለአግዚአእነ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ በ፲፻8ወ፳" ፡ ዓመት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የ ሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፣ አሜን ።
Samuel was judge for the children of Israel for twenty years, and he died in peace. His feast is also celebrated on the 27th of the month of Nahasë; but this day is the one on which God called him and prescribed to him what he would do. This prophet exercised the prophecy 1,035 years before the incarnation of Our Lord Jesus Christ. May his prayer and his blessing be with us. Amen.

[Hail to Samuel who grew, from his childhood, in the sanctuary of God. When long ago Saul, holding his garment with his hand so that he (Samuel) would not go away, tore his garment, God tore his (Saul's) kingdom.]

On this day also was martyred Saint Lukilyânos (?), as well as four other martyrs with him. This saint was (O P: at first) a priest of idols when he saw the sufferings of the martyrs who were being burned in the fire, whose limbs were being cut off, who were being scourged; then, when he saw regarding the many martyrs whom the Emperor Urlyânos (Aurelian) was throwing into a furnace whose fire was ardent, that the fire did not touch them at all but that they
