ቀ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጣዖታት ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ይትለአክሙ ፡ ኢይክሉ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ ከመዝ ፣፡ዳአሙ፣ለ እመ ፡ ወገርዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሳት ፣ እምውዕዩ' ። ወአምላክሰ ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ዘንተ ፡ ተአ ምራተ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘበአማን ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ጸርኃ" ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይብል ፡ አነ"፣ክር ስቲያናዊ ፡ ገሃደ ። ወአቅረብዎ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡፣ ወገሠጾ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኃዲገ ፡ መልአክ ተ" ፡ጣዖታት ። ወተካየይ ፡ ኪዳናተ ፣ ብዙኃተ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መልእክቶ ሙ ፡ ለጣዖታት ። ወኢያጽነነ?፣ለኪዳኑ ፣ ወኢሰምዓ ፡ ትእዛዞ ፡ ወኢፈርሃ ፡ እምኩነኔ ሁ ፡ ወኩነኖ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ዙነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፣ ፈድፋደ፤ ፡ ወሰበረ ፡ መንከሶ ፡ በአአባን ፡ ወ ቀሠፎ ፡ መቅሠፍተ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ "' ወሰቀሎ ፣ ቀልቀ“ሊተ'። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ወደዮ ፣፡ ውስ ተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ብዙኃ ፣ መዋዕለ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፣ አብጽሖ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ 8ክርስቲያን" ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ሙቁሐን" ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ውግሩ ፡ ፅጣነ፣ለ አማልክት ፡ ወአመአኮ ፡ እቤንነክሙ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወኢተአዘዙ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በክሕደቱ ፡ ወተምዓ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ወወደዮሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እቶነ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወአዝነመ ፡ አግዚአብሔ ር ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ” ፡ ዝናመ ፡ ብዙኃ” ፣ ወአጥፍአታ ፡ ለአሳት ፡ እምላዕሌሆሙ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሥቅልቻ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ መስቀል ፡ " እንዘ ፡ ይብል፣ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አምለከ ፡ ዘተሰቅለ" ። ወሰቀልዎ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ፅፀ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወቀነውዎ ፡
stood upright and walked in it while glorifying God as did the three children in the furnace of Babylon—then this saint was struck with admiration and he understood that the idols he served could not do the same, but that, on the contrary, if they were thrown into the fire, they would burn, and that certainly the God who performed these wonders was truly God. And immediately he cried out, saying: "I am a Christian openly." They brought him before the emperor, who reproached him for his abandonment of the service of the idols and made him a host of promises if he returned to the service of the idols; he did not yield to his promises, nor did he obey his order; he had no fear of his torture. The emperor made him undergo a harsh torture: he broke his jaw with stones, scourged him very violently, and hung him head down. Then he threw him into prison where he remained for a great number of days. After which, the emperor had him brought, and with him, four Christians who were imprisoned, and he said to them: "Offer incense to the gods, otherwise you will undergo great torments." And they did not obey him in his impiety; then he became angry with them and threw them into an ardent furnace. But God made an abundant rain fall which extinguished the fire from above them. The emperor ordered this saint to be crucified on a cross, saying: Let it be so,


[p. 574] {#po.1.p574}
ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ቅንዋተ ፡ ነዊኃተ ፡ ወመጠወ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ውስተ' ፡ አግዚአብሔ ር። "ወመተረ ፣ አርአስቲሆሙ ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፣ 8ዕደው"፣ ወነሥኡ ፣፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ አክሊለ፣ ስምፅ ፣ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ አሜን ።
since he has worshipped the Crucified." They crucified him on the cross, and they drove long nails into his whole body, and he rendered his soul into the hand of God. They cut off the heads of these four men, and they all received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May God have mercy on us, through their prayer. Amen.
ሰላም ፣ ለሉክያኖስ ፣ ዘአድምዓ ፡ ተወድሶ ፡= ምስለ ፣ ፪ፀዕደው ፡ ሥቃያተ ፡ ስምዕ" ፣ በተዓግሦ ፡፡ ዕበ ፡ ለአማልክት ፡ ኢሦዓ ፣ መሥዋዕተ ፡ ልሁል ፡ አስተኃይሶ ፡፡ ጊቬዜ ፡ ቀነውዎ ፡ መጠወ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡፡
[Hail to Lukyânos who obtained being praised by enduring the torments of martyrdom with four men. When he did not sacrifice to the gods, preferring the sacrifice of the Most High, at the moment he was nailed to the cross, he rendered his soul, after they had broken his jaw with stones.]
እምድኅረ ፡ ሰበሩ ፡ በአእባን ፣ መንከሶ ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፣ ካዕበ" ፣ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለዮሐንስ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ወአርኮላ' ፡ ጳ ጳስ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። አሜን ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። አመ፲ለ”ሰኔ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮና"፣0ቅዱሳት ፣ ሰማዕታት ፡፣ ዳባሞን"፣ወብስጣሞን ፡ ወሶፍያ ፡ እሞ ን ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ አስመ ፡ 8ብእሲ ፡ ዘስሙ ፣ ዋርስኖፍ ፡ ፈቀዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚምዎ ፡ ኤ
[On this day also, the commemoration of Yohannes, bishop, and of Arkolà (O: Arkalä), metropolitan. May God have mercy on us through their prayers, forever and ever. Amen.] In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. THE 10TH OF SANË (June 4). On this day, it is the holy virgin martyrs Dâbâmon and Bestâmon and Sophie, their mother. Here is the fact. A man, by the name of Waresnof, whom they

ኢስ ፡ ቆፅሰ ፡ ወጐየ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ጣህሙን' ፡ ዘእምደወለ ፡ በና" ። ወኃደረ ፡ በኅበ ፡ እላንቱ ፡ ቅዱሳት ። ወበይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፦ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለምንት ፡ አንተ ፡ ትነውም ፡ ወተጋድሎ ፡ "የሀሉ ፡ ወአክሊላት ፣፡ አስተዳ ለዎሙ" ። ተንሥእ ፡ ወሖር ፡ ኅበ ፣ መኩንን ፡ ወተአመን ፣ ቅድሜሁ ፡ በእግዚአእነ' ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ከመ ፡ ትንሣአ ፣ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነቅሃ ፣ አምንዋሙ ፡ አ ይድዖን ፡ ለአእላንቱ ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡ ዘከመ ፣ ርእየ ፣ ወተሰናአው ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሥ ኡ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ። ወተንሥኡ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወተ አምኑ" ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡”በስሙ ፡ ለአግዚእነ" ፣፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ”ክርስቶስ ፡ ወኩነኖሙ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወወደዮሙ ፡ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፣ ነሥኦመ ፡ "እምኅበ ፡ በል በል'፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ "ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሰንሁር ፡ ወኮነት ፡ አሞን ፡ ትተልዎን ። ወይቤሎን ፡መ ጐኩንን ፣ ውግራ ፡ ዕጣነ ፡ ለጣዖታት" ፡ ወአበያ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ጐኩነኖን ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መልአኮ ፡ ኀቤሆን ፡ ወአጽንዖን ፣ በውስተ ፣ ኩነኔ ፡ ወፈወሰ ፡ ቀቱስላቲ ሆን"። ወእምዝ" ፡ ነሥኦን ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ፃ ። ወገነውተ ፣ ጣዖታትሰ ፡ ነገር
wanted to ordain bishop, fled to the city of Tâähmun in the district of Banâ, and stayed near these holy women. That night, the angel of God appeared to him and said to him: "Why do you sleep, when there is fighting to be done and the crowns are ready? Rise, go to the governor, and, before him, confess Our Lord Jesus Christ, so that you may receive the crown of martyrdom." As soon as he awoke, he told these holy women of his vision, and they all agreed to receive the crown of martyrdom. Having immediately risen, they set out, arrived near the governor, and confessed, in his presence, the name of Our Lord Jesus Christ. He made them undergo great torments and threw them into prison; then he took them with him from Balbal to the city of Senhur. Their mother followed them. And the governor said to them: "Offer incense to the idols"; but they refused. After that, he tortured them, and God sent his angel to them; he strengthened them in the torments and healed their wounds. Then the governor took them with him to the city of 88, and the priests of the idols said to the governor, of Saint Dâbâmôn, that she was cursing the gods.

Now there was a woman, beautiful and very charitable, who prayed always without

ceasing ever; and she had a daughter named Yônâ. They wove beautiful garments of different colors, and they gave alms of what remained to them. Upon learning her story, the governor sent a lictor to cut off her head; this lictor was named Awlogi. Upon arriving near her and seeing the beauty of her face and her angelic air, he was inspired, by the grace of God, not to kill her, but he led her with him toward the governor. She therefore greeted the people of her house and left the city of Denfa. When she arrived at the city of 88, she met with Saint Waresnof and his companions and they greeted one another. The lictor Awlogi confessed Our Lord Jesus Christ, and was martyred: they cut off his head with the sword. Like him, these holy women confessed Our Lord Jesus Christ, and were martyred. Saint Dâbâmon was suspended on a wheel and they made her undergo great torments; she remained at the torture for many days; God strengthened her, gave her patience, and healed her wounds. Then, they threw her into prison where she remained for a few days; then they brought her out of prison and the governor said to her: "Offer incense to the gods." As she did not listen to his order, he commanded them to cut off her head; they took her


[p. 577] {#po.1.p577}
በክያ ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ትትፌሣሕ ፤ ወትትሐሠይ ። ወመተሩ ፡ ርአሳ ፡ በሰይፍ፣ ወከማሁ ፡ ለእማ ፡ ወአኅታ! ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶን ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡፣ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማ ያት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጽሎቶሙ ፡ አሜን ። ሂሰላም ፡ ለሶፍያ ፡ ወአዋልዲሃ ፣ ደባሞን ፡ ወብስጣሞን ፡ ዘተሳተፋ ፡፡ ይሰነዓዋ ፡ በስምዕ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ወርስኖፋ ። ' ዮና ፡ ወእማ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ድንፋ ፡=፡ እምነ ፡ ወዲቅ ፡ ኪያየ ፡ ይሕቅፋ ።
out of the city; all the women surrounded her and wept, but she, she rejoiced and exulted; they severed her head with the sword; likewise, to her mother and her sister they cut off their heads; and they received the crown of martyrdom, in the kingdom of heaven. May God have mercy on us through their prayer. Amen. [Hail to Sophie, and to her daughters Dâbâmon and Bestämon who united and agreed for martyrdom with Waresnofâ, Yônâ and her mother, from the city of Denfâ; may they embrace me with their hands, to prevent me from falling, as the hen covers her little ones with her wing.]

[On this day also, the commemoration of Aqlimos, martyr, of Abbâ Na'an and of Yesak. May God have mercy on us through their prayer, forever and ever. Amen.] On this day also, is the commemoration of the great joy, which reached the ends of the world and at which the celestial angels rejoiced; on this day, indeed, was promulgated the edict of the faithful emperor Saint Constantine the Just, in all the cities and provinces of his empire. He decreed the closing of the temples of idols and the opening of the Christian churches. This message, filled with joy, arrived at the city of Alexandria, just on this day; then they sent this


[p. 578] {#po.1.p578}
ወአምዝ ፡ ፈነውዋ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ መልእክት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዙሉ ፣፡ በ”ሐውርተ ፣ ግብጽ ። ወተፈሥ ሑ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ በልዕልና ፡ ቀርነ ፡ ሕገ' ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወክብረ" ፡ መስቀል ፡ ማሕየ ዊ ። ወዓንፀውዎሙ ፡ ለአብያተ ፡ ጣዖታት ፣ ወአርኃውዎሙ ፡ ለአብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ በዓለ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ አመ፲ወ፳ዓመተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ በ ቀዳሚት" ፣፡ ሚመቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ እስክንድሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጻጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፣ እስክንድርያ ። እ ግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ አሜን ፡፣ ወአሜን ።
message to all the provinces of Egypt. The faithful rejoiced at the elevation of the exaltation of the Christian law and the glory of the life-giving Cross; they closed the temples of the idols, opened the Christian churches, and held a feast, just on this day, and it was during the eleventh year of his reign to Constantine and in the first constitution of Alexander as patriarch of the city of Alexandria. May God have mercy on us through their prayer. Amen, Amen.
”ለዛቲ ፡ ዕለት" ፡ በዓለ ፡ ፍሥሐ ፡ ሰመይዋ ፤ ጽጉባነ" ፡ ዕደው ፡ አምሐሊበ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ጣዕዋ ።፡ መልእክተ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ጻድቅ ፣ አመ ፡ ተዜነዋ" ፡=፡ እንተ ፣ እምትካት ፡ በአምልኮ ፡ ጣዖት ፡ ተዓፅዋ ፣
[This day has been named the feast of joy by men satiated with the milk of wisdom like the little one who is still nursing. Receiving the message of the just king, the ancient temples dedicated to the worship of idols were closed, for all the Christian churches, on this day, were opened.]
አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ ኩሎን ፡ እስመ ፡ ዮም ፡ ተርኅዋ ፡ገ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፣፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። ፲ኔለሰኔ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡፤ ቅዱስ ፣ ገላውድዮስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ አርአያ ፣ መላእክት ፡ ወዘመደ ፡ መንግሥት ፡ መዋኢ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ፀብእ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ተጋደለ ፡ ተጋ
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. THE 11TH OF SANË (June 5). On this day died Saint Galâw yos, martyr, similar to the angels, of royal race, victor in the combat. This saint fought in an admirable way and

ድሎ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ዘኢይማስን' ። ወመነነ ፡ ክብረ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ኃ ላፊ ፡ ወወረሰ ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ዘኢየሐልቅ ፡ ወጸጋተ! ፡ ሰማያዊተ ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅ ዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ እኅወ ፡ ኖማርያኖስ ፥ ንጉሠ ፡ ሮም ፡ ወስሙ ፡ አብጥልማዎስ" ። ወ ኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አርአያሁ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ እስራኤል ። ወኮነ ፡ ጽኑዓ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ፀብእ ፡ ወይትሜካሕ ፡ ላዕለ፣ ዙሎሙ።፣ ውሉደ ፡ አንጾኪያ ። "ፍቁር ፡ በኀበ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሥኑ ፡ ወጽንዑ ። ወበእንተ ፡ ብገኃ ፡ ዘ ያፈቅሩ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ ሠዐሉ ፡ ሥዕሎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ዘአንጾኪያ" ፡ ወሠዐሉ ፡ ሥ ፅሎሙ ፡ ለጸሳእያን ፡ "ምውአን ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ወኾዩያን" ። ወኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አንበበ" ፣ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፣ እመጻሕፍተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ፣ ንጉሠ፣ሮ ሜ ፡ ወፈተወ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርአዮ ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አቡሁ ፣፡ ወኅሠሦ' ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወፈነ ዎ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኀበ" ፣9የ ፤ ወፅ”አ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ሮሜ ፣ምስለ፣፡ሰራዊቱ፣ ወተ ቀበሎ ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወዙሎሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ። ወበእማንቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ተን ሥአ ፡ ፀብእ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ቀ”ዝ ፡ ወእምሰብአ ፡ አርማን" ። ወወፅአ" ፣ ኅቤሆ ሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ገላውድዮስ ፡ ወሞኦሙ ፡ ወአኀዘ” ፡ ንጉሦሙ ፡ ወሰበሮሙ ። ወእምድኅ ረዝ ፡ ገብአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ወረከቦ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡እንዘ ፡ ክሕ
received the incorruptible crown of martyrdom; he despised the glory of this ephemeral world and inherited the eternal glorification that does not cease and the celestial grace. The father of this saint was the brother of Nomâryânos (Numerian), emperor of Rome, and was named Abtelmäwos (O: Abtelyânos, P: Abtelwos). This saint was very beautiful in appearance, like Joseph, son of Jacob-Israel; he was brave in war and distinguished himself above all the young men of Antioch, dear to everyone because of his beauty and his bravery. Because of the deep affection they had for him, they painted his image at the gate of the city of Antioch and they also painted the image of the enemies defeated before him and fleeing. The saint had read many books of ecclesiastical writings. The emperor of Rome heard his story and desired to see him; he sent to ask for him from his father, who sent him to him. When he arrived there, the emperor of Rome went out with his troops to meet him and was very happy with him, as were all the inhabitants of the city. In those days, the great war of the inhabitants of Quez and the inhabitants of Armân (Armenia) broke out; Saint Galäwdyos marched against them, beat them, took their king, and put them to rout; after which he returned to the city of Antioch and found that the Emperor Diyogeltyânos (Diocletian) had denied Our Lord Jesus Christ and was serving the idols.

He had a friend by the name of Figtôr, son of Hermânos, and at all times they conversed about the words of the holy scriptures and what was useful for the salvation of their soul. They agreed between themselves to shed their blood for the name of Our Lord Jesus Christ. Now Satan appeared to them in the form of an old man and pretended to be saddened for them, saying: "O my children, beautiful young men, sons of kings, I fear for you and am saddened for you, and I advise you to come to an understanding with this impious emperor in order to offer incense to his gods, when he has ordered you to do so. Do not transgress his order; for yourselves, you will worship Christ in your homes, for this emperor is energetic, powerful, and implacable." But Our Lord Jesus Christ made them understand that the one who was speaking with them was the devil. Then the saints said to him: "O being filled with all evil, and father of lies, get away from us, for you, you oppose the way of God, at every instant." Immediately his appearance changed and he became like a black slave. He said to them: "Behold, I will warn the king about you, and I will make him shed your blood." Satan went to Diyogeltyânos and said to him: "If you do not put Galäwdyos and Figtôr to death, they will revolt against you, they will kill you, and they will take your empire, for they are of royal family." That is why the emperor sent

to call Saint Galäwdyos, who came to him; and he asked him to offer incense to his gods and promised him to put him in the place and in the office of his father. But he did not yield to his promise and did not obey him at all. The emperor did not dare to converse with him in this city of Antioch; but Saint Galäwdyos spoke boldly to the emperor, without fear, and cursed him.

Then Hermânos advised the Emperor Diyogeltyânos to send Saint Galäwdyos to the land of Egypt and to have him killed there, "for he is, he said, a rebel, as is my son Figtôr." The emperor wrote a message for the governor of the city of Andênäw, saying: "Galâwdyos has not obeyed us and has not listened to our word and has not submitted; you therefore, win him over by all your efforts; if he yields to our prescription, it is well; otherwise, cut off his head with the sword." When Saint Galäwdyos learned that the emperor was having him sent to the land of Egypt, he called Sadrikos, his sister's husband, gave him his recommendations (O, P: regarding the latter, entrusted her to him), said goodbye to him, and left for the land of Egypt with the emperor's envoys. Upon his arrival at Aryânos, governor of the city of Andênäw, Aryânos, having seen him, rose toward him,


[p. 582] {#po.1.p582}
ኦ ፡ ቅዱስ' ፣ አንሰ ፡ ኢተፈነውኩ ፡ ኅቤክ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታስሕተኒ ፡ በቃልከ ፣ አላ፡ከመ፣ት ፈጽም ፡"ዘአዘዘከ ፡ ቦቱ! ፣ ንጉሥ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ይትናገሩ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተምዓ ፡ አርያኖስ ፡ ወሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ኩናት ፡ ወረገዘ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ ባቲ ፡ ወመጠ ወ ፡ነፍሶ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፣ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፣ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፣ ሰማያት ። ወመጽኡ ፣ ሰብእ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ገላውድዮስ ፡ ወገነዝዎ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ግ ንዘተ ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተ ፍጻሜተ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ስደት ። ወመጽአት ፡ እሙ ፡ ለፊቅጦር' ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንዴና ው ፣ ወገነዘት ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ ወጾረቶሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪየ ። አላ" ፣ ይብሉ ፡ ከ መ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ለቅዱስ ፡ ገላውድዮስ ፣"ይእዜ ፡ ይነብር" ፡ በሀገረ ፤ አስ”ዩጥ ። እግዚአ ብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
kissed his hand, and begged him with these words: "O my lord Galäwdyos, do not do this thing, do not transgress the order of the emperor!" Saint Galäwdyos replied by saying to him: "As for me, I have not been sent to you so that you may seduce me with your speeches, but so that you may accomplish what the emperor has commanded you." And they conversed together thus until Aryânos had become irritated; he had in his hand a javelin, and he pierced the saint with it, who rendered his spirit immediately and received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. There came faithful people who took the body of Saint Galäwdyos, wrapped it magnificently in funeral shrouds, and placed it with the body of Saint Figtôr. They remained thus until the end of the days of persecution; then the mother of Saint Figtôr came to the city of Andênäw, wrapped their bodies, and brought them to the city of Antioch; however, it is said that the body of Saint Galäwdyos rests now in the city of Asyut. May God have mercy on us
ሰላም ፡ ለገላውዴዎስ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ተዓሪዞ ፡፡ አልባሰ ፡ መንግሥት ፡ ዘኢሐወዞ ፡፡ ለመሎኩንን ፡ አላዊ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትገዐዞ ፡፡ እምይስማዕ ፡ ወአምይሠጠው ፡ ትእዛዞ ፡=፡ በኩናተ ፡ እዱ ፡ በሊሕ ፡ አብደረ ፡ ተረግዞ ፡
through his prayer and may his blessing be with us. Amen. [Hail to Galäwdëwos who, clothed in faith in Christ, did not love royal garments; while he was reprimanding the impious governor, he preferred to be pierced by the sharp sword of his hand, rather than lend him his ear and respond to his order.]


[p. 583] {#po.1.p583}
ሰላም ፡ ለክሙ ፡ አምአት ፡ ፳ወ8 ፡=፡ ዓዲ ፡ ፳ወ፪ለአርዑተ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ ፀማዱ ።፡ ማኅበረ ፡ ገላውዴዎስ ፡ ማር ፡ እምቤተ ፣ መንግሥት ፡ ሙላዱ ።፡ ለልበ ፡ ዚአየ ፡ በዘ ፡ ይትፈጸም ፡ መፍቅዱ
[Hail to you, O one hundred and twenty-one, and again sixty-seven companions under the yoke of martyrdom, company of Mar Galäwdêwos, of royal birth; please present my prayer on the altar of the Father, so that the desire of my heart may be accomplished.]
ውስተ ፡ ምሥዋዒሁ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ስአለትየ ፡ ሰዱ ፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ ተዝካረ ፡ ቅዳሴሃ ፡ ለቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያን ፣ ዘጣሰማዕታት' ፡ ዘ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘመድኃኒነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በሀገረ ፣ እስክንድርያ ። ወተዝክሩ ፡ ለኪሩብያኑ! ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶመሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ ለቅዳሴ ፡ ቤትከ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፣ መድኃኒነ ፡፡ በአለ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡=፡ ኀቤከ ፡ እግዚኦ ፣ አንቀዓዱ ፡ አነ ፡=፡ ከመ ፡ ተሀበኒ ፡ እምቅኔ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ግዕዛነ ፡፡
On this day also takes place the commemoration of the dedication of the church of the 40 (O, P: soldiers) martyrs, in the church of our Savior Jesus-Christ, in the city of Alexandria; [and the commemoration of Kirubyânu, patriarch]. May God have mercy on us through their prayer and may their blessing be with us. Amen. [Hail to the dedication of your church, Jesus our Savior, which is in Alexandria; toward you, O Lord, I lift my eyes; may you, from the slavery of sin, give me freedom, I wait for it from one moment to the next, always.]
እጸንሕ ፡ አንሰ ፡ እምዘመን ፡ ዘመነ ፡፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God.


[p. 584] {#po.1.p584}
አመ፲፪ ፡ ለሰኔ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ " ተዝካሩ ፡ ለመልአክ፤' ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ”ዘ ይተነብል ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘመደ ፡ እጓለ፣እአመሕያው ። ዝንቱ፣ዘአስተርአዮ ፡ ለኢያሱ፣ወ ልደ ፡ ነዌ ፡ወሶዕበ ፡ ፈርሃ ፣ እምኔሁ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ አነ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሰራዊተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወረድኦ ፡ ወአጥፍኦሙ ፡ ለሰብአ ፡ አማሌቃዊያን ፡ ወአርኃወ ፡ ኢያሪ ኮ ፡ ወአቀመ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ፀሐየ"።
THE 12TH OF SANE (June 6). On this day is the commemoration of the glorious angel Michael, chief of the angels, archangel, who intercedes for the human race. It is he who appeared to Joshua, son of Nun, and when Joshua was afraid of him, he said to him: "I am the captain of the host of God." He helped him, he destroyed the Amalekites, he took Jericho, and for him, for Joshua, he stopped the sun.
ወሀለወት ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ እንተ ፡ ስማ ፡ አውፊምያ"፣ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ኮነት'፣ ብእሲቱ ፡ ለብእሲ ፡ ዘይፈርሆ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ምጽዋታተ ፡ ብዙኃ ተ ። ወይገብር ፡ ቦበዓላተ ፡ ለለ ፡ ወር ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ በዓለ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሚካ ኤል ፡ አመ፲ወ፪ ፡ ለለ ፡ ወር ። ወበዓለ ፡ እግዚአትነ ፣፡ ቅድስት ፣ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ወኔለለ ፣ ወርጐ ፣ ወበዓለ" ፣ ልደቱ ፡ ለእግዚአነ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ ዘአመ ፳ወ፪ለለ ፡ ወርጐ ። ወሶበ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ አዘዛ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢታጽርዕ ፡ ምጽዋተ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ አሉ ፡ ይበዓላት ። ወሰአለቶ ፡ ለዎታ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስዓል ፡ ላቲ ፡ ሥዕለ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ውስ ተ ፡ ቤታ ፡ ወይመጥዋ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ዘንተ ። ወሶዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስ
There was a woman by the name of Awfimyâ; this saint was the wife of a man who feared God; he was accustomed to giving many alms and to celebrating three feasts each month, namely: a feast of the glorious angel Michael on the 12th of each month; a feast of Our Lady the holy Virgin Mary on the 21st of each month; and a feast of the nativity of Our Lord Jesus Christ on the 29th of each month. When the day of his death approached, he commanded this holy woman never to cease the alms he was accustomed to give, and especially those three feasts. She asked her husband to have the image of the glorious angel Michael painted for her in her house and to give it to her; and he did so for her. After the death of this man, this saint was

accustomed to doing what her husband did. Satan conceived jealousy against her and appeared to her in the guise of a religious widow; he came to her and began to converse with her, saying: "I feel sadness for you, and I have tenderness for you. Now, I advise you to marry; you will have children before your fortune disappears and you end up falling into misery." Then he added: "Your husband has inherited the kingdom of heaven and he does not desire alms." She answered him, saying: "I have made a promise to God not to attach myself to a second man: for if turtledoves and crows do not know a second mate, how could it be so among men who were created in the image of God?" As she had not listened to his advice, he changed his appearance and shouted at her, saying: "I will return to you another day." Then she took the image of the glorious angel Michael and, thanks to it, put him to flight. When the 12th of Sane arrived, she was thinking of celebrating the feast according to her custom, when Satan appeared to her again in the guise of an angel, and he said to her: "Greetings to you. I am Michael the archangel; God has sent me to you; he commands you to suspend these alms that you give and to marry a believing man." And he added: "Know that a woman without a husband is like a ship

without a captain." And he began to bring her testimonies from the books of the Old Testament, that Abraham, Isaac, Jacob, David, and others still, they had married and had pleased God. The saint answered him, saying: "If you are the angel of God, where is your sign of the cross? For the king's soldiers do not go anywhere without having the king's sign with them." Upon hearing her say this, Satan changed his appearance, then seized her and sought to stifle her; but she invoked the angel, Michael; he immediately arrived near her and delivered her from Satan, whom he seized and began to punish; and Satan begged him, saying: "Since God has patience with us until the end of the world, have pity on me and do not torture me now." The angel left him, then said to Saint Afomyâ: "Go and put your affairs in order, for you will leave this world today. Behold, God has prepared for you what the eye does not see nor the ear hear, which, in the soul of man, is not thought of." And he greeted her and ascended to heaven. After the completion of the celebration of the feast in a fitting manner, she sent a message to the bishop and the priests; they came to her and she gave them all her fortune so that they might give it to the poor, the unfortunate, and the needy. Then

[Greetings to Afomyâ who defeated the devil when he attacked her with lying speech. On this day, as it pleased her by placing the image of Michael on her forehead, she passed from the pain of this world to eternal rest.]

As for the reason why the feast of the glorious angel Michael is celebrated on this day, it is as follows. There was in the city of Alexandria a great temple that had been built by Queen Akla'obatrà (Cleopatra), daughter of Batlemos (Ptolemy), king of Egypt, in the name of the planet Zohal (Saturn), and its feast was celebrated in Alexandria on the 12th of the month of Sane. There was in this temple a great idol of bronze, extremely imposing, by the name of Zohal. It was customary to sacrifice many victims to it on the day of its feast. This practice of celebrating the feast of this idol continued until the days when Abbâ Alexander was ordained patriarch, that is to say, more than 300 years. When Abbâ Alexander was ordained patriarch of the city of Alexandria, when Constantine the just reigned, and when the orthodox Christian faith

had spread, Abbâ Alexander wanted to break this idol; but he was prevented by the populace of the city of Alexandria who said to him: "We are accustomed to celebrating the feast of this idol; behold, 18 patriarchs have preceded us and they have not changed our custom for us." Abbâ Alexander rebuked them greatly and declared to them by his words that it was an idol that did neither harm nor good; but that he who celebrated its feast worshipped demons. And Abbâ Alexander added: "If you listen to my advice, I will organize this feast for you in the way it has been until now; here is how: we will break this idol, we will consecrate the temple, making it a church under the name of the glorious angel, Michael the archangel, we will fix this feast in his honor, and the sacrifice will be for the Most High God; the oxen and sheep that are sacrificed, let the poor, the needy, and the destitute eat them so that the glorious angel, Michael, may intercede for you before Our Lord Jesus Christ." This wise advice pleased them; they obeyed him and arranged the temple as a church under the name of the glorious angel, Michael the archangel; this was the church known by the name of the church of Qaysâriyyâ (Caesarea); it remained until the reign of the Muslims in the land of Egypt, who ruined this church. inhabitants of all the provinces celebrated it in honor of the glorious angel, the archangel Michael, until today. May God have mercy on us all, through the intercession of the glorious angel Michael, and may his prayer and his blessing be with us. Amen. [On this day, God—may He be praised!—placed Michael, the archangel, in his rank, above all the angels, by the greatness and the many miracles of this glorious angel, Michael. Among other miracles is the following. There was a man who feared God and celebrated the memory of the glorious Michael on the 12th of each month, and held a greater feast in Hedär and in Sane. In his neighborhood lived a rich man, devoid of pity, hating this man who feared God when he celebrated the feast of the glorious angel, Michael, and he mocked him. When the days of this man who feared God were finished, and the moment of his death approached when he was to rest from the pain of this ephemeral world, he commanded his wife to be merciful and to celebrate the memory of the glorious angel, Michael, and more especially the commemoration that falls on the 12th of Hedär and the 12th of Sane. Now, at the time of this man's death, his wife was pregnant. After which this man died and they buried him. At this time, his wife was near giving birth, the pains seized her; she went through great torments

ላዕሌየ ፡ ወሰአሎ ፣ ለእግዚ”አብሔር ፡ በአንቲአየ ፡ ከመ ፣ ያድኅነኒ' ፡ አምንዳቤ ፡ ዘሀለ ውኩ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወዘበጽሐኒ" ፡ ዮም ፡ እስመ "ለከ ፡ ሞገስ ፡ ዐቢይ" ፡ በኀበ ፡ አግዚአብሔ ር ፡ወዕበ ፡ ትቤ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፣ አብርሃ ፡ ”ውስተ ፡ ቤት ፡ ዘሀለወት" ፣፡ውስቴቱ ፣ ወበጊ ዜሃ ፡ ድኅነት ፡ እምንዳቤሃ ፡ ወወለደት ፡ ሕፃነ ፡ ዘሠናይ ፡ አርአያሁ ፡፡ ወወረደ ፣፡ መልአ ክ ፣ ክቡር ፡ ሚካኤል ፤ እምሰማይ ፡ ወባረኮ ፡ ለሕፃን ፡ ወልዳ ፡፡ ወይቤ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይቤ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እስመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ይወርስ ፡ ንዋዮ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ”ብእሲ ፡ በዕል"፡ ዘአልቦቱ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ዙሎ ፣ ጥሪቶ ፡ ወገራውጊሁ ። ወበውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ውእቱ፣ በዕል ፡ ይኔጽር ፣ እመስኮተ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ቤታ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፣፡ ወሰምዓ ፣ ቃሎ «፣ ለመልአክ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዝንቱ" ፣ ሕፃን ፡ ይወርስ ፡ ንዋዮ ለዝንቱ ፡ ብአሲ' ፡ ባፅል ፡፡ ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ኀዘን ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ የኃ ሥሥ ፡ ምክንያተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትሎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ወኮነ ፡”እግዚአብሔር ፡ የዓቅቦ" ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ሕዓን ፡ በትንብልናሁ ፣ ለመልአክ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሚካኤል ፤ ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፣ መዋዕሊ ሁ ፡ ለሕየን"፣ ፲ዓመት ፡ ኃልቀ ፡ ንዋያ ፡ ለአሙ ፣ ወተመክነየ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ባዕል ፡ ዘውሑ ደ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ወልድኪ ፡ ይትለአከኒ ፡ ወአነ ፡ እሴ ስዮ ፡ ወአለብሶ ፡ ወእሁበኪ ፡ ፳ዲናረ ፡ ወርቅ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ዘ ንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ እምውእቱ ፡ ባዕል ፡ ተፈሥሐት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ንዴታ ፡ ወወሀባ ፡ ውእቱ ባ
and she said: "O angel of God, Michael! Have pity on me and intercede with God for the deliverance from the torments in which I find myself and which have come upon me today; for your favor is great before God." While she was saying these words, a light appeared in the house where she was, and at that very instant, she was delivered from her sufferings and gave birth to a child of beautiful appearance. Then the glorious angel, Michael, descended from heaven and blessed the newborn child of this woman, and said: "Thus has spoken God: this child will inherit the goods of this rich man who is without pity, all his riches and his lands." At this moment, this rich man was looking from a window of his house at the house of this woman, and he heard the voice of the glorious angel Michael when he said: "This child will inherit the goods of this rich man." When he had heard this, a great sadness came upon him, and he sought a pretext to kill this child. But God protected this child, thanks to the intercession of the glorious angel, Michael. The child having reached the age of ten, the resources of his mother began to fail. Then this rich man who had little pity imagined an expedient against this woman; he said to her: "Let your son serve me and I will feed him, clothe him, and I will give you 20 gold denarii." Upon hearing these words from this rich man, the woman rejoiced because of her poverty. The rich man gave her 20 gold denarii and she gave him

her child. When he took him, he felt a great joy and he said to himself: "Here is accomplished for me what I desired." Then he fashioned a wooden chest to the size of the child and had the child enter the chest, which he closed upon him. He carried it to the sea and threw the chest into it; and also the key to the chest, he threw into the sea; but, by the will of God, this chest floated on the surface of the water; finally, it reached the port of a city far from the sea. There was a shepherd of sheep who was guarding and grazing his sheep very close to the sea. Having seen this chest on the sea, he took it and carried it to his house; and he reflected on how he would open the chest; during this reflection, God cast into his soul the thought of going to the sea; immediately he went there and met a man who was catching fish. The shepherd of sheep said to this fisherman: "Cast the net into the sea for my sake, and whatever fish is caught, I will give you the price for it." The fisherman did as he had told him; a large fish having entered the net, the shepherd paid him for it and, taking it, went to his house; then he cut it up to cook it on the fire and eat it, he and the people of his house. But, while he was splitting the belly of this fish, he found a key in it, and he said to himself: "Perhaps this key is that of this chest"; and when he introduced the key into the hole of the chest, it opened suddenly. The shepherd was extremely

surprised, and in the chest found this child whom the rich man had wanted to kill. Having seen him, he felt a great joy and named him Bâhrân, because he had found him in the sea. The child lived near him as his son. As for the length of the road from the city where the child was born to the city where the shepherd of sheep lived, it was ten days' journey. After many days, the rich man without pity wanted to go on a journey. On his journey, he arrived at the shepherd of sheep's place at the moment of sunset. And he said to the shepherd: "Perhaps you have a place where we could stay until tomorrow; I will give you the price for it." The shepherd introduced the rich man into his dwelling. The shepherd called the young boy by his name, saying: "O Bâhrân," when the latter served him. The rich man, upon hearing this name, asked the shepherd questions and said to him: "Is he your son?" He said to him: "Undoubtedly; for I found this young boy, when he was a small child, in a chest, in the sea. I took him, and raised him for myself as a son." Upon hearing these words, this rich man without pity became deeply saddened in himself, and recognized that it was the child he had thrown into the sea to make him die. The next day, this rich man wanted to leave; he imagined a satanic pretext and said to the shepherd: "I ask you to give me this child, yours, so that I may send him with a message to

my home, to the city which is called such and such; here is his salary, twenty gold denarii that I give you; for I have forgotten something at my house." The shepherd was very happy because of the gold; he called this young boy and said to him: "My son Bâhrân, here, this illustrious man is sending you to his house regarding a domestic matter; then you will return in peace to my dwelling." And he said: "Yes, my father; what you command me, I will do." Immediately the rich man wrote a letter for his steward in these terms: "At the instant this letter is read, kill the one who brings it; his name is Bâhrân; then throw him into a pit so that no one knows, until my happy return." He inscribed on his letter the sign agreed upon between the two of them, sealed his letter, handed it to Bâhrân, and gave him provisions for his road. Bâhrân left. While he was walking on the road, and there remained one day more before he reached the city, behold, the glorious angel, Michael the archangel, arrived toward him, mounted on a horse, similar to a soldier of the emperor, and he said to him: "What do you have with you?" He said: "I have with me a letter from a rich man who sent me to his house, in such and such a city." The glorious angel, Michael, said to him: "Show me this letter." Bâhrân was afraid of him and gave him the letter. The angel blew on the paper and erased everything that was written there; then

the glorious angel, Michael, wrote with his pure hand on this paper in these terms: "I, the rich man such and such, send you the bearer of this letter; his name is Bâhrân; once this letter is read, give him my daughter such and such in marriage; for I give him all my riches, my house, and my lands; do not wait for me, for I will remain a long time on my journey. Let him do in my house what he wants, for I have given him power over all my fortune. And here is the sign agreed upon between me and you, steward of my house, my steward such and such." The glorious angel Michael sealed it, gave it to Bâhrân, and said to him: "Go to the house of the rich man and give this letter to his steward; but do not tell him that I met you on this road nor that I changed this letter, for fear that he might kill you." And Bâhrân said to him: "O my Lord, everything that you command me, I will do." And Bâhrân walked until he reached that city and he took information about the house of the rich man such and such; they led him there, and he gave his letter to the steward. Having read it, the latter understood the content and recognized that the sign of the rich man was true, authentic. Immediately, they celebrated the grandiose wedding of Bâhrân with the daughter of the rich man, with the rite of the nuptial crown in the church, in front of the sanctuary of God. They remained in the joy of the wedding for the space of 40 days, and at the end of this wedding, the rich man returned to his city. Upon hearing the noise of the festivities and the joy,

he questioned, saying: "What do I hear?" There were people who said to him: "Your letter arrived by the intermediary of a young man by the name of Bâhrân; you ordered that your daughter such and such be given to him in marriage and she was given to him. It has been forty days that they have been in festivities night and day for this wedding. And they have given him all your fortune, your house, your slaves, your servants, and all your goods, as you had commanded in your letter." Upon hearing these words, the rich man let out a great cry, collapsed, and died. Immediately the demons carried his soul to the lower Sheol, to hell. Then this young boy inherited all his fortune according to the command of God—may He be praised! This young Bâhrân was accustomed to celebrating the memory of the glorious angel, Michael the archangel, on the 12th of each month. The young Bâhrân recognized that it was the glorious angel, Michael the archangel, who had appeared to him, had blown on his letter, erased what was written there, and written for him what had been put in its place. Subsequently, he built a beautiful church under the name of the glorious angel, Michael the archangel, and painted his image there in front of the sanctuary; it was the cause of miracles, wonders, and healing for the unfortunate. Bâhrân, ordained priest of this church, occupied himself with the divine service until the day of his

ክቡር ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ "ሊቀ ፣ መላእክት' ፤ ወበዕለተ ፣ ትንሣኤ ፡ ይሰግድ ፣ መልአክ ፡ክ ቡር ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ቅድመ ፡፣ መንበሩ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ።፡ ወይስእሎ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሎ ፡ እ ስአለከ ፡ ወአኃሥሥ ፡፣ እምነ' ፡ ሣህልከ ። ኦአምላኪየ ፡ አነ ፡ ገብርከ ፡ ወላእክከ ፡ ከ መ።፡ትምሐር ፡ ሊተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡አለ፥" ኮኑ ፡ ይገብሩ" ተዝካርየ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ በእን ተ ፡ ኪዳነ" ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ መሐሪ ፡ ወመስተሣህል ፡ ወይሰጠዎ ፡ አግዚአብ «ሔር ፡ ”ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ኃይላት ፡ ሰማያዊያን ፡ ምእመ ናን ፡፡ ናሁ ፡ እኤዝዘከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትጹር ፡ ዲበ ፡ ክነፊከ ፡ ዘእሳት ፣ ዙሎ ፡ ዘይትከሀለከ ፣፡ ጸዊሮቶ ፡ የጊዜ ፤ ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ መልአክ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፣፡ወተኃሥየ፣ ወጾረ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ክነፊሁ ፡ ፻ጊዜ ፡ እምነ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዘይገብሩ ፡ ተዝካሮ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወአኅለፎሙ ፡ ባሕረ' ፡ አሳት ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ እልፍ ፡ አእላፍ ፡ ወትዕልፊተ ፡ አእላፋት ፣ ዝኢይኃልቆሙ ፡ መኑሂ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ባሕቲቱ ።፡ ወበእንተዝ ፣ ፍቁራን የ ፡ ይደልወነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንትጋደል ፡ ወንግበር ፣ ተዝካሮ ፡ ለመልአክ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሚካኤል ፤ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት ፡ በከመ ፡ ክሂሎትነ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዘይሁብ ፡ ጽዋዓ ፡ ማይ ፡ ቁሪረ፡ በስሙ" ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚእነ" ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፤ " በወንጌል ፣ ቅዱስ" ፡ ወንስአ ል ፡ ምሕረቶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በትንብልናሁ ፡ ለመልአክ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ከመ፣ ያድኅነነ ፣ አመሣግሪሁ ፡ "ለሰይጣን ፣ ርጉም" ፡ ወያርኅቅ ፣ እምኔነ ፣ ኩሎ ፣ ሕማመ ፡
death, he, his mother, and his children, and they inherited eternal life through the intercession of the glorious angel, Michael the archangel. And on the day of the resurrection, the glorious angel Michael will prostrate himself before the throne of God and will address his request to him thus: "I beseech you and I ask of your clemency, O my God, I, your servant and your minister, that you have mercy, for my sake, on all those who celebrated my memory on earth, because of your true promise; for you are merciful and clement." And God—may He be praised!—will answer him and say to him: "O Michael, chief of the faithful celestial hosts, behold, I command you to carry on your wing of fire all that you will be able to carry, three times." Upon hearing this, the angel Michael will rejoice and exult and will carry on his wing, three times, the portion of men who celebrated his memory on earth and will make them pass over the sea of fire; they will be ten thousand thousands and myriads of thousands; no one would count them except God alone. And consequently, our beloved, we must devote ourselves to asceticism and celebrate the memory of the glorious angel, Michael the archangel, according to our power, and even by giving a glass of cool water in his name, as says Our Lord—may He be praised!—in the holy gospel. We implore the mercy of God through the intercession of the glorious angel, Michael, so that he may save us from the nets of the

cursed Satan, and remove from us all suffering and plague, that he may multiply the fruits of our land, and help us to accomplish his will; that he may forgive us our sins; that he may give rest to the soul of those who die among us; that he may make our fathers and our brothers who are on a journey return to their dwellings, in health and in peace; that he may put affection among us; that he may strengthen us in the orthodox faith until the last breath, that he may fortify the king... in his reign, for many years and long years, that he may quickly cut his enemies to pieces before him, and that at the end he may place his dwelling near the holy martyrs and the just kings. Amen! The many wonders of this glorious angel, Michael the archangel, are incalculable. May his intercession be granted to us in the ages of ages. Amen!


[p. 597] {#po.1.p597}

Greetings to Michael, he is merciful and appointed to the happiness of men. At the sound of the breath of the trumpet of this angel, as was his ascension to the heavens of old, so will be the second coming of God.
ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ይከውን ፡ ዳግመ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡፣ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ዮስጦስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እምኖልቆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ። ዝንቱ፣ አብ፣
On this day also, died the holy father Justus, patriarch of the city of Alexandria; he is in the number of the patriarch fathers, the sixth. This father

ኮነ ፡ "ብእሲ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ማእምር ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፣ እስክንድርያ ። ዘአጥ መቆ፤' ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ ምስለ ፣ አቡሁ ፡ ወእሙ ፣ ወካልአን ፣ ብዙኃን ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጫሞ ፣ አናኾንስጢስ ፣ ወሜሞ ፡ አንያኖስ ፡ ዲያቆነ ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ሜሞ ፡ ቀሲሰ ፡ በክልእ ፡ ጊዜ ። ወልህቀ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስ ቲያን ፡ ወአእመረ ፡ ዙሎ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ። ወኮነ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ አንያኖስ ፣ ወይሜህሮሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ ወያጸንዖሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ። ወሶ በ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሪምዩ" ፣ ነሥእዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡፣ አብ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡ ፈቃዱ ። ወ ሜምዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፣ እስክንድርያ ፣ ወዓቀባ ፣ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን'፣ ዘእግዚአብሔር ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ ተዓቅቦ ፡ ወረዓየ ፡ መርዔቶ ፡በ ከመ ፡ ይደሉ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፣ ማርቆስ ፣ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ፲ወ፪ዓመተ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ፣ በርሥዕና ፣ ሠናይት ፣ ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለኦምላክነ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከ
was a learned and illustrious man among the inhabitants of the city of Alexandria. He was baptized with Christian baptism by Saint Mark the evangelist, as were his father and his mother and a great number of other people. Then he ordained him reader, and Anianus ordained him perfect deacon, then priest in another time, successively. He grew up in the precepts of the Church and knew all the Scriptures. He remained constantly near Saint Abbâ Anianus, taught the people, and strengthened them in the orthodox faith. At the death of the father Abbâ Primus, they took this father against his will and instituted him patriarch of the city of Alexandria. He kept the church of God in a beautiful manner throughout the land of Egypt and fed his flock as he should. After he had remained on the throne of Mark the evangelist for twelve years, he died peacefully in a beautiful old age and pleased our God. May the prayer and the blessing of this saint be with us! Amen.
ቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ ለዮስጦስ ፡ ማርቆስ ፣፡ ዘአጥመቆ ፡፡፡ ምስለ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወእሙ ፡ በተላጽቆ ፡፡ ረሲዖ ፡ በንጽሕ ፡ ወበቅድስና ፡ ልሒቆ ፡= በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ "አመ ፡ ድድቀ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ተዳደቆ ፡=፡ ለፈጣሪሁ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ ዘአርብሐ ፡ ወርቆ" ፡፡
[Greetings to Justus whom Mark baptized with his father and his mother all together. Having aged in purity, and gained in age in holiness, on this day when the common accident, death, happened to him, he returned to his creator his gold which he had made fruitful.]


[p. 599] {#po.1.p599}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ' ፡ አብ ፡ ቄርሎስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክን ድርያ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እምኖልቆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ ፳ወኒ ። ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ ዘእምቅድሜሁ ፡ ተሰና አው ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆኦሳት-፣0 ወሊቃውንተ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወዓርጉ፣ ኀበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ በገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ ፪አውራኃ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐትቱ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ዘይደ ልዎ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ሚመት ፡ ክብርት ። ወሀሎ ፡ በደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ከማ፣ ብ አሲ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ገብረ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይሬኢ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወአስተር አዮ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ንግሮሙ ፡ ለኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦሳት'፣0፣ ኢትሕዝ ኑ ፡ እስመ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ በማእከሌክሙ ፡ በደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ዘይደል ዎ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ሚመት ፡ ክብርት ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ጋአርጊ ።፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ነገሮሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ገብረ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ”ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ወአኃዝዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃዱ ፡ ወሜምዎ ፡ ሊ ቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወበርሃት ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወተፈ ሥሑ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ በሚመቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእ ቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ማአምረ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወሜመ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦሳተ ፡ ወቀሳውስተ ፡ ወዲያቆናተ ፡ ወሐደለሰ" ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናተ" ፡ ብዙኃተ ። ወበሚመቱ ፡ ጫመ ፡ ጸጳጳሰ ፡ ለብሔረ ፡
On this day, also died Abbâ Cyril, patriarch of the city of Alexandria; in the number of the patriarch Fathers, he is the 67th. At the death of the Father Abbâ Gabra Krestos, the patriarch his predecessor, the bishops and the elders of the people agreed to go up to the monastery of Saint Abbâ Macarius, in the desert of Asqetes (Scetis), and spent two months looking for who would be suitable for this honorable function. Now there was in the monastery of Saint Abbâ Yohannes Kamä, a just man, named Gabra Iyasus, who was a seer by the Holy Spirit. An angel of God appeared to him and said to him: "Speak to the bishops and say to them: 'Do not be afflicted, for, behold, in your midst, in the monastery of Saint Abbâ Macarius, is the one who is suitable for this honorable function; his name is Gâ'argi (George)." At the instant, Saint Gabra Iyasus spoke this language to them and they took this father, despite himself, and instituted him patriarch of the city of Alexandria. The church was enlightened by it. It was, regarding him, a general joy among the Christian people of the land of Egypt for his institution, for he was very learned; he ordained bishops, priests, and deacons, and renewed many churches. During his charge, he ordained a metropolitan for the land of Ethiopia; this one was named Sawiros and was the brother of the metropolitan


[p. 600] {#po.1.p600}
ኢትዮዲያ ፡ "ዘስሙ ፡ ሳዊሮስ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ እኅወ ፡ ጳጳስ ፡ በብሔረ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ' ፡ ወተሐ ፅነ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ በሀየ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳዊሮስ ፣ ኅበ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ኢትዮሏጴ ያ ፣ ተፈሥሑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወበእንተ ፡ አእምሮቱ ፡ ወጽድቁ ፡ ሜጦ”ሙ ፡ ለብ ዙኃን ፣ ሕዝብ" ፡ እምግባር ፡ እኩይ ፡ ወአብኦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ንስሓ ፡ ወፈሪሃ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ። ወሀለዎ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፣ ብዙኃት ፡ አንስት ፡ ወከማሁ ፣ ለመኳንንቲሁ ፡ ወበእንተ ፣ ትምህርቱ ፡ ሎሙ ፡፣ ኀደጉ ፡ ኩሎ”ሙ ፡ አንስቲያሆሙ ፡ ወተነስሑ ፡ 88እምኔሆጮ ፡ ወ ነበሩ' ፡ ምስለ ፡ አሐቲ ፣፡ ብእሲት ። ወሶበ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፣፡ ሎቱ ፣ በሚመቱ ፡ ፲ወ፳ዓመት፣ ወ፻አውራኅ ፡ ወመንፈቅ ፡ "ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ተመጠወ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፥ ቅድሳት" ። ወጾሩ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፡ ምስ ለ፣ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ ለሊቃነ ፡ጳጳሳት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፣ አሜን ።
of the land of Ethiopia; he had been raised there, at his place. At the arrival of Abbâ Sawiros in the land of Ethiopia, there was great joy, and, by his science and his holiness, he brought back a great number of inhabitants of the land from bad conduct and introduced them into penance and into the fear of God. The king possessed many wives and, like him, his officers; thanks to the instructions that the saint gave them, they all left their wives, each did his penance, and remained with only one wife. When he (Cyril) had reached, in his charge, 11 years and 3 months and a half, after having received the holy mysteries, he died peacefully. They carried his body to the monastery of Saint Abbâ Macarius and placed it with the bodies of the Patriarchs. May his prayer and his blessing be with us. Amen.
ሰላም ፡ እብል ፡ ለቄርሎስ ፡ ሊቅ ፤ ጵጵስናሁ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ በትንቢተ ፣ ብአሲ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡=፡ ለዝ ፡ መምሀር ፡ እምስብሐተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዕሩቅ'= በውጊዘ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ለምለሙ ፡ አዕፁቅ ፤
[Greetings, I say, to Cyril, patriarch; his patriarchate took place by the prophecy of a just man. By the river of the word of this master stripped of the praise of the world, branches sprouted, and in his teaching remained his children.]

[On this day also, it is the blessed, the pure, the seer of the

ላሊበላ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ወለድዎ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ሐፀንዎ ፡ በ ፈሪሃ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ልህቀ ፣ ወበጽሐ ፡ አምጣነ ፡ አካለ ፣ ብእሲ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እሁ ፡ ዘይልሀቅ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይወርስ ፣ መንግሥቶ ፡ ወይነብር ፡ዲበ፡፣ መንበሩ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ቅንአት ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ”“ወጸውዖ ።፡ ወዕበ' ፡ መጽአ ፡ ወቆመ ፡ቅ ድሜሁ ፡ አመክነየ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ነገረ ፤ ወአዘዘ ፡ ወቀሠፍዎ" ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ቅሥፈታተ ፡ እም ጊዜ ፡የቦሰዓተ ፡ መዓልት ፡ እስከ ፡ 8ሰዓተ"፣ መዐልት ።፡ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ያቅምዎ፣ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ወዕበ ፡ ቆመ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ አንከረ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወዙሎሙ ፡ ሠራዊቱ ፡ሶዕበ፡ር እዩ ፡ ከመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘበጽሐ ፡ እሙስና ።፡፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይሜውሮ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መልአከ ፡ አግዚአ ብሔር ፡፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ኦእተየ ፡ ዘገበርኩ ፡ ላዕሌከ ፤ ወእምዝ ፡ ገ ብሩ ፡ ዕርቀ ፡ ወሰላመ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ወርእየ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኩነኔሁ ፡ ዘይ እቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ ወአውረዕሶ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ይእተ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ ሐለየ ፡ በዘያ ሠምሮ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ምጽዋታተ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን ፡፡ ወዕበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ጽንዓ!" ፡ ፍቅሩ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ፣መልአከ ፡እ ግዚአብሔር ፡ በሕልም ፡ ወመሠጦ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ወአርአዮ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ይገብርን ፡ ለ፲አብያ ተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ ዘዘዚአሆን ፡ ኅብሮን ፣ ወገብረ ፣ በከመ" ፡ አርአዮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ሐኒዖቶን ፡ ለእማንቱ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፤ አውረሶ ፡ መንግሥ
mystery of the heavens, Lalibala, king of Ethiopia. This saint, from his birth, was raised by his parents in the fear of God. When he was grown and reached mature age, the king, his older brother, hearing that this one would inherit his kingdom and sit on his throne, was assailed by envy and sent to call him; this one having come and standing before him, he imagined an accusation against him and, on his order, they inflicted many blows of rods upon him from the 3rd hour of the day until the 9th hour of the day. After which, he made him stand in his presence; when he was before him, the king and all his generals were stupefied to see that no harm had come to him: he had been protected by an angel of God. Then he said to him: "Forgive me, my brother, for what I have done to you." Then they reconciled and made peace between them. God had regard for his torments of this day and he made him inherit and gave him this kingdom. During his reign, he reflected on how he would please God and gave many alms to the needy and the poor. When God saw the firmness of his love, the angel of God appeared to him in a dream and carried him before God; this one showed him how he would make 10 different churches. He did as God had indicated to him. Having finished the construction of these churches, he transmitted the kingdom to the son of his brother, then died peacefully.

[Greetings to Lalibala, skillful builder of sanctuaries in dry stone without damp dust, without mortar. To show that the reign and the administration, the government were to return to him, who, similar to honey, was the joy of the kings and the people, on the day of his birth, he was surrounded by bees.]

In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. THE 13TH OF SANE (June 7). On this day died the holy father Abbâ Yohannes, bishop of the city of Jerusalem. This saint led the monastic life from his childhood, at the monastery of Saint Abbâ Hilarion the Great, with the Father Epiphanius; he served God and practiced an extremely rigorous asceticism. They learned the history of his


[p. 603] {#po.1.p603}
ኤዲስ ፡ ቆፅሰ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ሀገረ ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ እምድኅረ' ፡ ተሠይመ ፡ ኤሏፋንዮስ ፡ ኤ ሏስ ፡ ቆኔስ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቆብሮስ ። ወሶበ ፡፣ ነበረ ፣ ዝንቱ ፣ አብ ፡ በሚመቱ ፡ አስ ሐቶ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ በአፍቅሮ ፡ ብሩር ፡ ወጥሪት ። ወአስተጋብአ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወገብ ረ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ጻሕላተ" ፡ ብሩር ፡ ለማዕዱ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይበልፅ ፤ ውስቴቶሙ” ። ወኢኮነ ፡ ይሁብ ፣ ለነዳይ' ፣ ወኢለምስኪን" ፡ ወኢአሐተ ፡ ፍታተ ፡ ኅብስት ። ወሰምዓ ፣ ዜና ሁ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኤሏፋንዮስ ፡ ወተዘከረ ፡ ተጋድሎቶ ፡ "ዘቀዳሚ ፡ ወመንኖቶ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወተጸምዶቶ" ፣፡ ወአምልኮቶ ፡ ወምሕረቶ ፡ ወገዓረ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ አማዕመቀ ፡ ልቡ። ወተዘከረ ፡ ፍቅሮ ፡ እስመ ፡ውእቱ ፡ እሁ ፡ በመንፈስ' ፡ ወዓርኩ ፡ አምቀዳሚ ። ወተ ንሥአ ፡ ኤሏፋንዮስ ፡ እምቆብሮስ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ። ወያስተርኢ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መጽአ ፡ በእ ንተ ፡ ሰጊድ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅ”ደስ ፣ በኢየሩሳሌም ፡ "ወበኅቡእ ፣ ከመ ፡ ያድኅ ኖ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ እምስሕተቱ"። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ "ኤጺፋንዮስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ጸውዖ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ወአንበረ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ማእደ ፡ "ወዲቤሃ ፡ እሙንቱ" ፡ ጻሕላት ፡ ዘብሩር ፡ ሠናያት ፡ ወውስቴቶሙ” ፡ ጸብ ሕ። ወእምዝ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ከመ ፣ አልቦ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ነዳያን” ፡ ወሐዘነ ፡ ኤኢፋንዮስ" ፡ በልቡ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ።
science and its virtues, he was chosen and consecrated bishop of the city of Jerusalem after the elevation of Epiphanius as bishop of Cyprus. And when this Father was installed in his office, Satan made him fall through the love of money and riches: he amassed great wealth and made silver dishes for his table. He was accustomed to eating from them, and he would not give even a piece of bread to any indigent or poor person. Saint Epiphanius learned his story and remembered his former asceticism and his contempt for this world, his observances, his religion, his mercy; he groaned for him from the depths of his soul and remembered his affection, for he was his brother in spirit and his friend of long standing. Epiphanius left Cyprus and came to the city of Jerusalem because of this Father, Abba Yohannes. Apparently, to the world, he had come to worship in the sanctuary of Jerusalem, but at heart, it was to deliver Abba Yohannes from his sin. Upon the arrival of Abba Epiphanius in the city of Jerusalem, this father, Abba Yohannes, invited him to come to his house, and he set the table before him with those magnificent silver dishes containing the food. Saint Abba Epiphanius saw that he had no pity for the poor, and he was saddened in his soul on his account.


[p. 604] {#po.1.p604}
ወእምድኅረ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ አምኅቤሁ' ፡ አመክነየ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ምክንያተ ፡ ሠናየ ። ወውእቱ ኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ" ፡ ኅደረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ 8ደብር ፡ እምአድባራተ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወፈነ ወ፡ኅበ፡፣ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ አእምር" ከመ ፡ ልሂቃነ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቆብሮስ ፡ በጽሑ"፡ኀቤየ ፡ ወአነ ፡ እፈቅድ ፡ እምኅቤክ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታክብረኒ'፣ በቅድሜሆሙ ፡ ወት ፈኑ ፡ ሊተ" ፣ ንዋያተ ፡ ማእድከ ፡ ዘብሩር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይብልዑ ፡ ቦሙ' ፡ ”“ወፈነዎሙ ፡ ኀቤ ሁ ። ወነሥኦሙ ፡ቅዱስ" ፡ ኤጴፋንዮስ ፡ ወሜጦሙ ፡ ”ወወሀበ፣ ጫጦሙ" ፡ ለነዳያን ፤ ወለምስኪናን። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሕዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ አባ" ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ለአባ”፣ኤ ጴፋንዮስ ፡ ሀበኒ ፡ ንዋያተ ፡ ማዕድየ” ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ እወ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ኅኀሠሦሙ ፡ እምኔ ሁ ፡ ፪” ፡ወሣልሳየ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ እ” ወ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኢወሀቦ ፡ ኪያሆሙ ፡ አኀዘ ፣ በዓጽፉ” ፡ በውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘትንሣኤሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ” ፣ "ወይቤሎ ፡ ኢየኃ ድገክ ፡ ትሖር ፡ እስከ ፡ ትሁበኒ ፡ ንዋይየ ። ወጸለየ ፣፡ ቅዱስ'' ፡ ኤሏፋንዮስ ፡ ወሰአለ ፡ "እምኅበ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወአዖሮ” ፣ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ። ”ወበከየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ” ፡ ወአስተብቀዓ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ ኤሏፋንዮስ ፡ ወሰአለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ” ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አሐተ ፡ ዓይኖ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ና ሁ ፡ እንከ ፡ አግዚእነ" ፡ ክርስቶስ” ፡ ኃደገ ፡ ለከ ፣ አሐተ ፡ ዓይነከ ፡ ፅውርተ ፡ ከመ፣
Having left him, he devised a clever stratagem against him. It is as follows: He took up residence in one of the convents of Jerusalem and sent word to Saint Abba Yohannes with these words: "Know that the elders of the city of Cyprus have come to me; I ask you, therefore, to do me honor before them and to send me the silver service from your table, so that they may take their meal upon it." Yohannes sent it to him, and Saint Epiphanius took it, sold it, and gave the price as alms to the indigent and the poor. A few days later, Abba Yohannes said to Abba Epiphanius: "Give me my table service." He said to him: "Yes." Then he asked him for it a second and a third time, and he replied: "Yes." And as Epiphanius did not return it to him, John grabbed him by his garment in the Church of the Resurrection of Our Lord and said to him: "I will not let you go until you have returned my service to me." Saint Abba Epiphanius prayed and besought Our Lord Jesus Christ, who deprived the eyes of Saint Abba Yohannes of light; Saint Abba Yohannes wept and asked for assistance from Saint Abba Epiphanius, who prayed to Our Lord Jesus Christ. He restored his sight to


[p. 605] {#po.1.p605}
ትዘከር ፡ ነፍሰከ ። ወገሠጾ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ተዘከር ፡ ገድለክከ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ "ዘቀዳሚ ፡ ወተጋድ ሎተከ' ፡ ወአእምር ፡ ከመ ፡ አነ ፣ ጫጥክዎሙ ፡፣ ለንዋያተ ፣ ማእድከ ፣ ወመጽወትክዎ ሙ ፣ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን ፡ በእንቲአከ ። ወአንሰ ፣ ኢመጻእኩ" ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌ ም ፡ዘእንበለ ፡ ”በእንቲአከ ፣ ወዕበ' ፡ ሰማዕኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ አንተ ፣ አፍቀርከ" ፡ ጥሪተ ። ወእምውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ነቅሃ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ እምንዋመ ፡ ሀኬት ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘን ቃሕ" ፡ እምንዋም ። ወሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ፈድፋደ ፣ እምዙሎሙ ፡ ሰብ እ ፡ ወመጽወተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ንዋዮ ፡ ወአልባሲሁ' ። ወመነነ ፡ "ዙሎ ፡ ጥሪተ" ፣ ወዙሎ፣ ንዋየ ፣ ዓለም" ፡ እስከ ፡ ኢተረክበ ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ፅረፍቱ ፡ ጸድርቲመ ፡ ብሩር”። ወወሀቦ ፡እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸጋ" ፡ገቢረ ፡ ተአምራት” ፡ ወኮነ፡ ይፌውሶሙ ፣ ለዙሎሙ፣ ዘቦሙ ፡ ደዌ ፣ ወሕማም ፡ ሶበ ፡፣ ይቀብኦሙ ፡ በዘይት” ፣ በትእምርተ ፣ መስቀል ፡ ማሕ የዊ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ዘንተ”! ፡ ገድሎ ፣ መንፈሳዊ”፣ ወአሥመሮ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአዕረፈ” ፣ በሰላም ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፣ አሜን ።
one of his eyes. Epiphanius said to him: "Behold, then, Our Lord Jesus Christ has left one eye blinded for you, so that you may remember your soul." He reproached him with these words: "Remember how well you sustained your struggles and your asceticism of old, and learn that I have sold your table service and given it as alms to the indigent and the poor in your name. As for me, I only came to Jerusalem because of you, and because I had heard it said that you loved riches." From that moment on, Saint Abba Yohannes awoke from the sleep of negligence, as one wakes from sleep. And he walked in the path of piety with more ardor than anyone else; he distributed as alms all the goods and clothing he possessed; he disdained acquiring riches and all these goods of this world to such a point that, at the moment of his death, not even a silver dirham was found on him. Thus, God gave him the grace to perform miracles; he healed all those who had sickness or pain by anointing them with oil and signing them with the sign of the redeeming cross. Having perfected his spiritual combat and having accomplished the good pleasure of God, he died peacefully. May God have mercy on us through his prayer. Amen.


[p. 606] {#po.1.p606}
ሰላም ፡ አብል ፡ ለዮሐንስ ፡ ፍጹም ፣ ኤዲስ ፡ ቆዕስ ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ።፡ ገዲፎ ፡ ዕበየ ፡ ወክብረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡፡ መጽወተ ፡ ለነዳያን ፣ እንበለ ፣ መሥፈርት ፣ ወአቅም ፡=፡
[Greeting, I say, to Yohannes, the perfect, bishop of Jerusalem; rejecting the greatness and glory of this world, he gave alms to the indigent without measure and without restraint, to the point that at his death not a dirham was found.]
እስከ ፣ በዕረፍቱ ፡ ኢተረክበ ፡ 8ድርሀም ፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ልማዶሙ ፣ ለሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣ በብሔረ ፡ ግብ ጽ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበሩ' ፡ በዓለ ፣፡ ለመልአክ ፡ ክቡር ፡ "ገብርኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት ። መ ልአክ ፣ ዘሐዳስ ፡ ዘሰበከ ፣ ባቲ ፡ ቀዲሙ ፣ ወሐዳስ"፣ ቀዲሙሰ ፣፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘሰበከ ፡ ለ ዳንኤል ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኮነ" ፡ ይዴሊ ፡ ወያስተበቀሦዕ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሚጠቶሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፣፤ እምፄዋዌ ፣ ወአድኅኖቶሙ" ፡ እምእዴሁ ፡ ለሰይጣ ን ። ወአስተርአዮ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወዜነዎ ፡ በአድኅኖቶሙ" ፣ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ እምፄዋዌ ፡ ባቢሎን ፡ ወሕንጸቱ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ። ወዜነ ዎ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ዘወሰነ' ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ዓመታ ተ ፡ ዘይመጽእ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በተፍጻሜቶሙ ። ወአጠየቆ ፡ መልአክ" ፡ ከመ ፣ ክርስቶ ስ፣ይትቀተል። ወእምድኅሬሁ ፡ ያመዘብርዋ ፡ "ለሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም" ። ወኢይመጽእ ፡
On this day also, it is the custom of the Christian people of the land of Egypt to celebrate the feast of the glorious angel Gabriel, archangel, who is the angel of the New Testament, which he announced in ancient times and in the time of the New Testament (?) anciently, for it is he who predicted to the prophet Daniel when he was praying and imploring the assistance of God for the return of the children of Israel from captivity and their deliverance from the hand of Satan. This great angel appeared to him and announced the deliverance of the children of Israel from the captivity of Babylon and the construction of the temple of Jerusalem; he also taught him of the coming of Our Lord Jesus Christ, after having fixed for him the years at the end of which Christ would come, and he made known to him that Christ would be put to death. After him, the destruction of the city of Jerusalem would arrive, and after him no other Messiah would come, except the false Messiah, the Antichrist.


[p. 607] {#po.1.p607}
እምድኅሬሁ ፡ ካልእ ፡ መሲሕ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሐሳዌ ፡ መሲሕ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ዓመታ ት ፡ ዘወሰነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወ”ጊዜ ፡ ዘይመጽእ ፣ መድኅን ፡ መጽአ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መልአክ' ፡ እም ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወዜነዋ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም" ፡ በእንተ፣ ምጽአቱ ፡ ለወልደ ፣ እግዚአብሔር" ፡ ኅቤሃ ። ወተሰግዎቱ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ወአስተርእዮቱ ፡ በሥጋ ፣ ወበእንተ ፡ አክብሮቱ ፣ እግዚአብሔር" ፡ ልዑል ፡ ለዝንቱ ፣ መልአክ ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ "በአሉ ፡ ፪መልእክታት ፡ ዓበይት ። አዘዙነ ፡ አበዊነ ፡ መምሀራነ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ከ መ፡፣ ንግበር ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በዓ”ለ ፡ ለለ ፡ ዓመት ። ወንስአሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይተንብል ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚእነ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያድኅነነ ፡ እምጸላእትነ" ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ቅሩብ' ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወይቀውም ፡ ቅድመ ፡ መንበሩ" ። ከመ ፡ በትንብልናሁ ፡ ንርከብ፣ ሞገሰ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ አምላክነ ፡ ወመድኃኒነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘሎቱ ፡ "ስብሐት፣ለዓ
At the completion of the years that he had fixed for him and at the time when the Savior was about to come, this angel came from God and informed Our Lady, the holy virgin Mary, of the coming to her of the Son of the living God, that he would take flesh from her, and would appear in the flesh. Because of the honor that the Most High God gave to this angel by sending him for these two important messages, we have received the order from our fathers, the doctors of the Church, to hold a feast for him each year and to pray to him to intercede for us before Our Lord Jesus Christ, so that he may deliver us from our enemies; for this saint is close to him and stands before his throne—so that, through his intercession, we may find grace before our God and Savior Jesus Christ, to whom belongs praise at all times. May the intercession of this glorious angel Gabriel be with us. Amen.
ለም" ፡ ትንብልናሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ገብርኤል ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ ለገብርኤል ፡ በእደዊሁ ፡ ዘተአስሩ ፤ መላእክተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ጌጋየ ፡ በአዋልደ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዘገብሩ ፡፡ ለዝመልእክ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዕበዩ ፡ ወክብሩ ።፡፡ በብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ መምሀራነ ፡ ሥርዓት ፡ አንበሩ ፤ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ይትገበር ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡
[Greeting to Gabriel, by whose hands were chained the angels of heaven who, with the daughters of men, committed iniquity. For his greatness and his glory, in the land of Egypt, the masters of the institutions have fixed that on this day the memory of this angel would be celebrated.]


[p. 608] {#po.1.p608}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ ተዝካሩ ፣ ለቃይናን ፡ ወልደ ፡ ሄኖክ ፣ እምድኅረ ፣ ሐይ ወ ፡ ፪፻ወ፲ዓመት ፡ ወአእረፈ ፡ በረቡዕ ፡ ዕለት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡=፡
[On this day also, memory of Cainan, son of Enoch; after a life of 910 years he died on a Wednesday. May God have mercy on us, through his prayer, in the ages of ages.] Amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፣ "ለአብ ፣ ላግ' ፡ ሰማዕት ፣ ወ፪፻”ሰማዕታት ፣ ወ ተዝክሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ማትያን" ፡ ዘተሰደ ፣ በእንተ ፡ ጽድቅ ። እግዚአብሔር ፣ ይምሐረነ ፡፤ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡= ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ ኮነት ፣ ቅድስት ፣ መግደላዊት ፡ ሰማዕተ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ እ መ፡፣ ፋኑኤል ፡ እመኳንንተ ፡ ደዋሮ ፣ እምዘመደ ፡ መንግሥት ፡ እስራኤላዊት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ እምዘርአ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሰይፈ ፡ አርአድ ፡=፡ ወኩነንዋ ፡ ተንባላት ፡ በዘመነ ፡ ግራኝ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ በዘዘ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡፡ ወፈጸመት ፡ ስምዓ ፡ በስቅለት ፡ ወተለወቶ ፡ ለ ክርስቶስ ፡ እግዚኡ ።፡ ወነሥአት ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ጸሎታ ፤ ወበረከታ ፡ ወሀብተ ፡ ረድኤታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለም ፣ ዓሜን" ፡፡
[In this day there is also memory of Ab Lag (O: Abulag) martyr and of 200 (O: 2) martyrs—then memory of Abuna Matyan (O: Matyas), exiled for justice. May God have mercy on us, through their prayer, in the ages of ages.] Amen. [On this day also is Saint Magdalawit, martyr of Christ, mother of Phanuel, of the family of the princes of Dawaro, of the royal race; she was a Solomonic Israelite, descendant of the glorious king Sayfa Ar'ad; the Muslims tortured her violently in the time of Gragn and by various harsh tortures. She ended her martyrdom by crucifixion and thus walked in the footsteps of Christ her Lord; she received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May her prayer, her blessing, and the gift of her assistance be granted to us in the ages of ages.] Amen.


[p. 609] {#po.1.p609}
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። አመ፲፻ለሰኔ ፡ ምንባብ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡፣ ኮኑ ፣ "ቅዱሳን ፣ ሰማዕታት' ፡ አባ ፣ አክራ ፣ ወዮሐንስ ፡ ወአ ብጥልማ ፣ ወፊልጳ"። ወኮነ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ አክሩ-፣ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፣ ደመን ሁር ፡ እምደወለ ፣ ቡቂር ፡ እምደበበ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ እምዕራበ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ ምስር ። ወሀለዎ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እኅው ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ፊልጳ" ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ባዕለ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወተሰናአው ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፪ ቀሳውስት ፣ ወስሙ" ፡ ለ8ዮሐንስ ፣ ወለካልኡ ፣ አብጥልማ ። ወበ"”ጽሑ ፡ አርባዕቲሆ ሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ሀገረ ፡ ቀርጥሳ" ፡ ወተአምኑ" ፡ በእግዚአነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ መኩንን ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንድፍዎሙ ፡ በአሕፃ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወኢቀ ረቦሙ ፣ አሕፃ ፣ ግሙራ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፣ ከመ ፣ ይደይዎሙ ፡፣ ውስተ ፡ እቶ ነ ፡ እሳት ፡ እንተ ፡ ትነድድ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወአንደዱ ፣ ዲቤሆሙ ፡ ወፈነ ወ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መልአኮ ፡ ወአድኅኖሙ ፡ እምአሳት ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ፣ ይእስርዎሙ ፡-ውስተ ፡ ዘነቦሙ ፡ ለአፍራስ ፡ ወይስሐብዎሙ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ቀርጥሳ'" ፣
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. THE 14TH OF SANE (June 18). On this day are the holy martyrs Abba Akra, Yohannes, Abtelma, and Filpa. This saint, Abba Akra, was one of the inhabitants of the city of Damanhur, in the district of Busir, in northern Egypt and to the west of the sea, of the river of Egypt. He had a brother named Filpa; he was very rich. They came to an agreement with two priests, one of whom was named Yohannes and the other Abtelma, and all four came to the city of Qartesa and confessed Our Lord Jesus Christ in the presence of the governor. The latter ordered arrows to be shot at them; this was done, but the arrows did not reach them at all. After that, he ordered them to be thrown into a burning and flaming furnace; this was done to them and fire was lit over them; but God sent his angel and delivered them from the fire. Then he ordered them to be tied to the tails of horses and dragged from the city of Qartesa


[p. 610] {#po.1.p610}
እስከ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ደመንሁር ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ወኢበጽሖሙ ፣ ወኢም ንትኒ ፡ እሙስና፣፣
to the city of Damanhur; all this was done to them, but not the slightest harm befell them.
ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርአሶሙ ፣ ወአውፅእዎሙ ፡ አፍአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ደመንሁር ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ አርአእስቲሆሙ ፡ በሰይፍ ፣ ወነሥኡ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፣ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰ ማያት ። ወመጽኡ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ፃ ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ አክራ ፡ ወሐነጹ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣ ሠናይተ ፡ ወአንበሩ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወኮ ነ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ተአምራት ፡ ብዙኃት ፡ ወከማሁ ፡ ለእሉ ፡ ይቅዱሳን ፡ "ዮሐንስ ፡ ወ አብጥልማ ፡ ወፊልጳ! ፡ ነሥእዎሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ገሀረ ፡ ደመንሁር ፡ ወገነዝዎሙ ፡ በአል ባሳት" ፡ ሠናያት ፣ ወሐነጹ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ሠናይተ ፡ ወአንበሩ ፣ ሥጋ ሆሙ ፡፤ ውስቴታ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፣ ተአምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ፡ ዓበይት" ። እግ
Then he commanded their heads to be cut off; they were led out of the city of Damanhur, their heads were cut off with the sword, and they received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. People came from the city of 88 who took the body of Saint Abba Akra and built him a beautiful church where they deposited his body; it was the cause of numerous miracles. The same for the three saints Yohannes, Abtelma, and Filpa; the inhabitants of Damanhur took them, wrapped them in magnificent garments, funeral shrouds, and built them a beautiful church where they deposited their bodies. They were the cause of miracles and signaled wonders. May God have mercy on us, through their prayer. Amen.
ዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ አሜን። ሰላም ፡ ለአብጥልማ ፡ ወፊልኦስ ፡ አብያጽ ፡፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወአክራ ፡ እምደቡበ ፡ ግብጽ ፡፡ አመ ፡ ቆሙ ፡ለስምዕ ፣ በአውደ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ስሑጽ ።፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ባልሖሙ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ይሰርር ፡ ሐፅ ፡=፡ ወአድኃኖሙ ፡ እምአሳት ፡ ብቀጽ ፡=፡
[Greeting to Abtelma and to Filpos, the companions; to Yohannes and Akra, of northern Egypt; when they stood, for martyrdom, before the tribunal of the fierce governor, God saved them from the flying arrows, and delivered them also from the prepared fire.]


[p. 611] {#po.1.p611}
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ። አመ ፡ ፲፳ለሰኔ ፡ ምንባብ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት' ፡በዓለ ፣ ቅዳሴ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘቅዱስ" ፣ ሚናስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ በሀገረ" ፣ መርዩጥ ፡ ወአስተርአዮ ፡ ተአምራቲሁ ፡ ባቲ" ፡ "ወዘከመ ፣ ተከሥተ" ፡ ሥ ጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ኅቡአ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ም ድር ፡፣ ወፈቀደ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክሥቶ ፡ ወበውእቱ ፡፣ መዋዕል ፡ ሀሎ ፡ 8፳ብ አሲ" ፣ ኖላዌ ፡ አባግዕ ፣ ዘየዓቅብ ፣ አባግዒሁ ፣ በህየ ፡ በቅሩብ ፡ እምነ ፡ ወግር ፣ ዘኮነ ፡ "ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ኅቡአ ፡ "ውስቴቱ' ። ወርእየ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኖላዊ ፡ ለ8በግዕ ፡ ዘይደዊ" ፣ ተጠምቀ ፡ በማይ ፡ እምነ ፣ ዓዘቅት ፡ ኀበ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፣ ወአንገርገ ረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መሬተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወግር ፡ ወተፈወሰ ፡ ሶቤሃ ። ወአንከረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኖላ ዊ" ፡ ወነሥአ” ፣ ለዙሎሙ ፡ አባግዕ ፡ አለ" ፡ ይደውዩ ፡ እምውስተ ፡ አባግዒሁ ፡ ወሐ ፀቦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወአንገርገሮሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መሬተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ወ ግር ፡ ወተፈወሱ ፡ ሶቤሃ ። ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡፣ ኖላዊ ፣ ይገብር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኩሎ
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. THE 15TH OF SANE (June 9). On this day falls the feast of the consecration of the church of Saint Minas the martyr, in the city of Maryut, of the miracles that occurred there, and of the discovery of the body of the saint. Here is how: The body of the saint having been hidden in the earth, God willed to show it. At that time, there was a man, a shepherd of sheep, who was guarding his sheep precisely near the hill where the body of the saint was hidden and buried. Now this shepherd saw one of his sheep that was sick and which, having plunged into the water of a cistern located in this place and rolled in the dust of this hill, was healed on the spot. Astonishment of this shepherd, who took all those of his sheep afflicted with sickness, washed them in this water, and rolled them in the dust of the hill: they were immediately healed. The shepherd acted in this way with everyone; every sick person came to him, and he would mix dust
ሙ ፡ ሰብእ ። ኩሉ" ፣ ዘይደዊ ፡ ይበጽሕ” ፡ ኅቤሁ ፣ ወይዴምር ፡ ውእተ” ፣መሬተ፣ ወግር ፣ ምስለ ፣ ማይ ፣ ወቀብኦ' ፡ ቦቱ ፣ ወይትፌወሱ" ፣ ሶቤሃ ። ”ወኢኮነ ፡ የአም ር፣ምክንያቶ፡፣ለውእቱ።
from the hill with water and anoint them with it, and they were healed immediately. He was ignorant of the cause of this fact.

The story of this shepherd reached the ears of the emperor of Rome, who had a daughter sick with an ulcer. He sent her to the shepherd, who healed her of her sickness as he had healed his sheep. Having been healed, she wanted to know the cause, what was in this place. Saint Minas appeared to her in a vision and said to her: "My body is hidden in this place. Behold, God commands you to dig and remove it." Upon waking, she did as the saint had commanded her and removed his glorious body, built a beautiful church on that spot, and his glorious body was deposited there. The emperor ordered his magistrates and his officials to build their houses in this place; and a great city arose, which was named Maryut. God caused many miracles to appear through the body of the saint. The patriarch came with his bishops and they consecrated the church on this very day. And the news spread of the miracles and wonders that were taking place there, in this church, through the intercession

ለቅዱስ ፣ ሚናስ ፡ ሰማዕት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ ለቅዳሴ ፡ ቤትከ ፡ እንተ ፣ ተሐንፀት ፣ በመርዩጥ ፡፡ በትእዛዘ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ስሉጥ ፡፡ ሚናስ ፡ አንቃህከ ፡ በድነ ፡ ሐራውያ ፡ አምጸጥ ፡፡ አስተዋጺኦሙ' ፡ ዕደው ፡ ዘበበ ፡ ፪ቂራጥ”፡=
of Saint Minas, martyr. May God have mercy on us through his prayer and may his blessing be with us in the ages. Amen. [Greeting for the dedication of your church which was built at Maryut, by the order of a powerful emperor; to Minas, you have awakened from tranquility, you have resurrected (?) the body of the pig, which men, by spending two girat each, had brought as an offering to you, bought by this price]
መበአ ፡ ለከ ፡ ዘነሥኡ ፡ በሜጥ ፡=፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 8አምላክ ። አመ ፡ ፲5ለሰኔ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፣ ጽሙድ ፡ ወመስተጋድል ፡ ዘዝክሩ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ወ ርሥእናሁ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡፣ ነፍር" ፣ ገዳማዊ ፡ በገዳመ ፡ ላዕላይ ፣ ግብጽ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ ዘዜነወነ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ በፍኖትዮስ ። እስመ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ በፍኖ ትዮስ ፡ ሆኮ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወፈተወ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርአዮሙ ፡ ለአግብርተ ፡እ ግዚአብሔር ፡ ገዳማዊያን ። ወርእየ ፡ "ብዙኃነ ፡ እምኔሆሙ! ፡ ወጸሐፈ ፡ ገድሎሙ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. THE 16TH OF SANE (June 10). On this day died the father, the devout and ascetic with the glorious memory and the beautiful old age, Saint Abba Nafr (Onuphre) the solitary in the desert of Upper Egypt. Here is what Saint Bafnotyos (Paphnutius) told us about him. Moved by the grace of God, Saint Bafnotyos wanted to see the servants of God, the hermits; he saw a great number of them and wrote their life; of their number is


[p. 614] {#po.1.p614}
ወእምኖፕልቆሙ' ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ነፍር ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዕበ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስ ተ ፣ ገዳም ፡ ረከበ ፣ ነቅዓ ፣ ማይ ፣ ወአሐተ ፡ በቀልተ ። ወነበረ ፣ ውስተ ፣ ይእቲ
this saint, Abba Nafr. The latter, upon entering the desert, found a spring and a palm tree and remained in this desert for sixty years.
ገዳም ፡ ፳ዓመተ ። ' ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ በፍኖትዮስ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ነፍር ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይመጽእ ፣ ኅቤሁ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ዕራቁ ፣ ወሥዕርተ ፡ ርእሱ ፣ ወጽሕሙ ፡ ከደኑ ፡ ሥጋሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርአዮ ፡ በፍኖትዮስ ፡ ፈርሀ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወመሰሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መን ፈስ" ። ወአጽንዖ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ነፍር ፡ ወአተበ ፡ ትእምርተ! ፡ መስቀል ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፣፣ › ወጸለየ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አቡነ ፡፣ ዘበሰማያት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ነፍር ፣ "ሠና ይ ፡ ምጽአትከ" ፡ ኦበፍኖትዮስ ፡ ወሶዕበ ፣ "ጸውዖ ፡ በስሙ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ቅድሜሁ" ፣ ወተአተተ፤' ፡ እምላፅሌሁ ፡ ፍርሀት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸለዩ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይትናገሩ ፣ በዕበያቲሁ' ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወሰአሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ በፍኖትዮስ ፡ለ
One day Saint Bafnotyos saw this saint, Abba Nafr, who was coming toward him, naked, his body covered by his hair and his beard. Upon seeing him, Bafnotyos was afraid of him, and it seemed to him that it was a spirit; then Saint Abba Nafr grabbed him and made the sign of the cross before him, and prayed by saying the "Pater noster." After which, Saint Abba Nafr said to him: "It is well for you to come to me, to Bafnotyos." After he had called him by his name, and after he had prayed before him, the fear left Bafnotyos. Having prayed together, they remained to converse about the greatness of God. Saint Bafnotyos prayed to Saint Abba Nafr to tell him how he had come into this desert and how he remained there.
ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ነፍር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንግሮ ፣ ዘከመ ፡ እፎ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ይእቲ ፣ገ ዳም ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ እፎ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ንብረቱ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ቅዱስ ፡ ነፍር ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ' ፡ ነበርኩ ፡ ውስተ ፡8"ደብር፣ ዘውስቴቱ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ጻድቃን ፡ ወራን ። ወሰማዕክዎሙ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይትናገሩ ፡ በክብ ሮሙ" ፡ ለገዳማዊያን ፡ እለ ፡ የኃድሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወይንእድዎሙ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ምግ ባረ ፡ ሠናይ ። ወአንሰ ፣ አቤሎሙ ፡ ቦኑ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ በህየ ፡ ዘይጌይስ ፡ እምኔክሙ ። ወይ
Saint Abba Nafr answered him in these terms: "I was living in a monastery where there were just and good monks; I heard them conversing about the greatness of the hermits who lived in the desert and praising them for all their beautiful conduct; and I said to them, 'Are there any here better than you?'" They said to me: "Yes, those who live

ቤሉኒ ፡ እወ ፡ እለ ፡ ”የኃድሩ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወአሙንቱ ፡ ክቡራን ፡ በኅበ ፡ እግዚአ ብሔር ። እስመ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ቅሩባን ፡ እምነ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወእምሰብእ ። ወለእመ ፡ ተከዝነ ፡ ወሐዘነ ፡ ረከብነ" ፡ ዘይናዝዘነ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ደወይነ ፡ ረከብነ" ፡ ዘይሔውጳነ ። ወለእመ ፡ ዓረቅነ ፡ ረከብነ" ፡ ዘያለብሰነ ። ወለእመ ፡ ፈተውነ ፡ ፍትወተ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ንረክባ ። ወለእ ለሰ" ፡ የኃድሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ የኃጥኡ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ሎ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰማ ዕኩ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ውዕየ ፡ ልብየ ፡ ከመ ፡ አሳት ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሌሊ ት" ፡ ነሣእኩ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ወወፃአኩ ፣፡ እምደብር ፡ ወጸለይኩ ፡ ወሰአልክዎ ፡ ለአግዚሕእ"ነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምርሐኒ ፣ ውስተ" ፣ መካን ፦ዘእነብር ፡ ውስቴቱ ። ወ እምዝ ፡ ሖርኩ ፡ ወአስተዳለወ ፡ ሊተ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ጻድቀ ፣ ወረከብኩ ፡ ወነበርኩ ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡ እስከ ፣ አመ ፣ ይምህረኒ ፡ ፍኖተ ፣ ገዳማዊያን ። ወአምዝ ፡ በጻ ሕኩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ወረከብክዋ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ በቀልት ፡ ወትፈሪ ፡ ፲ወ፪አስካለ ፡ ለለዓመት ። ወየአክለኒ ፡ ለለ"ጸ፳አስካል ፣ ለለ8ወርኅ ፣ ወእሴሰይ ፡ ቦቱ ። ወእሰቲ ፡ ማየ ፣ እምዛቲ ፡ ነቅፅ ። ወናሁ ፡ ነበርኩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ገዳም ፡ እስከ ፡ ዮም ፡ ፳ዓመተ ፡ ኢርኢኩ ፡ገ
in the desert are illustrious before God; for we, we are near the world and men; if we feel boredom and sadness, we find someone to console us; if we are sick, we find someone to visit us; if we are naked, we find someone to clothe us, and whatever our desires, we find means to satisfy them. But those who live in the desert are deprived of all that." When I had heard them speak this language, my soul burned like fire, and, night having come, I took a little bread and left the monastery. I prayed to Our Lord Jesus Christ to lead me to a place where I could live. Then I walked, and, God having disposed for me a just man, I found him and stopped near him until he had taught me the way of the hermits. I then arrived in this place and found this palm tree: it produces 12 clusters each year; one cluster, each month, suffices for my food and I drink water from this spring; behold, I have lived in this desert, until today, 60 years, and I have seen absolutely no human face, except for you."
ጸ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ግሙራ ፡ ዘእንበሌከ ። ወእንዘ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ይትናገሩ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወ መጠዎሙ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ቅድሳት' ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወደሙ ፡ ለእግዚአነ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶ ስ ፡ ወበልዑ ፡ ሲሳየ ፡ ንስቲተ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተወለጠ ፡ አርአያሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ነፍር ፡
During their conversation, the angel of God descended near them and delivered to them the holy mysteries, the body and blood of Our Lord Jesus Christ, then they ate some food. Then, the appearance of Saint Abba

Nafr was transformed and he became like fire, then he fell to his knees and prostrated himself in the presence of God; Saint Bafnotyos kissed him, and he rendered his soul into the hand of God. Saint Bafnotyos wrapped him in the garment of wool hair with which he was clothed, using it as a funeral shroud, and buried him in this cave. And Bafnotyos thought within himself to remain in this place, in his stead; but when he had buried him, the palm tree fell and the spring dried up. This took place by the will of God, so that Saint Bafnotyos would go into the world and publish there the story of the holy hermits he had seen and especially of Abba Nafr. Then Saint Bafnotyos came into the world and made known the life of this saint, Abba Nafr, and the day he died. May God have mercy on us through his prayer and his intercession, and may the blessing of this saint, Abba Nafr, be with us. Amen! amen!
አሜን ። ወአሜን ። ሰላም ፡ ለአቡናፍር ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ተዳደቆ ፡ መዊት =፡ እንተ ፡ ተወለጠ ፡ ገጹ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ እሳት ፡፡ ድኅረ ፡ ቀበሮ ፣ በብኑዳ ፡ በውሣጤ ፡ በዐት ፡=፡ ኢትትወረስ ፡ ለካልአን ፡ ዘተውሀበቶ ፡ ሲሲት ፡፡ ነቅዓ ፣፡ ማይ" ፡ የብሰ ፡ ወወድቀ ፡ በቀልት ፡፡
[Greeting to Abunafr! at the time when death surprised him, when his face was transformed like fire, after Babnuda had buried him in the interior of the cavern, no one else received as an inheritance the food that had been given to him: the spring dried up and the palm tree fell].


[p. 617] {#po.1.p617}

[Greeting to you, just ones more numerous in virtue than the sand, group of holy brothers who have lived in the land of Fesa. When the training of your justice like a sharp iron pricked his heart, Abunafr withdrew from the surface of the world, so that his soul might become a companion of the soul of the humble]

[On this day also, the angel of God appeared to Joseph in a dream, so that he might take the Child and his Mother and return to the land of Israel. May his blessing be with us in the ages of ages. Amen!]
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፳አምላክ ። አመ ፡ ፲፪ ፡ "ለሰኔ ፡ ምንባብ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ለትጾን"። ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምሰ
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. THE 17TH OF SANE (June 11). On this day died the holy father Abba Latson. This father was from the city
