The wisdom of Solomon greatly surpassed the wisdom of the ancient sons of Israel and of all the sages of Egypt. He dominated all the kingdoms and gifts were brought to him every day of his life. Here is what Solomon's dinner was for each day: 30 cors of fine flour, 60 cors of crushed flour, 10 fat oxen, 20 bulls and 100 sheep, not counting goats, deer and fattened chickens. Solomon possessed 40,000 draft horses for the chariots and 10,000 battle horses. Solomon offered 1,000 sacrifices at Gibeon. God appeared to him in a dream and said to him: "Make a request of me for yourself." And Solomon said to him: "You, O Lord, have treated David your servant, my father, with great mercy, because he walked with justice in your presence, and you have made his son sit on his throne. And now that I, a young child not knowing how to go out or come in, having no experience, you have made me live in the midst of your people, immense and innumerable, I do not have an intelligent heart with which I may understand and render a just judgment to your people."


[p. 659] {#po.1.p659}
ወይቤሎ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፣ እስመ ፡ ሰአልከ ፡ ለከ ፡ እምኅቤየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታአምር ፡ ፍተሐ ፡ ወኢሰአልከ ፡ ብዝኃ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ወብገኃ ፡ ብዕል ፡ ናሁ ፡ ወሀብኩከ ፡ ልበ ፣ ወጥበበ ፣ ከመ ፡፣ አልቦ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ እምቅድሜከ ፣ ዘከማከ ፡ ወእምድኅሬክኒ ፡ ኢይትነ”ሣ እ ፡= ወዘሂ ፡ ኢሰአልከኒ ፡ እሁበከ ፡ ብዕለ ፡ ወክብረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ እምሰብእ' ፡ ከማ ከ ፡ በመንግሥቱ ፣ ወእምድኅረዝ" ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወቆመ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ታቦተ፣ ሕጉ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአብአ ፣ መሥዋዕተ ፡ ሰላም ፡፡ ወይእተ ፡ አሚረ ፣ ቆማ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ፪አንስት ፡ ዘማት'፣ ወተቤሎ ፡ አሐቲ"፣፡ስ ምዓኒ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ አነ ፡ ወዛቲ ፡ ብአሲት ፡ ነኃድር ፡ ውስተ ፡ 8ቤት ፡ ወወለድነ ፡ ወል ደ ፤ ወአመ ፡ ሣልስት ፡ ዕለት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ንነውም ፡ ሰከበት ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ወልዳ ፡ ወቀተለቶ ፡ ወነሥአት ፡ ወልድየ" ፡ እምሕፅንየ ፡ ወትቤለኒ ፡ ወልድየ" ፡ ውእቱ ፡፡ ” ወክልእታኒ ፡ት ቤ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ሕያው ፣ ውአቱ' ፡=፡ ወይቤ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ አምጽኡ ፡ መጥባሕተ፣ ወምትርዎ ፡ ለክልኤ ፡ ወሀቡ ፡ መንፈቆ ፡ ለዛቲ' ፡ ወመንፈቆ ፡ ለእንታክቲ ፡ ወአውሥ አት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ብአሲት ፡ ዘሕያው ፡ ወልዳ ፡ ወትቤ ፡ ሀብዋ ፡ ሕያዎ ፣ ”ወኢትቅትል ዎ ። ወይቤ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ሀብዋ ፡ ለዘትቤ ፡ ኢትቅትልዎ ፡ ሕያዎ" ።፡ ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ እ ስራኤል ፡ ፈርሁ! ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ገጹ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ጥበበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡=፡ ወነበበ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ጣ፻ ፣ አምሳለ ፡ ወ፳፻ማኅሌተ ፡፡ ወበራብፅዕ" ፡ ሣረ
And God said to him: "Since you have asked me for yourself to know judgments and you have asked neither for many days nor for great riches, behold, I give you intelligence and wisdom such that no one before you was like you, nor after you will anyone like you arise. And what you have not asked of me I give you also, riches and glory, so that no man will have been like you during his reign." Then Solomon went to Jerusalem and stood before the ark of the law of God and presented peace offerings. And that same day, two prostitute women stood in his presence; one said to him: "Listen to me, O Lord. This woman and I live in the same house, and we each had a child. But on the third day, while we were sleeping, she lay on her child and killed him. And she took my child from my bosom and she said to me: "It is my child." But the other also said: "This one is my child, who is alive." Then the king said: "Bring a sword and cut him in two and give one half to this one and one half to that one." And, speaking up, the woman whose child was alive said: "Give it to her alive, and do not kill him!" And Solomon said: "Give it alive to the one who says: "Do not kill the child!" At this news, Israel was filled with fear in the presence

of the king, having seen the wisdom of God that was upon him. Solomon pronounced 4,000 proverbs and 500 canticles. The fourth year of his reign he laid the foundations of the temple of God, in the month of Nisan, the second month; in the 11th year of the reign of Solomon, it was finished. This temple was 60 cubits long, 20 cubits wide, 25 high. Then, after having finished the construction of the temple of God, Solomon assembled the elders of Israel to transport the ark of God from the city of David to Zion, in the month of 'Atamen. The priests carried the ark and the tent of the testimony. The king as well as all Israel were before the ark and were sacrificing innumerable sheep and oxen; the priests introduced the ark to its place, in the debir of the holy of holies, under the wings of the cherubim, for cherubim shading it extended their wings over the place of the ark. Solomon, standing before the altar of God and before the whole assembly of Israel, raised his hands to heaven and prayed and addressed to God, Lord of Israel, a long prayer. And when he had finished his prayer, God made the sun appear from the height of heaven. The king blessed the whole assembly of Israel and King Solomon immolated, as peace offerings to God, 22,000 oxen and 1,000 sheep. The king also made the basin (?) of the temple of God. This appeared again to Solomon as it had appeared to him

at Gibeon and said to him: "I have heard your prayer and the request that you addressed to me. I have acted toward you according to your prayer." Solomon reigned 40 years and, before reigning, he was 12 years old; all the days of his life were 52 years and he lay down in peace. May his blessing be with us, in the ages of ages. Amen].

[Hail to Solomon; the food of science was his nourishment, and the riches of wisdom, his riches. As this king says, in his Canticle, in the month (at the time) of Christ, go out to see where the vines bloom, where the pomegranates give their fruit].

On this day died the holy and pure father Abbâ Nob, the confessor. This saint was a monk, devout and ascetic in one of the monasteries of Upper Egypt. When Diocletian tortured many martyrs and shed their blood, in one day, he had 80 martyrs brought and shed their blood. When they wanted to transport their bodies, they remembered Abbâ Nob and he was brought to Aryânos, governor of Andênaw. The governor said to him: "Offer incense to Apollo; and remove

from yourself this monastic habit." And Saint Abbâ Nob answered him, saying: "As for me, I will not do it at all, and I will not abandon my Lord Jesus Christ, and I will not serve the idols of stone." Then he inflicted harsh tortures of different kinds upon him. And he endured the torments by the strength of Our Lord Jesus Christ. Then he exiled him to the Pentapolis and threw him into a pit. He remained there 7 years until God had made Diocletian the impious one disappear and instituted Constantine the just emperor. The latter sent copies of his letters to all the provinces so that all the men imprisoned for the name of Our Lord Jesus Christ, who were in the prisons, might be released; and he ordered that they be brought to him to be blessed by them, and he said to his officers: "If it is not possible for you to send them all to me, send me the most illustrious, their doctors and their elders, so that they may bless me and lay their hands on my head, and especially these four well-known ones, who are Zakäryâs (Zacharias) of the city of Ahnâs, Maksimos (Maximus) of the city of Feyüm (Fayyoum), Agâbyos (Agapius) of the city of Dehni and Abbâ Nob of the city of Bâlâ'os."

ለቅዱሳን' ፡ እምአብያተ ፡ መዋቅሕት ። ወኮኑ ፣ ይትፌሥሑ ፡ ወይሴብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአ ብሔር ፡ ወይዜምሩ ፡ ሎቱ «። ወኮነ ፡ ላእኩ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ የኃሥሦ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ኖብ ፡ ወቅዱ ስሰ ፡ አባ ፣ ኖብ ፣ ዕበ ፡ ሰደድዎ ፡ እም፳አህጉር ፡ ወሖረ" ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ በሰላ ፡ አንጻ ረ ፡ ሀጉሩ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በሀየ ፡ አንዘ ፡ ይለብስ ፡ ልብሰ ፣ ኃጺን ። ወረከቦ ፡ ላእኩ ፡ ለንጉ ሥ ፣ ወነሥኦ" ፡ ወተጽዕኑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሐመር ፡፥ እስከ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንዴናው ፡ወ ተጋብኡ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወሀለው ፣ "እምውስቴቶሙ ፡ ፪8ኤሏስ ፡ ቆኦፅሳት ። ወነሥአዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ ኖብ ፡ ወሜምዎ' ፡ ቀሲሰ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃዱ ፡ “”ወረሰዩ ፡ ቀደሰ"፣፡ቀ- ርባነ ፣ ወመጠዎሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፣፡ አምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡፣ ቅዳሴ ፡ጸ ርኃ ፣ ወይቤ ፡ ዝንቱ" ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ቅዱሰ ፡ ይንሣ”እ' ፡ አምቅድሳተ ፡ አግዚአብሔር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኩልክሙ ። ወአምዝ ፡ ርአዮ ፡ ለእግዚአነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶ ስ ፡ አንዘ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምሥዋዕ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ የኃድግ ፡ ኃጣውኢሆሙ”"፣ ለሕዝብ፣
saints detained in the prisons; they rejoiced, they glorified God and sang canticles to him. Now this officer of the emperor was searching for Abbâ Nob; and this saint Abbâ Nob, when he had been driven from the Pentapolis, had gone to the city of Basalâ opposite his city and had remained there, dressed in a habit lined with iron. The emperor's officer having found him, took him with him; they boarded a ship until they reached the city of Andênaw. The Christians gathered; there were four bishops among them, who took and ordained Saint Abbâ Nob priest, against his will; they had him consecrate the Eucharist and he dispensed the sacred mysteries to the peoples. Having finished the sacrifice, he cried out, saying: "This thing is a holy thing to the saints! Whoever is holy, let him receive of the holy thing! May God be with you all!" Then he saw Our Lord Jesus Christ, sitting on the altar, who was forgiving the sins of the persons who were doing penance.
አለ"፣0ይኔስሑ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ አስተዳለው" ፡ ቅዱሳን ፣ ለሐዊር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወኮነ፣ ኖልቆሙ፣ ድወ፪አስተዳለው ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ፳ወፄሰረገላተ ፣ ለለ፪እምኔሆሙ ፡ ተጽዕኑ ፡ ዲበ ፣ አሐቲ፣ ሰረገላ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኀለፉ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ በህየ ፡ አድባራት ፡ ዘደናግል ፡ ወፅ አ"' ፡ ወተቀበላሆመ-”፣ "ውኖልቆን ፡ ለደናግል ፡ ፪፻ ። ወክና ፡ ይዜምራ ፡ ቅድሜሆሙ ፡
After the saints had prepared themselves to go to the emperor, their number being 72, 36 chariots were prepared for them, two of them mounting each chariot. On their way during the journey, there were convents of virgins who came out to meet them. The number of the virgins was


[p. 664] {#po.1.p664}
ለቅዱሳን ፡ እስከ ፡ ተኃብኡ ፣ እምኔሆን ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ኀበ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ቄ ስጠንጢኖስ ፣ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ይሕፅብዎሙ ፡ በማይ ፡ ወያልብስዎሙ ፡ አልባሳተ ፡ ሐዲ ሳተ ፡እምቅድመ ፡ ይባኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ አባ0፣ ኖብ ፡ ኢተሐፅበ ፡ በማይ ፡ ወኢወለጠ ፡ ልብሶ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ቦኡ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉ ሥ ፡ ወተባረከ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወሰዐመ ፣ ቆቀ“ስላቲሆሙ ፡ ወአክበሮሙ ፡ ወአዘዘ'፣። ሎ ሙ ፣ ንዋየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወኢፈቀዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሥኡ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ዘእን በለ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ቅድሳት ፣ ወአልባሳት ፡፣ ለአብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፣ ባሕቲቱ"። ወአምድ ኅረዝ ፤ ተባረከ ፣ እምኔሆሙ ፣፤ ንጉሥ ፣ ወተአምጥፕሙጮ ፡ ወአስተፋነዎሙ ፣ ወተመይ ጡ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብሔሮሙ ፡ በሰላም ። ወሖረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ኖብ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ደብሩ ፡ ወፈጸመ ገድሎ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ "ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘአፍቀሮ ። እግዚአ
of 600; they sang before the saints until they had disappeared from their presence. Upon the arrival of the saints near the Emperor Constantine, he had them washed with water and clothed in new garments before they entered to him. It was done for them according to his order. But Saint Abba Nob neither washed himself with water nor changed his clothes. The saints having entered to the Emperor, he received their blessings, kissed their wounds, heaped honors upon them, and ordered that great goods be given to them; but they would not accept the slightest thing from him, except for sacred vessels and vestments for the churches, and nothing else. After the Emperor had received their blessings, he greeted them, and they were dismissed and returned in peace to their country. Saint Abba Nob went to his monastery and finished his struggle well; he died in peace and went to the Christ whom he had loved. May God have mercy on us through his prayer and may his blessing be with us forever and ever. Amen.
ብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ ለኖብ ፣ ተአማኒ ፡ ዘተብሀለ ፤ መትልወፀ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ፳'ዘዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ አቅተለ ።፡ ቁስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ቃለ ፡፡ አስተብፅፅዎ ፡ አብያጺሁ ፡ አለ ፡ ሀለው ፡ ህውለ ፡= ሶበ ፡ ፈቀዱ" ፡ ይሕፅብዎ ፡ ዮስለ ፡
[Hail to Nob, says the confessor, who followed the 10,000 martyrs whom Diocletian had killed; his companions who were together declared him blessed, when they wished to wash his wound, as Constantine the Just ordered].


[p. 665] {#po.1.p665}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ'፣ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ "ለመርቆራ ፡ ወቶማስ ፡ ወፊልዕስ ፣ ሰማዕታት ፡፡ በረከቶሙ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡፡
[On this day also is the commemoration of Margorä, of Tomas, and of Filpos, martyrs. May their blessing be with us forever and ever.]
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 8አምላክ ። አመ ፡ ፳ሀለሰኔ ፡ ምንባብ ። በዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ጸሊም ፡ ሰማዕት ። "ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ዘያ ነክሩ" ፡ ሰብእ ፡ አምገድሉ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ገፍዓ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ በከመ፣ይ ቤ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ አስመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ጽኑዓ ፡ በሥጋሁ ፡ ወኀያል ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ምግባሩ ፡ ይበልዕ ፣ ወይሰቲ ፡ ወይቀትል'፣ ወይዜሙ ። ”"ወኢይክ ል፣፡መኑሂ ፡ ከመ፣ይትቃወሞ ፡ ወተብሀለ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኮነ፣ይበልዕ፣ 8በግዓ ፣ ፍጹመ ፡ ወይሰቲ ፡፣ ዝቀ ፡ ወይን ፡ "ወኮነ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ እለ ፣ ያመልኩ፣ ፀሐየ ። ወኮነ ፡ "በኩሉ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ይኔጽር" ፡ ኅበ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ወይቤ ፣ ኦፀሓይ!" ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ "አንተ ፡ አምላከ' ፡ አይድዓኒ ። ወኮነ ፡ ይቤ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ኦአምላክ ፡ ዘ ኢያአምረከ ፡ አጠይቀኒ ፡ ርአሰከ ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. THE 24TH OF SANE (June 18). On this day is Saint Abba Muse (Moses) the Black, martyr. This saint caused wonder to men by his life, for he did violence to the kingdom of heaven, as the holy Gospel says. Here is how. This saint was vigorous of body and strong in all his actions; he ate, drank, killed, was debauched, and no one could resist him. It was said of him that he ate an entire sheep and drank a skin of wine; he was in the service of people who worshipped the sun; and, looking at the sun at all times, he would say: "O sun, if you are God, you, let me know!" And he would say to himself: "O God whom I do not know, make yourself known to me!"


[p. 666] {#po.1.p666}
ወሰምዖሙ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይብሉ ፡ "ከመ ፡ መነኮሳተ ፡ ገዳመ' ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ያ አምርዎ ፡ ለአግዚአብሔር ። ወተንሥአ" ፡ ወቀነተ ፣ ሰይፎ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ወረከቦ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ኢስድሮስ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርአዮ ፡ ውእ ቱ ፣ ፈርሀ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ አነ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ሙሴ ፡ ጸሊም ፡ ወመጻ እኩ ፡ ኅቤክሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታአምሩኒ" ፡ አምላከ ። ወነሥኦ ፡ አባ" ፡ ኢስድሮስ ፣ ወወሰ ዶ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ወገሠጾ ፡ ወመሀሮ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ወአጥ መቆ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡፣ ክርስትና ፣ ወመንኩሰ ። ወኃደረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወተጋደለ ፡ ተ ጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ፣ ፈድፋደ ፣ እምብዙኃን" ፡ ቅዱሳን ። ወኮነ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ይፃብኦ ፡ በዘ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ እመብልዕ ፡ ወመስቴ ፡ ወዝሙትት ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ያየድዖ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ኢስ ድሮስ ፡ "በኩሉ ፡ ዘበጽሐ" ፡ ላዕሌሁ' ፡ እምፀብአ ፡ ሰይጣን" ። ወኮነ፣ አባ ፣ ኢስድሮስ፣ ይናዝ”"ዞ ፣፡ ወይምህሮ ፡ ዘይገብር ። ወእምብዝኃ ፡ ተጋድሎቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይነውመሙ፥፡ አረጋዊያን ፡፣ ቅዱሳን ፣ የዐውድ ፡ ውስተ ፣ አብያቲሆሙ ፡ ወይነሥአ ፣ ማሕየባቲሆ
He heard people saying that the monks of the desert of Asqetes (Scetis) knew God. He arose, girded his sword, departed, came to the desert of Asqetes, and met Saint Abba Isderos (Isidore), the priest. The latter, upon seeing him, was afraid of him: Abba Muse said to him: "It is I, Muse the Black; I come to you so that you may show me God." Then Isderos took him and led him to Saint Abba Macarius, who trained him, taught him the "Creed," and conferred Christian baptism upon him. He became a monk and remained in the desert, leading an ascetic life much harsher than many saints. Satan attacked him by means of his former works of gluttony, drunkenness, and debauchery; and Moses would make known to Abba Isderos all the struggles with Satan that befell him, and Abba Isderos would console him and teach him what he had to do. His asceticism was so great that, when the holy elders slept, he would circulate in their houses, take their jugs, go and fill them with water, and then replace them in their houses: yet the water was far from them.

full of envy against him, inflicted a painful blow upon his foot by causing a disease there: he suffered much and was bedridden, sick. When he knew that it was Satan who had struck him, he increased his devotion and his penance, to the point that his body was withered like wood burned in fire. God considered his patience and healed him of his disease; the suffering and the war of Satan departed from him and the grace of the Holy Spirit descended upon him. 500 brothers gathered to him; he was the superior of the monastery, and they elected him priest. When they had placed him before the sanctuary, the patriarch wished to examine him and know his life. And the patriarch said to the holy elders: "Why do you bring this black man here? Send him away." And Muse went out, reproaching himself and saying: "They have acted well toward you, O Black one, with your ugly face the color of ash!" After that, the patriarch called him and laid his hand upon him, and ordained him priest. The patriarch said to him: "O Muse, here you are all white, inside and out!" One day the holy elders came to him and he had no water. They saw him go out of his cave and go back in several times; then it

ተ ፡ በዓቱ ፡ ወሰአልዎ ፡ አረጋዊያን ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ለምን"ት ፡ ኮንከ ፡ ትወፅ እ ፡ ወትበውእ ፡ ብዙኃ ፣ ጊዜ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አንሰ ፡ እቤሎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ለእ መ ፡ ኢወሀብከኒ ፡ ማየ ፡ ከመ ፡ አስትዮሙ ፡ ለአግብርቲከ ፡ እምአይቴ ፡ ሊተ ፡ "አነ፣ ማይ' ፡ ዘአሰትዮሙ ። ወበምሕረቱ ፡ ተሣሀለ ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ለነ ፡ ማየ ።
rained heavily, and it filled the cistern of his cave. And the holy elders asked him, saying: "Why were you going out and coming in several times?" He said to them: "I was saying to God: 'If you do not give me water so that I may give your servants to drink, from where shall I draw water, I, to give them to drink?' And by his mercy, he sent us water."

Once, Abba Muse went with elders to Saint Abba Macarius, who said to them: "Behold, I see that one of you has the crown of martyrdom." Abba Muse answered him, saying: "Perhaps it is I, my Father, for it is written: He who has killed by the sword shall die by the sword." At the coming of the barbarians, Abba Muse said to the brothers who were near him: "Behold, the barbarians are coming; whoever among you wishes to flee, let him flee!" They answered him, saying: "And you, our father, will you not flee?" He said to them: "Behold, I have been waiting for this day for many years, because of the word of Our Lord who says: He who has killed by the sword shall die by the sword." The barbarians came and killed him and with him seven brothers, who had not wished to flee; and one of them, having hidden behind a mat (a curtain), saw the angel of God, with a crown in his hand, standing and waiting for him; then he went out to
ተኃብአ ፡ ድኅረ ፡ ምንጻፍ"። ወርእየ" ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወበእደዊሁ” ፡ አክ ሊል ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይፀንሖ' ። ወወፅአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ በርበር ፡ ወቀተልዎ። ነ ጽሩ ፡ ኦአኃዊ፣ነ ፡ ለኃይለ ፡ ንስሐ ፣ ወምንተ ፡ ዘገብረት ፣ ወለጠቶ ፡ ለገብር ፡ ከሐዲ ፡ ወቀታሊ ፡ ወዘማዊ ፡ ወሰራቂ ፡ ወረሰየቶ ፣ አበ ፡ ወመምሀረ ፡ ወናዛዜ ፣ ወካህነ ፣ ወሠ ራዔ ፡ "ሕግ ፡ ወቀኖና ፡ ለመነኮሳት ። ወዝኩር ፡ ዲበ ፡ መሥዋዕት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዙሎሙ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ። ወሥጋሁ ፡ ይነብር ፡ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ በደብረ ፡ በርሞስ" ፡ በገ ዳመ ፡ አስቂጥስ ፡ ወያስተርኢ ፤ እምሥጋሁ ፡ ተአምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ፡ ብዙኃት ።፡ እ ግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ አብል ፡ ለአኃው ፡5ኒቱ ። ዘምስለ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ጸሊም ፡ በመጥባሕተ ፡ በርበር ፡ ዘሞቱ ።፡ ዘተኀብአ ፡ በፈሪሆ" ፡ 8እምነ ፡ እልክቱ ፡=፡ ጸዋሬ ፡ አክሊሎሙ ፡ መልእአክ' ፡ ዕበ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ ፤
the barbarians who killed him. Consider, our brothers, the strength of penance, what it has wrought; it transformed a servant, an impious black slave, a murderer, a debauched man, a thief, and made of him a superior father, a doctor, a consoler, a priest, a teacher of the law and the rule for monks, and a man whose memory is celebrated, at the Sacrifice, in all the churches. His body rests until now in the monastery of Barmos in the desert of Asqetes. His body has been the cause of the production of many miracles and wonders. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, and may his blessing be with us. Amen. [Hail, I say, to the seven brothers who, with Muse the Black, perished by the sword of the barbarians. One of them, out of fear, hid himself; but, when the angel who carried their crown appeared to him, his fear changed into courage].


[p. 669] {#po.1.p669}

[Hail, I say, to Muse the Black, who remained withdrawn in the interior of the desert; remembering all his past evil deeds, he was killed

ተቀትለ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ በመጥባሕተ ፡ በርበር ፡ ዮም ፡፡ እንበለ ፡ በደም ፡ ኢይነጽሕ ፡ ደም ፡፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፣ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 8አምላክ ። አመ ፡ ፳፳ለሰኔ ፡ ምንባብ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ' ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ሰማዕት" ፣ ወልደ ፣ ዮሴፍ ፡ ጸራ ቢ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ዘእም፳ወ፪አርድእት ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሰበከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አህጉር ፡ ብዙ ኅ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደሴት ፡ ወሰበከ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወሐነጸ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ። ወሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሃ ፡ ወፈወሶ ፡ ለአውጋርዮስ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ሮሃ ፡ እምደዌሁ ፡ ወአጥ መቆ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አራጥ ፡ ወሰበከ ፣ ውስቴታ ፡ ወአጥመቆሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ዚአሃ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ። ወአ ኃ። ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወኩነኖ ፥ ኩነኔ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወቀነወ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ቅንዋተ ፡ ሐጺን ፡ "ከመ ፡ አሣእን" ፡ ወአሮጾ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ምፅራፍ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሰቀሎ ፡ ወነደፎ ፡ በአሕፃ ፡ ወመጠወ ፡ ነፍሶ ። ወአምቅድመ ፡፣ ይኩንንዎ ፡ ፈነወ ፣፡ መልአክ ተ ፡ ለመሃይምናን ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ መልእክት ፡ ሳብዕት' ፡ አመጻሕፍተ"፣ ሐዋርያ ፣ ምልእ
by the sword of the barbarians, on this day, saying: "Blood is only purified by blood". In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. THE 25TH OF SANE (June 19). On this day was martyred Saint Jude, apostle and martyr, son of Joseph the Carpenter, and one of the 72 disciples. This saint preached in many cities; having come to the island, he preached there and built a church. Then he went to the city of Roha (Edessa), and healed Awgaryos (Abgar), king of Roha, of his disease, and conferred Christian baptism upon him. After which he went to the city of Arat, preached there, and baptized a great number of its inhabitants with Christian baptism. He was taken by the governor of this city, who inflicted many torments upon him; he drove iron spikes arranged like shoes into his feet and made him run the distance of a stadium. Then he suspended him and shot arrows at him; and the saint rendered his soul. Before his torture, he had sent a letter to the faithful, and this letter is the seventh


[p. 671] {#po.1.p671}
ት' ፡ አምኩሉ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ ወጸጋ” ። ወባቲ ፡ ሜጦሙ ፣ ለብዙኃን" ፣ ወአብኦሙ ፡ ው ስተ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ በሕይወቱ ፡ ወእምድኅ”ረ' ፡ ዕረፍቱ ። እግዚ አብሔር ፡ ይም"”ሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ፡፡
among the writings of the Apostolos; it is filled with all wisdom and grace; by it, he converted a crowd of people and made them enter into the faith of Our Lord Jesus Christ, during his lifetime, and after his death. May God have mercy on us through his prayer and may his blessing be with us. Amen.
ሰላም ፡ ለይሁዳ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ገብሩ ።፡ በቀኖተ ፡ ኃዊን ፡ ርሱን ፣ዘተቀነዋ ፣ አአጋሩ ፡፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ወንጌል ፡፤ ፍጹመ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይሜህሀሩ ።፡ ውስተ ፡ መልእክቶሙ ፡ ሶበ ፡ መልእክቶ ፡ ደመሩ ፣
[Hail to Jude, servant of God, with a burning iron spike; his feet were pierced. Since the Apostles inserted his letter into their epistles teaching the perfect faith of the Gospel, his memory was written together with them all].
ምስለ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ደርገ ፡ ተጽሕፈ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ "ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካፅበ ፣ አፅረፈት ፡ ዮስቲና ፡፣ ወለተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ሚናስ ፡ ወተቀብ ረት ፡ መቃብረ ፡ዘይብልዋ ፡ ደጋ ፡ አግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በትንብልናሃ ፡ አሜን"። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መስተጋድል ፡ አባ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ሊ ቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እምኖልቆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳ ሳት ፡ ወ፪ ። ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ተሠይመ' ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ሰደዶ ፡ ንጉሥ ፣፡
On this day also died Yostina, daughter of King Minas; she was buried in the tomb that is called Daga. May God have mercy on us through her intercession. Amen. On this day also died the holy father, the ascetic Abba Petros (Peter), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; in the number of the patriarch fathers, he is the 34th. This father was instituted patriarch after the sending into exile, by the Emperor Asbesyanos, of the patriarch Tewodosyos (Theodosius), following his disagreement


[p. 672] {#po.1.p672}
አስብስያኖስ፤' ፡ ለቴዎዶስዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኢተሰነአወ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፣ በምክሩ ። ወነበረ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ማርቆስ ፣ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ ብዙኃ ፣ ዓመታተ ፣ ወኢያብሖሙ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ወኢመኳንንቲሁ ፥ እለ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ከመ፣ ይሚሙ፣ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ። ወእም”ድኅረ ፡ ሕዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ተሰይመ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድር ያ ፡ ብአሲ ፡ ቴር ፡ ወሠናይ ፡ ወርቱዕ ፣ ሃይማኖቱ ። ወተጋብኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ልሂቃን ፡መሃ ይምናን" ፡ ወአይድፅዎ ፡ ሐዘኖሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ። ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ይፃኡ ፡ ኀበ ፣ ደብረ ፣ ዝጋግ"፣0 በአምሳል ፡ ከመ ፡ አሙንቱ ፡ ይጸልዩ ፡ ወይ ሚሙ፡ሎሙ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣፡ ወተፈሥሑ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ "ወነሥአዎሙ ፡ ለኤሏስ ፡ ቆ ኦሳት ፡ ምስሌሆሙ" ፡ ወነሥእዎ ፣ ”ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ" ፣ አባ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ቀ ሰ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ። ወወፅኡ፤" ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፣፡ ዝጋግ ፡ ወሜምዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ፡ ወ
with him. The see of Mark the Evangelist remained without a patriarch for a great number of years, and neither the king nor his officers who were in the city of Alexandria allowed a patriarch to be instituted. But after some days, a good, just, and orthodox man was appointed to the city of Alexandria. The notables of the faithful gathered to him, and they told him their sorrow because they were without a patriarch. He commanded them to go to the monastery of Zegag as if to pray and to name a patriarch for themselves. They rejoiced at this and took with them the bishops and this brother Abba Petros, then a priest. At that instant they went to the monastery of Zegag; they instituted him patriarch and were happy about it. Before this nomination, Saint Abba Sawiros (Severus) had died and the city of Antioch had remained without a patriarch.

The faithful of the city of Antioch, at the news of the institution of Abba Petros as patriarch, also gave themselves a patriarch in the person of a faithful man by the name of Tawfanyos (Theophanes), who was in agreement with the father Abba Petros on the orthodox faith. They sent divine letters to each other, and each of them proclaimed the mention of the other at the moment

of the prayer and the Mass; but it was not possible for them to enter their respective cities: Abba Petros dwelt at the monastery of Anyana which is to the north of the monastery of Zegag; and Abba Tawfanyos dwelt at the monastery of Aftonyas outside the city of Antioch.
ወበውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ሀሎ ፡ በአፍአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ሄ፪፻አድባራት ፣ ፀወ፪አ ፅጻዳት ፡ ወዙሎሙ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ርቱዓነ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘሀገረ፣ አስክንድርያ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ደወለ ፡ ምስር ፡ ወላዕላይ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወመነኮሳት ፡ዘአድባራተ ፣ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ወኢትዮጵያ ፡ ወኖባ ። እሉ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ርቱዓነ ፡ ሃይማ ኖት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሚመቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ ጴጥሮስ! ፡ ወየሐውሩ ፡ በትእዛዙ ። ወ ኮነ ፡ ኢያጸርዕ ፡ እምጽሒፈ ፣ መልእክታት ፡ ወይፌንዎሙ፡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዙሎሙ ፡ መሃይ ምናን ፡ ወያጸንዖሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ። ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ የዐውድ ፣፡ ዙሎ ሙ ፣ አድባራተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡፤ ወአፅጻዳቲሃ ፡ "ወይጌሥጾሙ ፣ ወይሜህ
At that time, there were outside the city of Alexandria 600 monasteries, and 32 villages whose inhabitants were all orthodox faithful, without counting the Christians of the city of Alexandria and of the whole territory of Cairo, Lower Egypt, and Upper Egypt. The monks of the monasteries of the desert of Asqetes, of Ethiopia, and of Nubia, all of them being orthodox, were under the government of this father Abba Petros, patriarch, and walked in accordance with his orders; he did not cease to write letters, and to send them to all the faithful and to strengthen them in the orthodox faith; he traveled through all the monasteries of the city of Alexandria and its villages; he instructed them, admonished them, and strengthened them.
ሮሙ' ፣ ወያጸንዖሙ ። ወሀለዎ ፡ ፳ረድኣ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወማአምር ፡ "ዘስሙ ፡ ዲሚያኖስ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ዘኮነ"፣ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ እምድኅሬሁ ፡ ወኮነ ፣ ይትራድኦ" ፡፤ በዙሉ ፣ ግብር ፣ ወሥርዓተ ፣ ሕዝ ብ ። ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ‹፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፣፡ ይበውእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡
He had a disciple, holy and learned, by the name of Dimyanos, who was patriarch after him and from whom he received assistance for every affair and for the administration of the people. This holy father Abba Petros entered from time to

time into the city of Alexandria, and looked after the affairs of its inhabitants; he took care of them and fortified them. He spent thus, in his charge, walking according to the manner of the apostles and guarding his flock, the space of 2 years. After which, he died in peace: he had been firm in the orthodox faith. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, and may his blessing and his intercession be with us forever and ever. Amen. [Hail to Petros, whose dispositions and law were good, who was named patriarch at the monastery of Zegag. From the water that flowed upon him like a river, from the clear and untroubled water of his teaching, the children drank and the elders were refreshed].

[Hail to you, Peter and Paul, each in his turn, sparkling gems in the foundations of the Church; doctors of a unique institution, although being two, bless the entry of this winter so that it may fertilize the fields of the earth that have been sown].

[Our Lord, who hast established winter as the sequel to summer, and who hast established summer as the end of spring, we beseech thee, may our flight not arrive in this winter of our days, when we have only leaves without fruit].

In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. THE 26TH OF SANE (June 20). On this day, memory of the glorious angel, Gabriel, archangel, of the dedication of the church of the monastery of Naglon, in the desert of Fayum, and of the miracles that occur there, performed by him. It is in the wood of the roof of his church that the announcement of the flooding of the river of Egypt occurs. For at the anniversary time of the dedication, water appears in this wood like sweat; if


[p. 676] {#po.1.p676}
ወለእመ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ጽጋብ ፡፣ በይእቲ ፡ ዓመት ፣ ያንጠበጥብ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ነጠብጣበ ፣፡ ማይ" ። ወለእመ ፣ ይከውን ፡፣ ረኃብ ፣ ታስተርኢ ፡፣ እምኔሃ ፡ ማይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሃፍ ። ወብ ዙኃት ፣ ተአምራቲሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ መልአክ ፣ ክቡር ፡ ገብርኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት ። እግዚአብሔር ፣ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፣ ወትንብልናሁ ፡፣ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ፣
there is to be abundance that year, abundant drops of water fall from it, but if there is to be famine, the water appears there like drops of sweat. Numerous are the miracles of this glorious angel Gabriel, archangel. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, and may his blessing and his intercession be with us forever and ever. Amen.
አሜን። ሰላም ፡ ለቅዳሴ ፣ ቤትከ ፡ ገብርኤል ፡ ዘውገ" ፡ ኪሩብ ፡፡ በደብረ ፡ ነቅሎን ፡ ይእቲ ፣ እንተ ፡ ተሐንፀት ፡ በጥበብ ፣ ለለበቅዳሴ ፣ ታውኅዝ ፡ እምፅፀ ፡ ተድባብ ። በብዙኅ ፡፣ ነጠብጣብ ፡ ይትአመር ፡ ጽጋብ ፡=
[Hail for the dedication of your church, Gabriel, the equal of the cherubim, it which, at the monastery of Naglon, was built with art; for each anniversary of the dedication, it makes water flow from the wood of the roof; by the numerous drops one knows that there will be abundance, and by water not abundant, as if from a little sweat, one knows that there will be famine].
ወበማየ ፡ ንስቲት ፡ ሐፍ ፡ ይትፈለጥ ፦ረኃብ ፡፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ አዕረፈ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዓቢይ ፣ ኢያሱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ነዌ ፡ ረድኡ፣ ለሙሴ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ነቢያት ። ዝንቱ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ” ተአዛዚ ፡ ወትሑት ፡ ፍጹም ፡ ወድኑ ን"፣ ለሙሴ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ኀደረ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፣ መንፈሱ ፡ ለሙሴ ፡ ነቢይ ። ወተነ በየ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለሙሴ ፡ ነቢይ ። ወሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፣ ሙሴ ፡ ነቢይ ፣ ተመጠወ'፣ ለሕዝብ ፡ በትእዛዘ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በከመ ፡ ኮንኩ፣ምስ ለ፣ሙሴ ፡ ገብርየ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ "እከውን ፣ ምስሌከ" ። ጽናዕ ፡ ወተኃየል ፡ ወዕቀብ ፡ሕ
On this day also died the great prophet Joshua, son of Nawe, auxiliary of Moses, prince of the prophets. This prophet was obedient, submissive, and perfectly deferential toward the prophet Moses. That is why the spirit of the prophet Moses remained upon him, and he prophesied in the time of the prophet Moses. At the death of the latter, he received the government of the people, by the order of God, and God said to him: "As I have been with Moses, my servant,

so I will be with you. Be strong and full of firmness and keep the precepts of the Law that I have fixed for Moses, my servant; do not turn from it to the right or to the left; let your mouth not cease to read the book of the precepts of the Law, but repeat them night and day, so that you may be firm, so that you may keep and accomplish all that is written therein." The heart of Joshua was fortified and he sent spies to Jericho; they came, saw the land, and hid themselves near Rahab the prostitute, to whom they promised, as well as to all the people of her house, not to kill her, then she made them leave. It was so, for Joshua parted the river Jordan for them and the children of Israel passed through it; he made the waters of the river stand up for them like a wall, took Jericho which seven walls surrounded, killed all who were there, men and beasts, and took many cities: their number is 32 cities. He killed 32 kings. All the peoples and the children of Israel feared him.
ወበአንተ ፡ ዕበየ ፡ ፍርሀቶሙ ፡ ለአሕዛብ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ተመክነዩ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሰብአ ፣ ሀገረ ፡ ገባኦን ፡ ወለብሱ ፡ አልባሳተ" ፡ አጽርቅተ ፣ ወጾሩ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ሠቃተ ፡ ጸጐ ር ፡ ብሉያተ ፡ ወዝቃተ ፡ ብሉያተ ፡ ወአሣእነ ፡ "ብሉያት ፣ ወኅብስተ ፡፣ ይቡስ ፣፣ ሙሱ ን" ። ወሖሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወይቤልዖ ፡ ንሐነ” ፡ መጻእነ ፡አምርሑቅ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ወን
Because of the great fear that the peoples had of Joshua, the inhabitants of the city of Gibeon used guile against him. They put on rags and brought with them old hair sacks, old wineskins, old shoes, and dried and rotten bread; then they came to Joshua and said to him: "Behold, we come from a far country, and we would like to obtain from you the promise that you will not


[p. 678] {#po.1.p678}
ፈቅድ ፡- እምኔክሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተሀቡነ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢትቅትሉነ « ወአውሥኦሙ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወረበናተ ፡፣ ደቂቀ ፣ እስራኤል ፣ ወይቤልዎሙ ፡ በአማን ፡፣ ንግሩነ ፡ ለአመ፣ አንትሙ ፡ ትነብሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ምድር ። ወአውሥአዎሙ ፡ ወይቤልዎሙ ፡ ዘአማ ን ፡ ንሕነ ፡ መጻእነ ፡ ”“አምብሔር ፣ ርሑቅ' ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ አርአይዎሙ ፡ ሲሳዮሙ ፡ ዘይ ቡስ ፡ ወሙሱን ፡ ወአልባሷሆሙ ፡ ዘብሉይ ፡ ወአምንዎሙ ፣ ወመሐሉ ፡ ሎሙ”።ወ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፣ ኢያሱ ፡ ከመ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ ቅሩብ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፣ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ለምንት፣ በጐሕሉት ፣ መጻአክሙ ፡ ኀቤነ ። ወእምዝ ፣ ረሰዮሙ ፡ አግብርተ ፡ ለቤተ ፣አግዚአብ
kill us." Joshua and the leaders of the children of Israel answered them, saying: "Tell us truly if you dwell in this land." They answered them, saying: "In truth, we come from a far country." Then they showed them their dry and rotten food and their old clothes. So they believed them and swore to them. Upon learning that they were their neighbors, Joshua said to them: "Why have you come to us with deceit?" Then he made them slaves of the house of God.
ሔር። ወዕበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ፳ነገሥት ፡ አሞራዊያን ፡ ተንሥኡ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ገባኦን ፣ ወተ ራድኡ ፡ በኢያሱ ፡ ወረድኦሙ ፡ ወቀተለ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ቀትለ" ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወአለ ፡ ጐዩ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወገረ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ አአባነ ፡ በረድ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ወአጥ ፍኦሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዕርብ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ቅድመ ፡ደቂቀ ፡እ ስራኤል ፡ ኦፀሐይ ፡ ቁም ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡፣ ገባኦን ፣ ወወርኅኒ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ገዳመ' ፡ አባኮ ን። ወቆመ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ወጸንዓ ፡ ወርኅ ፡ እስከ ፣ አመ ፣ ተበቀለ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምጸ ላእቱ ። ወከፈለ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ምድረ ፡ርስት ፡ ወወሀቦሙ ፡ ለካህናት ፡ አህጉረ ፣ ይኅድሩ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ወምድረ ፡ ለእንስሳሆሙ ። ወፈለጠ ፡ ፳አህጉረ" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፀ
At this news, five kings of the Amorites rose up against the people of Gibeon; they asked for help from Joshua, who indeed helped them and made a great slaughter of them; as for those among them who fled, God cast hailstones upon them from heaven and caused them to perish. When the sun was about to set, Joshua said in the presence of the children of Israel: "O sun, stand still over the city of Gibeon! And you, moon, over the field of Aijalon." And the sun stood still and the moon became motionless until God had taken vengeance on his (Joshua's) enemies. He divided the land of the inheritance among the children of Israel, and gave the priests cities to dwell in and land for their cattle. He set apart seven cities to which anyone who had committed homicide unknowingly would flee


[p. 679] {#po.1.p679}
ወኑ ፣ ውስቴቶሙ ፡፣ ዙሉ ፡ ዘቀተለ ፡ ነፍሰ ፣ ዘእንበለ ፣ አእምሮ' ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ እ ግዚአብሔር ።፡ ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፣ መዋዕሊሁ ፣ ፻ወ፲ዓመት ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ርሥዕና ፡ ሠ ናይት ። አስተጋብኦሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡፣ እስራኤል ፡ ወአዘዞሙ ፡፣ ከመ ፡ ይዕቀቡ ፡ ትእዛዘ፣ ሕገ ፣ ኦሪት ፡ ወኢይትዓደውዎ ፣ ወይጽንፁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አምልኮቱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወአይድፆሙ ፥፤ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር" ፡ አምላክ ፡ ቀናኢ ፡ ወመሳጢ"። ወለአመ ፡ አምለክሙ ፡፣ ባዕዳነ ፡ ውእቱሰ" ፡ ያጠፍአክሙ ፡ ፍጡነ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አፅረፈ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ወተቀብረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መቃብር ፡ ዘተሣየጠ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ እምደቂቀ ፡ አሞር ፡ በ፻በግዕ" ፣ ውስተ ፡ ምድረ ፣ "ናብሎስ ። ወላሐው ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እስራ ኤል ፡ ላሐ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ 8”መዓልተ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ "በጸሎቱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓ
according to the command of God. When his days were 110 years and he had reached a good old age, he gathered the children of Israel and commanded them to keep obedience to the precepts of the Law and not to transgress, but to be firm in the worship of God; he made known to them and told them that the Lord is a jealous and very powerful God, and "if you worship," he said to them, "other gods, He will soon cause you to perish." Then he died in peace and was buried in the tomb that Jacob had bought from the sons of Hamor for 100 sheep in the land of Nablus (Shechem). The children of Israel wept for him greatly for 30 days. May God have mercy on us, through his prayer, in the ages of ages. Amen!
ለም ፡ አሜን። ሰላም ፡ ለኢያሱ ፡ ለሙሴ ፡ ዘተፀምደ ፡፡ እስከ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ካዕበተ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ክቡደ ።፡ አድኅኖ ፡ ሕዝቡ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ሶበ ፣ በእዴሁ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡፡ ቀተለ ፡ በገባኦን ፡ ሠራዊተ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡፡ ወበእለ ፡ ጐዩ ፡ አዝነመ ፡ በረደ ።፡
[Salutation to Joshua who served Moses, to the point that he even received double his grave(?) spirit. When God willed, by his hand, to deliver His people, he inflicted a great defeat, at Gibeon, upon the troops of the kings, and upon the fugitives He caused hail to fall].


[p. 680] {#po.1.p680}
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። አመ፣ ፳ሂ፪ለሰኔ ፣ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፣ ሐናንያ' ፡ ሐዋርያ ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጫምዎ" ፡ ሐዋርያ ት ፡ ኤጴስ ፡ ቆጾሰ ፡ ሳዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ደማስቆ ። ወሰበከ ፣ ውስቴታ" ፡ በስብከተ ፡ ወንጌ ል ፡ ማሕየዊ ። ወሰበከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ገብሪል" ፡ ክዕበ፣ ወሜጦሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ እም ኔሆሙ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወአጥመቆሙ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፣ ክርስት ና ፡ ወአብርሆሙ ፡ በብርሃነ ፣ ሃይማኖት ፣ ርትዕት ። ወአጥመቆ ፡ ለጳውሎስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ዕበ ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወአንበረ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወላዕለ ፣ አዕይንቲ ሁ። ወገብረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዲበ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ዓበይተ ፣ ወአምኑ ፡ በስብከቱ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ እምነ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ወአሕዛብ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አኃዞ ፡ ሉቅያኖስ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወኩነኖ ፡ ኩነንያተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ ፅፁባተ" ። ወሰጠቀ ፡ ገበዋቲሁ ፣ በመጥባሕት ፣ ወአውዐዮሙ" ፡ በመኃትወ" ፡ እሳት ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፣ አውፅኦ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አፍአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወአዘዘ ፣ ከመ ፣ ይውግርዎ ፡ በአአባን ። ወወገርዎ ፡ ወመጠወ ፡ ነፍሶ ፣ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ዘሞተ ፡ በእንተ ፣፡ ስሙ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይ
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. THE 27TH OF SANE (June 21). On this day died Hananya (Ananias), apostle. This saint was established by the holy Apostles as bishop of the city of Damascus. He preached the saving gospel there, and also preached in Beth Gubrin, and he converted a great number of them to the faith in Our Lord Jesus Christ; he conferred upon them Christian baptism and illuminated them with the brightness of the right faith. He baptized Saint Paul, the apostle, when God had sent him to him, and laid his hand upon him and his eyes. God performed great miracles through the hands of this saint, and through his preaching, many Jews and Gentiles came to the faith. After which, he was taken by the governor Lucianus, who inflicted upon him many and harsh torments, tore his sides with knife blows, and had him burned with flaming torches. Then he had him taken outside the city and commanded that he be stoned, and he was stoned. He rendered his soul into the hands of Our Lord Jesus Christ, for whose name he died. May God have mercy on us, through his prayer. Amen!
ምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ፡፡ ሰላም ፡ ለሐናንያ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ዘረከቦ = ተወግሮ ፡ በእብን ፡ ወበአፈ ፡ መጥባሕት ፡ ስጥቀተ ፡ ገቦ ። እምሰማይ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ነበቦ ፡=፡ እምትይኅድና ፡ አመ ፡ ሐብለ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ሰሐቦ ፡፡
[Salutation to Hananya, he whom, because of righteousness, stoning and the laceration of his sides by the edge of the knife reached; as the voice of God had spoken to him from heaven, he gave baptism to the apostle Paul, when the cord of mercy had pulled him from Judaism].


[p. 681] {#po.1.p681}
ለሐዋርያ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡፡ ሰላም ፡ ለመቃብሪከ ፡ አንተ ፡ ከረያ ፡ በአዕምቆ ። ጠቢብ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ ምስጢረ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ሶበ ፣፡ አጽሐቆ ። ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እምደመ ፡ ርግዘትከ ፡ ደመ ፡ አጽድቆ ። ሕፅበኒ ፡ ወአጥምቀኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሐናንያ ፡ አጥመቆ ።
[Salutation to your sepulcher which the wise Joseph (of Arimathea) dug to a great depth, when he took to heart the mystery of the faith; to Jesus Christ, wash me and give me baptism with the blood of the wound of your pierced side, blood which produces justification, as Hananya baptized Paul the Jew, turning me away from all unbelief].
ለአይሁዳዊ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ናፍቆ ።1]' ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ ቶማስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፣ ዘእምሀገረ ፡ ሰንደላት ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ፲ወ፳ዓመት ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአ ብሔር ፡ ሚከኤል ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነውም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሐቅል ፡ ወየዓቅብ ፡ አሕር ወ ። ወአዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ወይትአመን ፡ በስሙ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወተንሥአ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ጥብጣቤ ፡ ዘሐብል ፡ ባሕቲቱ ። ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድ ርያ ፡ ወተአምነ" ፡ በእግዚአነ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ዚአሃ ። ወይ
On this day also, it is Saint Thomas, martyr, of the city of Sandalat. This saint was 11 years old when the angel of God, Michael, appeared to him, and this while he was sleeping in a field: he was guarding pigs. The angel commanded him to go and confess the name of Our Lord Jesus Christ. He arose, took only a rope whip, went to the city of Alexandria, and confessed Our Lord Jesus Christ in the presence of its governor. The latter


[p. 682] {#po.1.p682}
ቤሎ ፡ ሁዕ ፡ ለጣዖታት ፡ ወተካየይዶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይረስዮ ፡ ጸሐፊሁ ። ወተምዓ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቶማስ ፡ ወአውፅአ ፣ ለውእቱ ፡ ጥብጣቤ ፡ዘሐብል ፡ ወዘበጦ ፣ ለመኩንን ፡ ”ዝ ብጠተ ፡ ብዙኃ! ። ወአኅዝዎ ፡ ወኩነንዎ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወሰጠቁ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ በመዋስርተ ፡ ኀጺን ። ወኮነ ፡ የኃሥሥ ፡ ረድኤተ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወ ፈነወ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መልአኮ ፡ ወፈወሶ ፣ እምቀሌስላቲሁ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ሞቅሕዎ ፡ ሰአሎ ፡ ዓቃቤ፣ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ዘይደዊ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈውሶ ። ወወሀቦ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ጥብጣ ቤ ፡ ዘሐብል ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አንብር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ወልድከ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ የሐዩ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፣ በኃይለ ፡ "እግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ" ። ወገብረ ፡ ዓቃቤ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ በከመ፣
said to him: "Sacrifice to the idols!" and promised to make him his secretary; and Saint Thomas became angry with him, took out his rope whip, and inflicted many blows upon the governor. He was seized and made to suffer harsh torture. His whole body was torn with an iron saw. Thomas asked for assistance from Our Lord Jesus Christ, who sent him his angel and healed him of his wounds. Once he was incarcerated, the prison guard asked him to heal his son, who was sick. The saint gave him this rope whip and said to him: "Place it upon your child, and he will be immediately restored by the power of my Lord Jesus Christ." The prison guard did as the saint had commanded him, and his child was immediately restored.
አዘዞ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወሐይወ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ሶቤሃ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፣ መኩንን ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አብጽሖ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሠዕ ፣ ለጣዖታ ት ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እወ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሳለቅ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወነ ሥኦ ፡ ”ለቅ”ዱስ ፡ ምስሌሁ" ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ጣዖታት ። ወሰአሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ "ክርስቶስ ፡ ክመ ፡ ያጥፍኦሙ ፡ ለጣዖታት ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተሰብሩ ፡ ዙሎሙ። ወተንሥአ፣ ሰይጣን ፡ ዘውስቴቶሙ'" ፡ ወተጽዕነ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወሐጎቆ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ተአመ ነ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አልቦ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አግዚአነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግ ዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያው ። ወሶበ ፣ ርእዩ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተኦምረ ፣ አንከሩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወ
At this news, the governor had the saint brought and said to him: "Sacrifice to the idols!" And he said to him: "Yes," while mocking him. The governor was filled with lively joy; he took the saint with him and led him to the temple of the idols. The saint prayed to Our Lord Jesus Christ to destroy the idols, and immediately they were all broken, and Satan, who was upon them, went off to possess the governor and suffocated him until he made this confession: "There is no God but Our Lord Jesus Christ, son of the living God." At the sight of this miracle, the people marveled greatly and cried out, saying: "There is no God but the Lord Jesus Christ!" After that, the saint was


[p. 683] {#po.1.p683}
ጸርሑ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ አልቦ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አግዚአእ' ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሞቅሕዎቻ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አለ ፡ ኢአምኑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዓት ፡ ጸሊም ፡ ወነበ ረ ፡ ፲ወ8መዓልተ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ መብልዕ ፤ ወኢመስቴ"፣ ወመልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ኮነ ፣ ይሔውዶጾ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሰቀልዎ ፡ ጐልቀሊተ ፡ እስከ ፡ ወረደ ፡ እምአንፉ" ፡ ደም ፡ ብዙኅ ፣ ወወረደ ፣ ኅቤሁ ፣፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአድኅኖ ። ወሀለዋ ፡ ለአሐቲ ፤ ብአሲት ፡ ወልድ ፡ "ዕውር ፣ ወነሥአት ፡ እምውእቱ ፡ ደም ፡ ወረሰየት ፡ ዲበ ፡ አዕይን
imprisoned by those who had not believed, who had not converted at the sight of this miracle, in a dark cavern, where he remained 15 days without food or drink. But the angel of God visited him. Then he was suspended head down, so that blood fell in abundance from his nose; and the angel of God descended to him and delivered him. Now a woman who had a blind son took some of this blood and put it on the eyes of her son, who was healed immediately.
ቲሁ ፡ ለወልዳ ፡ ወሐይወ' ፡ ሶቤሃ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፣ ሞቅሕቻ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ሕዳጠ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ወአውፅእአዎ ፡ ወሰደዳዱ.፣ ላዕሌሁ ፡ "አንበሳ ፡ አንስተ" ፡ ወበጽሐት ፣ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወለሐሰት ፣ እገሪሁ ። ወአዲ ፡ ዘበ ጥዎ ፡ በድቡሳተ" ፡ ሐጺን ፡ ዲበ ፡ አፉሁ ። ወሀሎ ፣ ምስሌሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩኔኔ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በብኑዳ ፡ ዘእምሀገረ ፡ ብንደራ' ፡ ወቅዱስ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ዘእምሀገረ ፡ በልኪም ። ወኮኑ ፣ ያሥ ተዔግሱ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፣ አፍልሐ ፡ ቅብዓ ፣፡ ወዝፍተ ፣ ወወደዩ ፡ ዲ በ፣ርአሱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ቶማስ ፡ ወኢበጽሖ ፣ ወኢምንትኒ ፡ እሙስና ። ወዓዲ ፣፡ መተሩ፣ እስኪቶ ፡ ወኬድዎ" ፡ በምክያድ ፡ ወሰቀሉ ፥ ውስተ ፣ ክሳዱ ፡፣ እብነ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወሶበ ፣ ደክመ ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ ነሥኦ ፡ አርያኖስ ፣ መኩንን ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሰዶ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀ
Then the saint was imprisoned for a few days, then he was brought out and a lioness was released upon him; she approached him and licked his feet; he was struck again with an iron club on the mouth. With him, Saint Babnuda (Paphnutius) of the city of Bendar and Saint Muse (Moses) of the city of Balkim were subjected to torments. They supported each other. Then they boiled grease and pitch and poured it on the head of Saint Thomas, but not the slightest harm came to him; they also cut off his testicles; he was trampled underfoot and a large stone was hung around his neck. At the end of his strength from torturing him, Arianus the governor took him with him to lead him to the city of Antinoe. Upon arriving at Tuwa, they cut off the head

of Saint Thomas, and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. There were 700 men who suffered with him, who were martyrs, as well as 9 women, in the days of his torture. May God have mercy on us, through his prayer, and through the prayers of them all, in the ages. Amen!

[Salutation to you, Thomas, tortured by harsh blows of the club equipped with iron on the outside. May the stone attached to your neck, coming without being carried by hands, be able to break like an earthen vessel the head of my enemy Satan, in the place where I am].

[Salutation to you, company of Thomas, to you who on the day of his suffering and his martyrdom, sought to receive with him the crown of life, 700 men and nine women; come visit me from the height of heaven].

"A man, by the name of Lazarus, left at the gate of a rich man..." and who is mentioned in his holy gospel. May God have mercy on us, through his prayer, in the ages of ages]. [Salutation, I will say, for the glorious dedication of your church, which was built for you at the end of exile and suffering, O father Timotheos, by the inhabitants of Banhur. Teach me the rule of humility, O you who raise the poor from the earth, for where there is pride, there also is shame].

In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. THE 28TH OF SANE (June 22). On this day died the holy father Abba Theodosius, patriarch of the city of Alexandria; in the number of the patriarch fathers, he is the 33rd. It is from his name that the Christians were named, in Egypt, Theodosians. As for this father, after his nomination as patriarch, perverse men

rose up against him and drove him from his office, at the instigation of the emperor, and named in his place an individual named Gaianus, who was archdeacon of the city of Alexandria, and was of the number of those who had written with their own hands, concerning this father, that he was worthy of the patriarchal office. They led Father Theodosius to Germanos, and this father remained in the city of Germanos for 3 months. Abba Severus was in Egypt at that time. And he consoled this father by reminding him of what had happened to the holy apostles, of tribulation, and likewise to Saint John Chrysostom. Then this father went to the city of Melig and remained there for 2 years. The inhabitants of the city of Alexandria rose up against the governor and demanded their pastor; he returned this Father Theodosius to his office and sent away Gaianus.

His story reached the ears of the Emperor Justinian, and of the Empress Theodora who loved God. And the latter wrote a letter in these terms: "Let the first instituted remain in his office." He had them gather an assembly of 120 priests who wrote with their own hands, saying: "It is Abba Theodosius who was named in the first place." And immediately Gaianus rose up in the presence of the people and said: "It is

የ!፣ ቀዳሚ ። ወሶቤሃ ፣ ተንሥአ ፡ ዓቃቅያኖስ ፣ በቅድመ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አነ"፣ዘ ተዐደውኩ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እኩያን ፡ ያጌብሩኒ' ፣ ዘንተ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሰአልዎ ፣ ሕዝ ብ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ" ፣ ይፍትሖ ፡ ለዓቃቅያኖስ ፡ እምግዘት ፣፡ ወኢይከክውኖ ፡ "ክህነት ፡ ወኢዲቁና" ፣፡ ለግሙራ ። ወይቤ ፡ ዓቃቅያኖስ ፡ እወ ፡ ወፈትሖ ፡ እምግዘት ።
I who have transgressed because perverse men forced me." Then the people prayed to this father to release Gaianus from excommunication and he would never again have either priesthood or diaconate; Gaianus consented and Theodosius released him from excommunication.
ወንጉሥሰ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሙሱነ ፡ ሃይማኖቱ" ፡ ወመሰሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ አባ ፣ ቴዎይስዮስ ፣ ይሰናአው ፣ ምስሌሁ ፡ በሃይማኖት ፣ እኪት ። ወጸሐፈ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ ኀ በ ፣መኳንንቲሁ ፡እለ ፡ ሀገረ' ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይብሎሙ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሊ ቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ ቴዎዶስዮስ ፡ ይሰነአው ፡ ምስሌነ ፣ በሃይማኖትነ ፣ ይትወሰክ ፣ሎቱ ፡ላ ዕለ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ጵጵስናሁ ፡ ክልእ ፡ ሚመት ፡ ወይከውን ፣ መኩንነ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክን ድርያ ። ወለእመ ፡ ኢተሰነአወ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ በሃይማኖት ፡ ወእመ ፡ አኮ ፡ ይወፅእ" ፣ እምሚመቱ ።፡ ወዕበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ይቤ ፡ "ከመዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ሰ ይጣን ፡፣ ለአግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በገዳም ፡ እሁበከ ፡ ዙሎ ፣ መንግሥታተ ፡፤ ዓለም ፣ ወክብሮመ ፡ ለአመ ፡ ሰገድከ ፡ ሊተ ። ተንሥአ ፡ "ቅዱስ ፣ አባ" ፣ ቴዎዶስ ዮስ ፣ ወወጽአ ፣ እምሀገረ ፣ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፣ ላዕላይ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወነበ ረ ፡ ህየ ፡ ሕዳጠ ፡ መዋዕለ" ፡ ”እንዘ ፡ ያጸንዖሙ ፣ ለመሃይምናን" ፡ ውስት ፡ ሃይማ
Now the emperor was corrupted in the faith and he imagined that the patriarch, Abba Theodosius, would agree with him in the evil faith; he wrote a letter to his magistrates of the city of Alexandria, saying to them: "If the patriarch Theodosius agrees with us in our faith, one will add to his patriarchate another office, he will be magistrate of the city of Alexandria; but if he does not agree with us in the faith, he will leave his office." Upon learning this word, this brother said: "Thus said Satan to Our Lord Jesus Christ, in the desert: I will give you all the kingdoms of the whole world and their glory if you worship me." Then the holy father Theodosius rose up, left the city of Alexandria, and went into Upper Egypt, where he remained a few days, strengthening the faithful people in the right faith. When the emperor learned that he had left the city of Alexandria, he sent him a letter, caressing him and
ኖት ፡ ርትዕት ። ወሰምዓ” ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ አምሀገረ ፡ እስክንድር ያ ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፣ መልእክተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የኃይጦ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አንሰ ፡ አፈቅድ' ፡ እ ትራከብ ፣፤ ምስሌከ ፡ ወትባርከኒ ፣ ወታምክረኒ" ። ወሖረ ፡ "አባ ፡፣ ቅዱስ"፣ ቴዎዶስ ዮስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቀሩስጥንጥንያ ። ወተቀበልዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወዙሎሙ ፡ ሕዝ ብ ፡ ወሰራቂት ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበር ፣ ልዑል ። ወእምዝ ፣ ተዋሥኡ ፡ በበይ ናቲሆሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሃይማኖት" ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የኃይጦ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ ብዙኃ ፡ መዋዕለ ፣ ወውእቱ ፣ ይመውኦ ፣ እምቃላተ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ቅ”ዱሳት ፡ ወእምቃላቲሆ ሙ ፡ ለአበው ። ወሶበ ፣ ኢተሰነአወ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ሰደዶ ፡ እመንበሩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ላዕላይ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወሴመ ፡ ሀየንቴሁ ፡ 8ብእሴ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ "ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ጳውሎስ" ፡ ኅበ ፣ ሀገረ ፣ እስክንድርያ ፡ ኢተወክፍዎ ፡ ሕዝብ ። ወነበረ ፡ አሐተ ፡ ዓ መተ ፡ ፍጽምተ ፣ ወኢተመጠው"' ፡ ፉርባነ ፡ እምእዴሁ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሕዳጣን' ። ወሶ በ፣ ሰምዓ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ዘንተ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዕፅው ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡-እስከ፣ ይትኤዘዙ ፣ ሕዝብ ፤ ለጳውሎስ ፡ ሊቀ ፣ ጳጳሳት ። ወኮኑ ፣ እመሃይምናን"፣፡ ይወፅኡ፣ አፍአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘቅዱስ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ “ወኀበ ፣፡ቤተ"፣ ክርስቲያን ፣ ዘቆዝሞስ ፣ ወዲሚያኖስ ፣ ወይቄድሱ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ወያጠምቁ ፡ ውሉዶይ ሙ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡፣ ዘንተ ፡ አዘዘ ፣ ወአርኃወ ፣ሎ ሙ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናቲሆሙ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ አባ ፣ ቴዎዶስዮስ ፣ ዘንተ ፡ ፈርሀ ፡
saying to him: "I desire to meet with you and receive your blessing and your advice." Saint Abba Theodosius went to Constantinople; to meet him came the patriarch, all the people, the troops, and they made him sit on a high throne. Then they discussed the faith together; the emperor spent many days flattering the saint, but the latter defeated them by the words of the holy Scriptures and the words of the Fathers. Not having agreed with the emperor, the latter drove him from his seat toward Upper Egypt and named in his place a man by the name of Paul; when this Paul came to the city of Alexandria, the people did not receive him. He remained an entire year and no one received the eucharist from his hand, with the exception of a few people. Upon learning this, the emperor ordered the churches to be closed until the people obeyed the patriarch Paul. The faithful would go outside the city, going to the church of Saint Mark the evangelist and to the church of Cosmas and Damian where they celebrated the mysteries and gave their children Christian baptism. At this news, the emperor ordered their churches to be opened for them. When Abba Theodosius learned this, he feared that the emperor would

ከመ ፡ ኢያስሕቶሙ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወጸሐፈ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ እንተ ፣ ምልእት ፡ እምኩሉ፣ ኑዛዜ ፣ ወፈነዋ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያጸንዖሙ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ። ወ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ተዓቀቡ ፡ ወኢትትአዘዝዎ ፣ ለውእቱ ፡ ዓላዊ ፡ ጳውሎስ ። ወነበረ ፣ ውስ ተ ፡፣ ስደት ፣ ፳ወኗዓመተ ፡ በላዕላይ ፣ ግብጽ ፣ ወበሀገረ' ፣ እስክንድርያ ፡ ፪ዓመተ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወቱ" ፡ ፳8ወ፪ዓመተ ። ወደረሰ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ድርሳና ተ ፣ ወትምህሀርታተ ፡ ብዙኃተ" ። ወነበሩ ፣ በብሔረ ፣ ግብጽ ፣ ይሰመዩ ፡ ቴዎዶሳዊ ያን ፣ እስከ" ፣ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለአባ ፣ ያዕቆብ ፡፣ ወተሰምዩ ፡፣ ያዕቆባዊያን ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸ መ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ፣ ሑረቶ ፡ "በሠናይ ፡ ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአዕረፈ" ፡
cause them to fall into error; he wrote a letter full of all sorts of consolation and sent it to them, strengthening them in the right faith and saying to them: "Take care and do not obey this heretic Paul." He remained in exile for 28 years in Upper Egypt and, in the city of Alexandria, four years; all the days of his life were 32 years; this Father composed many homilies and instructions. The faithful of the land of Egypt remained called Theodosians until the time of the father Jacob Baradaeus, and then they were named Jacobites. When this father had well finished his career and had pleased God, he died in peace. May God have mercy on us, through his prayer, and may his blessing be with us. Amen!
በሰላም ። አግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፣ አሜን። ሰላም ፣ ለቴዎዶስዮስ ፣ ዘተወክፈ ፣ ክበዶ ፡=፡ ለሕማም" ፣ ወለተሰዶ ፡፡ በቃለ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት" ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይሜህር ፣ ውሉዶ ፡=፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፣ ፈቃዶ ፡=፡
[Salutation to Theodosius who received, bore the weight of suffering and exile, while he instructed his children with the words of the right faith. Because he did the will of Christ, nothing was taken away from him of the reward of the apostles].

ወአምጥጻ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ዘብዙኅ ፡ፍቅዱ ፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፣ "ክዕበ ፣ ተዝካረ'፣ ባስልዮስ ፡ ወቢፋንስ" ፣ በረከቶሙ ፣ትኩን፣0 ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡
[On this day also, memory of Basilius and Bifanes Bifantios. May their blessing be granted to us in the ages of ages. Amen!]
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ፣አምላክ። አመ ፡ ፳ወ፪ለሰኔ ፡ ምንባብ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተዝካረ ፣ በዓለ ፣ ልደቱ ፣ ለእግዚእነ ፣ "ወአምላክነ ፡ ወመድኃኒ ነ" ፡ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፣ ለዓ”ለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን። ሰላም ፡ ለልደትከ ፣ አግዚአብሔር ፡ ልዑል ።፡ በኢሩካቤ ፡ ወዘርእ ፡ እምነ ፡ ባሕቲታ ፡ ድንግል ፡=፡ በዘይትዓወቅ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ዘልደትከ ፡ አምሳል ፤ አውፅአት ፡ ምድር ፡ ሐመልማለ ፡ ከመ" ፡ አዘዝከ ፡ ታብቀል ። እንበለ ፡ ዝናም ፡ ይረድ ፡ ወይትከዓው ፡ ጠል ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. THE 29TH OF SANE (June 23). On this day, memory of the feast of the Nativity of Our Lord, God and Savior Jesus Christ, may he be praised in the ages of ages. Amen! [Salutation to your Nativity, O God Most High, which took place from the only Virgin without carnal union or seed. So that the figure of your Nativity, of old, might be manifest, the earth produced greenery, as you had commanded it to make it germinate, without rain having fallen, nor dew poured out].


[p. 691] {#po.1.p691}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፣ ሰማዕታት ፣ ሂመስተጋድላን ፣ ዘእምደብረ ፡ ቱና' ። እለ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አባ፣ ባሲዲ" ። ወአባ ፡ ኩቱሎስ" ። ወአባ ፡ አርዳማ ። ወአ በባበ፣ሙሴሌ ። ወአባ ፡ ኤሴይ'። ወአባ ፡ ባኒክላስ"” ። ወክልእ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ኩቱሎ ስ" ፣ወአባ ፣ ባሲዲ ፣ ወኩቱሎስ ፡ አሉ"፣ እሙንቱ ፡ ቀሳውስት ። ወአስትርአዮሙ ፤ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያግህዱ ፡ ስሞ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወተንሥኡ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መኩንንን ፡ ወረከቡ ፡ ሐመረ ፡ እ ንዘ ፡ ይነብሩ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ አሉ ፣ ፳'መስተጋድላን ። ወተሰነአው ፡ ኩሎሙ ፣ ወበጽ ሑ፡ግኅበ ፡ መኩንን ። ወኮነ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ አባ ፣ ባሲዲ"፣0 ይትናገሮ ፡ ለመኩንን ፡ ገሃደ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ፍርሀት ። ወተስእለ ፡ እምኔሁ ፣ መኩንን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሀገሩ ፡ ወአጠየቆ ፡ከ መ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ቱና ' ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ወአብያጺሁ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ይሞቅሕዎሙ ። ወእ ምድኅረዝ ፣ አውፅኦሙ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወ"ኩነኖ"ሙ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፣ ወሰቀሉ" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ክሳዶሙ ፡ አአባነ ፡ ወሞቅሕዎሙ ፡ ዳግመ። ወአስተርአዮሙ ፡እግ ዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ውስተ" ፣ ቤተ ፣ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወአጽንዖሙ ፡ ወአስተዓገሦሙ ፡ ወተካየዶ ሙ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ፡
On this day also are the holy martyrs, the 7 ascetics of the monastery of Typa. They are: Abba Basidi, Abba Kutulos, Abba Ardama, Abba Muse, Abba Esey, Abba Baniklas, and another monk by the name of Kutulos. Abba Basidi and Kutulos were priests. The angel of God appeared to them and gave them this command: "Proclaim openly the name of Our Lord Jesus Christ." They rose at once to go to the governor, and met a boat where these five ascetics were installed; they all agreed and arrived before the governor; and the priest Abba Basidi spoke frankly to the governor, without fear. The governor questioned him about his city, and he let him know that he was from the city of Tuna, he and his companions. He had them imprisoned, then brought them out of prison and inflicted a harsh torture upon them. He ordered stones to be hung from their necks, and they were imprisoned again. Our Lord Jesus Christ appeared to them in the prison and strengthened them, exhorted them to endurance, and promised them the kingdom of heaven.


[p. 692] {#po.1.p692}
ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ፈነዎሙ ፡ መኩንን፤' ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፣፡ ወኩነንዎሙ"፣ በህየ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወእምዝ ፣ ወደይዎሙ ፡ ውስተ" ፡ ቴገናት ፡ እንተ ፡ ምሉአት 0፣ ተይ ፡ ወዝፍት ፣ ወአንደዱ ፣ መትሕቴሆሙ ፡ እስክ ፡ አመ" ፡ ተለዓለ ፡ ላህቡ" ፡ ፳በአ መት ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፣ አውፅእዎሙ ፡ እምቴገናት ፣ ወወገርዎሙ" ፡ ወአስተርአዮሙ ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፣ ወአንሥኦሙ ፡ ጥዑያነ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ፡ ወአጽንዖሙ ፡ ወአስተዓገሦሙጮሙ' ። ወእምዝ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መኩንን ፣ ወተአመኑ ፡ ቅ ድሜሁ ፡ በእግዚእአነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ”ወተአምኑ" ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ ፻ወ፪ሰብ እ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕታተ ። ወእምዝ" ፡ ኩነኖሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፣ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፣ ወአብጽሐ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ አብሎንሃ ፡ ጣዖተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበር ፡ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ይስ ግዱ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወረገጽዎ ፡ በእገሪሆሙ ፡ ወወድቀ ፣ እመልዕልተ ፡ መንበር ፡ ተሰብረ" ፣ በበስባር ፣ ወአዘዘ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወመተሩ " ፡ እገሪሆሙ ። ወለቀሲስ” ፡ አበ ፡ በሲ ዲ”።፣ መተሩ ፡ ርአሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፣ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፣ ወከማሁ ፡ "እሉ ፡ ፳ካዕፅበ” ፡ እምድኅሬሁ ። ወለቅዱስሰ ፡ ኩቱሎስ” ፡ ወአውዓይዎ ፡ በ እሳት ፣ወነሥኡ ፡ ዙሎሙ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ሕይወት" ፣ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ አሜን ፡= ።
After that, the governor sent them to the city of Alexandria and, there, made them undergo great torments. Then he threw them into pans filled with sulfur and pitch and made a fire beneath them until the flame rose to a height of 20 cubits. Then they were taken out of the pans and thrown [down]. Our Lord Jesus Christ—to whom praise is due!—appeared to them and raised them up safe and sound, without harm, and strengthened them and exhorted them to endurance. Then they came to the governor and confessed Our Lord Jesus Christ before him, and because of them, 130 people believed and were martyred. Then he inflicted great torments upon the saints and had an idol of Apollo brought, placed on a throne, and he ordered them to prostrate themselves before it; but they struck it with their feet, it fell from the throne and broke into several pieces. The governor commanded that their feet be cut off; as for the priest Abba Basidi, his head was severed with a sword and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. And thus the 5 also, after him; as for Saint Kutulos, he was burned in the fire; they all received the crown of eternal life in the kingdom of heaven. May God have mercy on us, through their prayer. Amen!


[p. 693] {#po.1.p693}
ሰላም ፣ ለክሙ ፡ ፻ወጓቅዱሳን ፡፡ ማኅበራነ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ፪ቱ ፡ ኃራምያን ፡፡ ዘአስተዳለዉ ፡ ለክሙ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ መኩ”ንን ፡ ኢያፍርሃክሙ" ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትኔጽሩ ፡ በዓይን ፣
[Hail to you, 140 saints, companions of the 7 martyrs, abstinent penitents. The sharp sword and the boiling pan that had been prepared for you before the judge did not frighten you, even though you saw them with your own eyes.]
በሊሕ ፡ መጥባሕት ፡ ወፍሉሕ ፡ ጠገን ፡፡ ሰላም ፡ ባሰዒ' ፡ ወኮስላስ" ፡ ወአርዳማ" ፡ ፻ ፡=፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወዕሜ ፡ ወለልኪኖስ' ፡ እኅወ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡፡ ወክትላስ" ፡ ክልእ ፡ ተውሳከ ፡ እልክቱ ።፡ በአደ ፣ ስኩራን ፡ ተሐራምያን ፡ኒቱ ።፡
[Hail, Basadi, Koslas, and Ardam the three, Muse, Ese, and Bilakanos, their brothers, and the other Kotolas who joined them. The seven abstinent ones, tortured, died for Jesus Christ, by the hands of the drunkards.]
በአንተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ጽዑራን" ፡ ሞቱ ፡፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፣ሰማዕታት ፡አባ፡፣ሆር ፡ወአባ።፣ብሶይ። ወደ ይደራ"፣እሞሙ ። ወአባ ፡ሆርሰ ፡ኮነ ፡ ሐራ ፡ እምሰራዊተ ፡ አንጾኪያ ። ”ወበጽሐ ፡ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ፣አስክንድርያ"'፣ወተአምነ” ፡ በእግዚእነ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ክርስቶስ ። ወአዘዘ ፣መኩንን፡፣ ወመተሩ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ዘየማን ፡ ወአሰርዎ ፡ በሐብል ፡ ወሰሐብዎ ፡ በብዕራ” ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቬ ላ ፡ ሀገር ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፣ አርሰኑ ፣ ሐጺናተ ፣ ስፉሐተ ፣ በእሳት ፣ ወአንበርዎሙ ፡ ዲ በ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ዘፀጋም ። ወአፍልሑ ፡ ፍዓረረ ፡ ወከዐው ፡ ውስተ ፡ አ
On this day also are the holy martyrs Abba Hor, Abba Besoy, and Daydara their mother. Abba Hor was a soldier in the troops of Antioch; he came to Alexandria and confessed Our Lord Jesus Christ. On the order of the governor, his right hand was cut off, he was tied with a rope and dragged with an ox throughout the city. Then large iron plates were heated in the fire and placed on his body; then they cut off his


[p. 694] {#po.1.p694}
ፉሁ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ገደፍዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓዘቅት ፡ እንተ ፡ ምልአት ፡ አፍዖት ፡ ወአክ ይስት ፡ ወኢቀርብዎ' ፡ ግሙራ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ዘበጥዎ ፡ በአብትርት ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይጸርጎኅ ፡ እ ንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኦኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ርድአኒ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ኩነኔ ። ወኮነ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ "ይረድኦ ፡ ወያጸንዖ ፡ ወአንሥአ" ፡ ጥዑየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ፡ በከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቀዳሚ።
left hand; they melted lead and poured it into his mouth. After which, they threw him into a cistern filled with vipers and snakes, but they did not approach him at all. Then they beat him with rods and he cried out, saying: "O Jesus Christ, help me in all this torture!" And Our Lord Jesus Christ helped him, strengthened him, and raised him up safe and sound, without harm, as he was before.
ወእምዝ ፡ መጽአት ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ እሙ ፡ ወናዘዘቶ ፡ ወተፈሥሐት ፡ በተጋድሎቱ ። ወ ነገርዎ ፡ ለመኩንን ፡ በእንቲአሃ ፡ ወአብጽሐ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ”ለእሙ ፡ለቅዱስ'"፣ ወይቤላ ፣ ታዲ ፡ ለጣዖታት ፡ ወተምዓ ፡፣ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ወአፍርሀ ፡ ወኢፈርሀት ፡ እምቀ፡ጥዓሁ ፣ ወኢ ተአዘዘት፣፤ሎቱ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያርስኑ ፡ አብትርተ፤' ፡ ሐጺን ፡ በእሳት ፡ ወይደ”ይዎ ሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገበዋቲሃ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ወዘመረት ፡ ለእግ ዚአብሔር ፡ ወቀደሰቶ ፡ ወሰብሐቶ ፣ ዘረሰያ ፣ ድልወ" ፣ ከመ ፡ ትሕምም ፡ በእንተ ፡ስ ሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ መጠወት ፡ ነፍሳ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀለወት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ወነ
Then his mother came to him, who consoled him and was happy in his struggle. The governor was spoken to about her; and the governor had the saint's mother brought and said to her: "Sacrifice to the idols!" He grew impatient with her and sought to frighten her; but she was not afraid of his indignation and she did not obey him. He ordered iron rods to be heated in the fire and placed on her sides. When this had been done to her, she rejoiced and sang in honor of God, celebrated his holiness and his praises, he who had made her worthy to suffer for his holy name. Then she gave up her soul, while she was in the torments, and she received the crown of eternal life.
ሥአት ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ሕይወት' ። ወለቅዱስሰ ፡ አባ ፡ ሆር ፡ አብሰልዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቴገን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቅብዕ ፡ ወቅጥራን ። ወኮነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቴገን ፡ ይሴብሖ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣፡ ሕማም ። ወአ ይድፅዎ ፡ ለመኩንን ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወአንከረ ፣ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወደንገፀ ፡ ወተምዓ ፣ ወተንሥአ ፡
As for Saint Abba Hor, he was cooked in a pan with oil and liquid pitch, and, in this pan, he glorified God, without suffering. This was made known to the governor who marveled greatly and


[p. 695] {#po.1.p695}
ወበጽሕ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቴገን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ኩናት ፡ ወረ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ው ስተ ፡ እንግድዓሁ ፡ ወመጠወ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ውስተ ፣ እዴሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወከማሁ ፡ "ለአእጉሁ ፡ አባ ፡ ብሶይ ' ፡ ኩነን ዎ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፣ ወነሥአ ፣ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፤ በመንግሥ ተ ፡ ሰማያት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፣ አሜን ። 'ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፡ ብሶይ ፡ ሐራ ። ዘአሰሩከ ፡ በሐብል ፡ ወሰሐቡከ" ፡ በብዕፅራ ።. አቀርብ ፡ ለከ ፣ መሥዋዕተ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ "ከመ ፡ ጵርስፎራ ። ለእቱከ ፡ ሖር ፡ ወለአምከ ፡ ይድራ ።
was terrified; then he became angry and came to the pan, a spear in his hand, and he pierced the saint in the chest, and the latter surrendered his soul into the hands of God and received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. His brother Abba Besoy was also subjected to harsh torments; his head was severed with a sword, and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdoms of heaven. May God have mercy on us, through their prayer. Amen! [Hail to you, Besoy, soldier, who was tied with a rope and dragged by an ox; I offer you a sacrifice of praise, as an oblation, and also to your brother Hor and to your mother Yedra who, for Christ, endured great tribulations.]

[On this day also died Theodore, king, son of David, king of Ethiopia. This saint, from his childhood, was raised in wisdom and good education; he learned all the Books of the Church, and then learned to ride a horse, and to shoot with a bow; for he was a vigorous man. From his early age, he

አብሔር ፡ ወይዘሩ ፡ ንዋዮ ፡ "ላዕለ ፣ ነዳያን ፤፣ ወምስኪናን'፡፣ ወይሔውጽ"፡፣ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ ወይትጋደል ፡ በጾም ፣ ወበጸሎት ፡ ወ"ኢያውሰበ ፡፣ዘአንበለ ፣ አሐቲ፣ ብእሲት ፡ ወኢገብረ ፡ ዓመፃ ፣ ወግፍዓ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ፳ሂ = ወሶበ ፣ ኀለየ ፣፡ ይሖር ፡ ኢየሩ ሳሌም ፡ ተማከሮ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ወውእቱሂ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ኢኮነ ፣ መክፈልተከ ፡ እስ መ ፡ አእመረ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ =፡ ወእምዝ ፡ " አእረፈ ፡፡ ወእንዘ ፣ ይወስዱ" ፡ በድ ኖ ፡ በዘመነ ፡ ክረምት ፡ ተከፍለ ፡ ማየ ፡ ፈለግ ፡ ምሉዕ ፡ ለፌ ፡ ወለፌ ፣ ወኅበ ፡ ቀበ ርዎሂ ፡፣ ነቅዓ ፣ ማየ ፡ ሕይወት ፣ ወሀሎ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ = እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፣ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፡ ለብሔረ ፡ አግዓዚ ፡ ንጉሣ =፡ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አንበሳ ፡፡ ተዝካረ ፡ በዐልከ ፡ዮም ፡ በጥብሐ ፡ አባግዕ ፡ ወአንስሳ ፡፡ አኮ ፡ ዘገብረት ፣ ባሕቲታ ፡ እምከ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ሞገሳ ፡፡
was attached to the love of God and distributed his goods to the needy and the poor; he visited the churches, and exercised himself in fasting and prayer. He married only one woman, he committed no injustice or violence against anyone whatsoever. When he thought of going to Jerusalem, he consulted Abba Margos who said to him: "It is not your portion"; for he had had knowledge of it through the Holy Spirit. Then Theodore died. While his body was being carried, in the time of winter, the water of the river flowing at full banks parted to one side and the other; furthermore, where he was buried, a salutary (thermal) water gushed forth which exists until now. May God have mercy on us, through his prayer, in the ages of ages. Amen!] [Hail to you, king of the land of Ethiopia, Tewodros, son of the lion of Judah. Not only your mother, Sion Mogase, celebrated the commemorative banquet of your feast today with the slaughter of sheep and large cattle, but the clouds also let fish drip down.]
ደመናትኒ ፡ አንጠብጠቡ ፡ ዓሣ ፡፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ አዕረፈ" ፣ ማርቆስ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ሮሜ ፡=፡ ወዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ነግሠ ፣ በድንግልናሁ ፡ ፳ዓመተ ፡ ወዓቀበ ፣ ሕዝቦ ፣ በፍትሕ" ፡ ወበርትዕ ፡፡ ወእምዝ ፣ ሶበ ፣ አገበርዎ' ፣ ከመ ፣ ያውስብ ፡ ብአሲተ ፡ ቦአ ፡ በሌሊት ፣ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ክርስቲ
[On this day also, died Mark, king of Rome. This saint reigned, during his virginity, five years, and guarded his people with justice and uprightness. Then, when he had been forced to take a wife, he entered, at night, into


[p. 697] {#po.1.p697}
ያን ፣ ወቆመ ፣ ቅድመ ፡ ሥዕላ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ "ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ በ፪' ፡ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፣ አምላክ ፡= ወይቤላ ፡ ኦእግዝእትየ ፣ ምርሕኒ ፡ ኅበ ' ፡ አሐውር ፡ ፍኖተ ፤ ወትቤሎ ፣ ሑር ፡ በሰላም ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌከ ፡፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ሖረ ፣፡ዘእንበ ለ ፡ ሐመር ፡ አዲዎ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ ኢያሪኮ ፡ ወበጽሐ" ፡ ደብረ ፡ ቶርማቅ! ፣ ውስተ፣፡መካክን፣፣ ይቡስ ፣ ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋደል ፡ "ምስለ ፡ አራዊት" ፡ ፳ዓመተ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ0፣ ቀበርዎ ፣ መላእክት ፣ በስብሐት ።፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡፣ በጸሎቱ ፡፣ ለዓለመ ፡
the church and stood before the image of Our Lady, the holy Virgin in both (spirit and body), Mary, mother of God, and said to her: "O my Sovereign, lead me in the way that I must follow!" She said to him: "Go in peace! God be with you!" Then he went, crossing without a boat the sea (the river) of Jericho and arrived at the convent of Torma in an arid place; he passed into the life of an ascetic, in the company of beasts, for sixty years. At his death, the angels buried him with glory. May God have mercy on us, through his prayer, in the ages of ages!]

In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. THE 30TH OF SANE (June 24), On this day was brought into the world Saint John the Baptist; he is such that no one born of woman is greater than he. It is he also who glorified Our Lord Jesus Christ, and adored him while he was in his mother's womb; he was judged worthy to lay his hand on the head of the son of God. The holy gospel says: "When the days of Elizabeth's pregnancy for the delivery were finished, she brought into the world a son; her neighbors and her relatives learned that God had multiplied his mercy toward her and they rejoiced with her. When the eighth day arrived, they came to circumcise the child and named him by the name of his father, Zechariah. And his mother said: "No, but name him John." And they said to her: "There is no one in your family who bears this name." And they made a sign to his father and said to him: "How do you want to

name him?" And he wrote these words: "John is his name." And the father's mouth opened, his tongue was untied from his muteness and he glorified God; he prophesied concerning his son that he would be called the prophet of the Most High, that he would walk before the face of God to make straight his way. The completion of his second year coincided with the coming of the magi and the massacre that Herod made of the children; this prophet Saint John was denounced to him, and his soldiers searched for him to kill him. His father Zechariah took him, carried him on his shoulders and begged the soldiers to come with him to the place where he was going to place him; they would take him then. The soldiers came with him until they entered the sanctuary, and he placed him on the corner of the altar and said to them: "It is from this place that I received him." The angel of God took him and led him to the desert named Zipata. The soldiers, not finding him, entered into a great rage, wondering how they had not found him, and killed Zechariah his father. Saint John the Baptist remained in the desert until God had ordered him to come to the desert of the Jordan and to preach, announcing Our Lord Jesus Christ, and to manifest him to the people of Israel, as had prophesied concerning him the

prophet Malachi when he said: "It is the angel, he who preaches before the Savior"; for he was truly like the angels; he was, in effect, filled with the Holy Spirit, and that while he was in his mother's womb; he remained in the desert, did not taste bread, and did not drink wine at all, during all the days of his life, as Our Lord Jesus Christ has borne witness to him, may he be praised! He had no relations with a woman, did not commit sin, neither great nor small, and preached Our Lord Jesus Christ. He saw the Holy Spirit descending from heaven upon him. He received the true crown of martyrdom, the incorruptible, in the kingdom of heaven. And Our Lord Jesus Christ said: "There is no one among the children of women who is great, except (who surpasses) John. May God have mercy on us, through his prayer, and may his intercession and his blessing be with us. Amen!
ቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ ለልደትከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ገብርኤል ፡ አደሞ ፡=፡ ስድስተ ፡ አውራኃ ፡ ለልደተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እንተ ፡ ቀደሞ ፡=፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ምዑዝ ፡ አምስጊን ፡ ወቀናንሞ ።፡ ለለ ፡ ነበብኩ' ፡ በጽሒቅ ፡ ለመ" ልክዕከ ፡ ሰላሞ ፡=፡ ከመ ፡ ከላስስት ፡ በሕፅንከ ፡ ሚሞ ፡፡
[Hail to your Nativity, as Gabriel had fixed it, which by six months preceded the Nativity of Christ. John, every time I read with eagerness the greeting of your "malke" (effigy) more sweet than incense and cinnamon, like a sheaf, place it in your bosom.]


[p. 701] {#po.1.p701}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮና ፡ ማርታ ፡ ወማርያ ፣ ወተዝካሩ ፡ ለገብረ ፡ክ ርስቶስ ፣ መነኮስ ፡፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፣ ጌራን'፣ መስተጋድል ፣ዘይነብር ፣ ውስ ተ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ደሴት ፡ እምደሴያተ ፡ ሀንደኬ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይፈርሆ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ፈድ ፋደ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ መፍቀሬ ፡ ጸሎት ፡ ወጾም"፣ ወምጽዋት ።፡ ወኩሉ ፡ "ዘሰአለ ፡ እምእግ ዚአብሔር፤ ፡ ይሰምዖ ፣ ወበጸሎቱ ፡ ተአተተ ፡ እምይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ብድብድ ፡ ወአባ ር ፡ ወጹዋዌ ፡ ወክፅወተ ፡፣ ደም ፣ ወስጥመተ ፣ አሕማር ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ዘሰአለ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ይረክብ ፡ መፍቅይዶ ፤ ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ዘተውህቦ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ቀንዓ ፡ ላዕ ሌ፣- ፣ ወመጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ብአሲት ፡ "ሠናይት ፡ ራእያ" ፡ ለቢሶ" ፡ አልባሰ፣ ነገሥት ፡ ወሥርግው ፡ ”በፅንቀ ፡ ወበባሕርይ' ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ታንሶሱ ፡ ባሕቲታ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእያ ፣ ሖረ ፣ ኀቤሃ ፣ ወተስእላ ፡ ግብራ = ወትቤሎ ፡ አነ ፡ ወለቱ ፡ ለስርስባን" ፡ ንጉ ሥ ፡፡ ወሶዕበ ፡ አበሰት ፡ እኅትየ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አግብርተ ፡ አቡሃ ፣ ወፈቀደ" ፡ ይቅትለነ ፡ ለኩ ልነ” ወበእንተዝ ፡ ወጻእኩ ፣ ሌሊተ ፣ ወበጻሕኩ ፣ ውስተዝ ፣ ገዳም ፣ ወስብሐት ፡ለ
[On this day also, were martyrs Marta and Marya, and memory of Gabra-Krestos, monk. May God have mercy on us, through his prayer, in the ages of ages. Amen!] [On this day also, died Abba Geran, ascetic, who dwelt in one of the islands of India. He feared God greatly and loved prayer, fasting, and almsgiving. In everything he asked of God, he was answered; through his prayer, the plague was warded off from that country as well as famine, slavery, bloodshed, the sinking of ships; whoever addressed a request to him obtained what he wanted. Satan, at the sight of the gift that had been made to him of heavenly grace, was filled with envy against him, and came to him in the form of a woman beautiful of appearance, dressed in royal clothes, adorned with precious stones and pearls; she walked alone. Seeing her, the saint went to her, and asked her for her news. She said to him: "I am the daughter of King Serseban. My sister having sinned with the servants of her father, he wanted to kill us all; that is why I went out by night and came into this desert. Praise to God who has given me to meet you, you, holy man." He said to her: "Come into this rock, so that you may not be seen by those who come to me." During the night, she came as if she were afraid of the beasts and pushed cries


[p. 702] {#po.1.p702}
እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘአስተራከበኒ ፡ ኪያከ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ቅዱሰ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ሑሪ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛ ቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ከመ ፡ ”ኢይርአዩኪ ፡ ሰብእ' ፡ እለ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ ወበሌሊት ፡ መ ጽአት ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘፈርሃት ፡ እምአራዊት ፡ ወጸርሐት ፣ ኅቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያርኅዋ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ በዐት =፡ ወሶበ ፡ አርኃወ" ፡ ቦአት ፡ ኅቤሁ ። ወኖመት ፡ በገቦሁ ፡ ወሐቀፈት ፡ እንግድዓ ሁ ፡ ወአለምለመት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ነገራ ፡ ” እስከ ፡ አጽነነት ፡ ልቦ" ፡ መንገለ ፣ ፍቅራ ፡፡ ወዕ ቤሃ ፡ ተአተተ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ እምኔሁ! ፡ ወጸልመ ፡ ዓይኑ ፡ ወኢገብአ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኔጽር ፡ ኀቤሃ ፤ ወሶበ ፣ ጽሕወ ፡ እምስካረ ፣ ስሕተት ፣ አአመረ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ውእቱ ፣ አበ ሰ፣ ወከመ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምኾሕሉተ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ መስተፃርር ፡ ርጉም ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእ ቱ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ተመውአ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸሐፈ ፡ ዘረከቦ ፡ እምግብር ፍ፡ ወዙሎ"፣ ዘገብረ ፡ በሥጋሁ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ እብነ ፡ እምአዕባነ ፡ እንትኩ ፡ ደሴት ፡ ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይ ዘብጥ ፡ እንግድዓሁ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሞተ ፡፣ ወኮነት ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ዘለዓለም = ወእምዝ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ አድያም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትባረኩ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ በከመ ፣ ልማይሙ =፡ ወሶበ ፣ ኢረከብዎ ፡ ኦዳ- ፡ ውስተ ፣ ማኅደሩ ፡ ወረከብዎ ፣ ድቁ ሰ"፣ ወመሰሎሙ ፡ ከመ' ፡ ንውም።ወዕበ ፡ አንቃህዎ ፡ ረከብዎ ፡ ምውተ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሰዓምዎ ፡ ወገነዝዎ ፡ ወቀበርዎ ፣ ውስተ ፣ መሬት" ፡= ወእምዝ ፡ ረከቡ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ዘጽሑፍ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፣ ምግባረ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ እፎ"፣ ምክንያ ተ ፡ሞቱ ፡፡ በረከቱ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡
to him so that he would open the door of his cave. When he had opened, she entered to him and slept at his side; she wrapped her arms around his chest; she addressed tender words to him to the point that she made his heart incline to love her. And immediately the grace was withdrawn from him, his eye darkened and he did not turn his gaze away from her. When he was freed from the drunkenness of sin, he recognized immediately that he had committed a sin and that it was the effect of a ruse of Satan, the cursed adversary, for behold he had been conquered by him. Then he wrote what had happened to him and everything he had done in his body; then he took one of the stones of this island and remained striking his chest until he died, and his soul was in eternal life. Then people from those surroundings came to him to receive his blessing according to their habit; not meeting him, they circulated in his dwelling and found him asleep and it seemed to them that he was sleeping; but seeking to wake him, they found him dead. Then they kissed him, buried him, and put him in the earth; then they found the writing where was recorded what Satan had done against him, and how it was the cause of his death. May his holy blessing be granted to us in the ages of ages. Amen!]


[p. 703] {#po.1.p703}
ሰላም ፡ እብል ፡ ለአባ ፡ ጌራን ፡=፡ በውስተ' ፡ደሴት ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ አጽንዖ ፡ መካን በአምሳለ ፣ ብእሲት ፡ ላሕይ”ት ፡ አመ ፡ አስፈጦ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡፡ እስከ ፡ ረከበቶ ፣ መክፈልተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሙታን ፡፡
[Hail, I shall say, to Abba Geran, who in an island, in this place, made great efforts of penance. When, under the appearance of a pretty woman, Satan had seduced him, he broke the bones of his chest by striking them with a stone, until he had reached the portion of all mortals.]
ዓፅመ ፡ እንግድዓሁ ፡ ሰበረ ፡ ዘቢጦ ፡ በእብን ፡፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ምንባብ" ፡ ወርኃ ፡ ሰኔ" ፡ ወስብሐት ፣፤ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወላዕሌነ ፡ ይ ኩን ፡ ምሕረቱ ፡ አሜን ፡፡
End of the sacred reading of Sane. Praise to God and may his mercy be upon us. Amen!
